📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 总共纳入了 894 名登革热患者,其中 59 名免疫功能低下。临床表现相似,但不发烧更为常见(10.2% vs 1.1%)。实验室检查结果在就诊时具有可比性,但免疫功能低下患者的血小板计数最低点较低且血小板减少时间较长。重症登革热 在免疫功能低下患者中更为常见(22% vs 4.9%),住院率更高,住院时间更长,死亡率 也更高。
  • 在 1,242 名参与者中,68.7% 的人表示有意为自己的孩子接种 登革热 疫苗。与较低 疫苗接种 意向相关的因素包括 7-11 岁儿童和较低的自我感知家庭收入,而较高的感知蚊子密度、对 疫苗 的有利态度、更强的主观规范、更强的感知行为控制和更好的登革热预防实践与较高的意向相关。
  • 已确定 67 例与旅行相关的致命登革热病例,其中 55 例被归类为确诊/疑似病例,12 例信息有限。在 55 例确诊/疑似病例中,有 22 例报告了居住国和感染源,跨越 14 个流行目的地。确诊/可能死亡病例发生在年龄<1-79岁的患者中;25.5% 为女性,21.8% 为男性,52.7% 的人没有记录性别。在可获得感染状态的情况下,大多数是原发感染 (9/16)。报告的致命症状包括神经、心脏、出血和高炎症并发症。旅行者中与登革热相关的 死亡率 很少见,但可能未被充分认识和报告。
  • 与其他器官相比,半乳糖凝集素 8 在血细胞裂解物中的表达显着更高。分子对接显示半乳糖凝集素 8 和乳糖之间的结合能为 -8.5 kcal/mol,表明具有特异性。蛋白质-蛋白质对接表明 抗病毒药物 对登革热病毒非结构蛋白 NS1 和 NS5 具有潜在活性。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • A total of 894 dengue patients were included, including 59 were immunocompromised. Clinical presentation was similar, although absence of fever was more common (10.2% vs 1.1%). [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014901].
  • Among 1,242 participants, 68.7% reported an intention to vaccinate their children against dengue fever. [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014896].
  • Sixty-seven fatal travel-related dengue cases were identified, of which 55 were classified as confirmed/probable and 12 cases had limited information.
  • Galectin-8 showed significantly higher expression in hemocyte lysate compared to other organs. Molecular docking revealed a binding energy of -8.5 kcal/mol between galectin-8 and lactose, suggesting specificity.

  • 本地获得的登革热病例减少;卫生官员报告最近几周情况有所缓解。 [news-b609bfed66a990d6]。
  • A cross-sectional seroepidemiological investigation identified a dengue virus seroprevalence of 2.59 percent among Iranian seafarers in Bushehr Province, indicating evidence of previous and recent arboviral exposure.
  • 基因组监测 调查揭示了 2023 年越南 暴发 期间多种 登革病毒 基因型的共同传播,颠覆了之前的单谱系优势模式。该研究记录了 2023 年 传播 季节期间越南发生 149,557 例登革热感染和 36 例死亡。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • Florida has reported 669 confirmed cases of dengue as of November 21, 2026, marking the largest outbreak in the United States in decades.
  • Decrease in locally acquired dengue cases; health officials report relief in recent weeks. [news-b609bfed66a990d6].
  • A cross-sectional seroepidemiological investigation identified a dengue virus seroprevalence of 2.59 percent among Iranian seafarers in Bushehr Province, indicating evidence of previous and recent arboviral exposure.
  • A genomic surveillance investigation revealed the co-circulation of multiple dengue virus genotypes during Vietnam’s 2023 outbreak, overturning previous patterns of single-lineage dominance.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

马来西亚登革热的神经表现:两个新的 病例报告 和已发表病例的回顾。

Neurological Manifestations of Dengue in Malaysia: Two New Case Reports and a Review of Published Cases.
作者： Thai Lun Tan, Daniel Tze Wei Yeap, Azureen Binti Azmel, Stefanie Kar Yan Hung, Katrina Kieng Huong Lau
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 感染是马来西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,其典型特征是自限性发热性疾病,尽管一部分患者可能会发展为严重的全身并发症。其中,神经系统表现越来越多地被报道,并被认为是由病毒的直接神经效应或免疫介导机制(包括分子拟态和自身炎症)引起的。本病例系列旨在描述与 登革病毒 感染相关的神经系统并发症的范围,并强调早期识别和治疗的重要性。我们报告了两名新感染登革热患者的临床特征,他们在患病过程中出现了神经系统症状,并对来自马来西亚的九个已发表病例进行了回顾。这篇基于病例的综述说明了与登革热感染相关的多种神经系统并发症,包括小脑炎、脑病、局灶性颅神经病、吉兰-巴利综合征和肌炎。大多数患者在登革热发病的第一周内出现神经系统症状。管理人员在很大程度上是支持性的,有选择地使用免疫疗法。结果范围从接近完全恢复到残留神经功能缺损,强调与这些并发症相关的可变预后。我们中心的本病例系列中描述的两名患者均被证实感染了 DENV-2 血清型。总之,登革热的神经系统并发症具有临床意义,对于出现感觉改变或神经功能缺损的患者需要高度临床怀疑。早期识别和适当的支持或免疫调节 治疗 对于减轻神经系统后遗症仍然至关重要。

问题与背景
登革病毒 感染是马来西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,其典型特征是自限性发热性疾病,尽管一部分患者可能会发展为严重的全身并发症。

本病例系列旨在描述与 登革病毒 感染相关的神经系统并发症的范围,并强调早期识别和治疗的重要性。我们报告了两名新感染登革热患者的临床特征,他们在患病过程中出现了神经系统症状,并对来自马来西亚的九个已发表病例进行了回顾。
核心方法
我们中心的本病例系列中描述的两名患者均被证实感染了 DENV-2 血清型。
主要结果
其中,神经系统表现越来越多地被报道,并被认为是由病毒的直接神经效应或免疫介导机制(包括分子拟态和自身炎症)引起的。
解释与创新
这篇基于病例的综述说明了与登革热感染相关的多种神经系统并发症,包括小脑炎、脑病、局灶性颅神经病、吉兰-巴利综合征和肌炎。
科研与公卫意义
大多数患者在登革热发病的第一周内出现神经系统症状。
局限与证据强度
管理人员在很大程度上是支持性的,有选择地使用免疫疗法。结果范围从接近完全恢复到残留神经功能缺损,强调与这些并发症相关的可变预后。

Neurological Manifestations of Dengue in Malaysia: Two New Case Reports and a Review of Published Cases.

Authors: Thai Lun Tan, Daniel Tze Wei Yeap, Azureen Binti Azmel, Stefanie Kar Yan Hung, Katrina Kieng Huong Lau
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus infection is a major public health concern in Malaysia and is typically characterized by a self-limiting febrile illness, although a subset of patients may progress to severe systemic complications. Among these, neurological manifestations are increasingly reported and are believed to result from either the virus's direct neurotropic effects or immune-mediated mechanisms, including molecular mimicry and autoinflammation. This case series aims to describe the spectrum of neurological complications associated with dengue virus infection and to highlight the importance of early recognition and management. We report the clinical features of two new patients with dengue infection who developed neurological manifestations during the course of their illness, together with a review of nine published cases from Malaysia. This case-based review illustrates a diverse range of neurological complications associated with dengue infection, including cerebellitis, encephalopathy, focal cranial neuropathy, Guillain-Barré syndrome and myositis. Most patients developed neurological symptoms within the first week of dengue onset. Management was largely supportive, with selective use of immunotherapy. Outcomes ranged from near complete recovery to residual neurological deficits, emphasizing the variable prognosis associated with these complications. Both patients described in this case series from our centres were confirmed to be infected with the DENV-2 serotype. In conclusion, neurological complications of dengue are clinically significant and require a high degree of clinical suspicion in patients presenting with altered sensorium or neurological deficits. Early recognition and appropriate supportive or immunomodulatory treatment remain crucial in mitigating neurological sequelae.

Dengue virus infection is a major public health concern in Malaysia and is typically characterized by a self-limiting febrile illness, although a subset of patients may progress to severe systemic complications.

科特迪瓦 登革病毒 的基因组 流行病学 揭示了来自布基纳法索反复传入的病毒五年传播情况

Genomic Epidemiology of Dengue Virus in Ivory Coast Reveals Five Decades of Viral Circulation with Repeated Introductions from Burkina Faso
作者： Xing-Hua Tao, Hervé Alberic Kadjo, Sylla Yahaya, Kamo Emily, Adjogoua Edgard, Marc A. Suchard, Nídia S. Trovão, Emilie Kamo, Nidia S. Trovao
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 是非洲一种迅速出现的蚊媒疾病,但非洲大陆在 基因组监测 工作中的代表性仍然严重不足。尽管系统发育研究仅集中在布基纳法索等少数国家,但这种差异在西非尤其明显。在这一更广泛的区域差距内,研究人员尚未系统地描述在科特迪瓦流行的 登革病毒 (DENV) 谱系。在这项研究中,我们展示了 2017 年至 2023 年间收集的来自科特迪瓦的 24 个新测序的 DENV 基因组,涵盖血清型 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 DENV-3。结合公开可用的序列,我们组装了该国迄今为止最全面的数据集。我们使用全球背景数据集进行了最大似然和贝叶斯系统发育地理学分析,并重建了科特迪瓦 登革病毒 环流的空间和时间动态。我们的研究结果表明,登革热病毒多次独立传入科特迪瓦,主要来自邻国布基纳法索。我们确定了 2017 年至 2019 年间 DENV-1 的 5 次引入,以及 2015 年至 2016 年间 DENV-2 的 4 次引入。DENV-3 显示了两次单独的引入,一次在 2015 年,另一次在 2018 年。值得注意的是,我们还确定了 1977 年左右从印度引入的早期 DENV-1,这表明长距离历史病毒传播可能与殖民和后殖民军事存在有关。这些结果强调需要持续的 基因组监测 来监测病毒 进化 并为旨在控制西非登革热 暴发 的区域公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决西非 基因组监测 工作代表性不足的问题,特别是在象牙海岸,登革病毒 (DENV) 谱系尚未得到系统的表征。该研究试图通过分析来自科特迪瓦和邻国布基纳法索的 DENV 基因组 来填补这一知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究对 2017 年至 2023 年间收集的来自科特迪瓦的 24 个 DENV 基因组 进行了测序,涵盖血清型 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 DENV-3。将这些序列与公开可用的序列相结合,汇集了该国最全面的数据集。
核心方法
使用全球背景数据集进行最大似然和贝叶斯系统发育地理学分析,以重建科特迪瓦 登革病毒 环流的空间和时间动态。
主要结果
该研究揭示了登革热病毒多次独立传入科特迪瓦,主要来自布基纳法索。具体而言,确定了 2017 年至 2019 年期间有 5 次 DENV-1 引入,以及 2015 年至 2016 年期间有 4 次 DENV-2 引入。DENV-3 显示了两次单独的引入,一次在 2015 年,另一次在 2018 年。还发现了 1977 年左右从印度引入的早期 DENV-1,这表明历史上存在长距离病毒传播。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要持续的 基因组监测 来监测病毒 进化 并为旨在控制西非登革热 进化 的区域公共卫生应对措施提供信息。这项研究提供了迄今为止最全面的科特迪瓦 DENV 数据集,强调了 基因组监测 区域合作的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为科特迪瓦及邻近地区的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 登革热策略提供参考。他们还强调了持续的 进化 对监测病毒 进化 并为公共卫生应对措施提供信息的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对公开序列的依赖和潜在的抽样偏差的限制。然而,所收集的综合数据集为 DENV 多次传入科特迪瓦提供了强有力的证据。

Genomic Epidemiology of Dengue Virus in Ivory Coast Reveals Five Decades of Viral Circulation with Repeated Introductions from Burkina Faso

Authors: Xing-Hua Tao, Hervé Alberic Kadjo, Sylla Yahaya, Kamo Emily, Adjogoua Edgard, Marc A. Suchard, Nídia S. Trovão, Emilie Kamo, Nidia S. Trovao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue fever is a rapidly emerging mosquito-borne disease in Africa, yet the continent remains critically underrepresented in genomic surveillance efforts. This disparity is especially pronounced in West Africa, despite phylogenetic studies being concentrated in only a few countries, such as Burkina Faso. Within this broader regional gap, researchers have yet to systematically characterize the dengue virus (DENV) lineages circulating in the Ivory Coast. In this study, we present 24 newly sequenced DENV genomes from Ivory Coast, spanning serotypes DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3, collected between 2017 and 2023. Combined with publicly available sequences, we assembled the most comprehensive dataset to date for the country. We performed maximum-likelihood and Bayesian phylogeographic analyses using global background datasets and reconstructed the spatial and temporal dynamics of dengue virus circulation in Ivory Coast. Our findings reveal multiple independent introductions of DENV into Ivory Coast, predominantly from neighboring Burkina Faso. We identify five introductions of DENV-1 between 2017 and 2019 and four introductions of DENV-2 between 2015 and 2016. DENV-3 showed two separate introductions, one in 2015 and another in 2018. Notably, we also identified an early DENV-1 introduction from India around 1977, suggesting long-distance historical viral movement likely linked to colonial and post-colonial military presence. These results underscore the need for sustained genomic surveillance to monitor viral evolution and inform regional public health responses aimed at controlling dengue outbreaks in West Africa.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the underrepresentation of genomic surveillance efforts in West Africa, particularly in Ivory Coast, where dengue virus (DENV) lineages have not been systematically characterized. The research sought to fill this knowledge gap by analyzing DENV genomes from Ivory Coast and neighboring Burkina Faso.
Design and population
The study involved sequencing 24 DENV genomes from Ivory Coast, spanning serotypes DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-3, collected between 2017 and 2023. These were combined with publicly available sequences to assemble the most comprehensive dataset for the country.
Core methods
Maximum-likelihood and Bayesian phylogeographic analyses were performed using global background datasets to reconstruct the spatial and temporal dynamics of dengue virus circulation in Ivory Coast.
Main results
The study revealed multiple independent introductions of DENV into Ivory Coast, predominantly from Burkina Faso. Specifically, five introductions of DENV-1 between 2017 and 2019 and four introductions of DENV-2 between 2015 and 2016 were identified. DENV-3 showed two separate introductions, one in 2015 and another in 2018. An early DENV-1 introduction from India around 1977 was also identified, suggesting long-distance historical viral movement.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings underscore the need for sustained genomic surveillance to monitor viral evolution and inform regional public health responses aimed at controlling dengue outbreaks in West Africa. This study provides the most comprehensive dataset for DENV in Ivory Coast to date, highlighting the importance of regional collaboration in genomic surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for dengue in Ivory Coast and neighboring regions. They also highlight the importance of sustained genomic surveillance to monitor viral evolution and inform public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on publicly available sequences and the potential for sampling bias. However, the comprehensive dataset assembled provides strong evidence for the multiple introductions of DENV into Ivory Coast.

DYCAST 登革病毒 风险区域预警系统,巴西里贝朗普雷图

DYCAST early warning system for dengue virus risk areas, Ribeirão Preto, Brazil
作者： Ryan M. Carney
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dengue is currently the fastest-spreading vector-borne disease, with an estimated 100-400 million infections per year and nearly 6 billion people at risk – more than half the world’s population.
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究的目的是评估使用人类病例和 DYCAST 的改编来预测登革热风险的有效性:一种基于生物学的时空聚类模型,已证明作为西尼罗河病毒早期预警系统在操作上是成功的。
核心方法
该研究使用疑似病例来评估 DYCAST(一种基于生物学的时空聚类模型)在预测登革热风险方面的性能。
主要结果
结果表明,DYCAST 能够准确、早期地检测巴西里贝朗普雷图的登革热高风险区域,包括严重流行之前的隐秘流行病间焦点 传播。
解释与创新
这些研究结果表明,DYCAST 可用于实时前瞻性地识别登革热高风险区域,以快速、精确地减少蚊子传播和公众教育。
科研与公卫意义
使用免费开源软件的实施为世界各地的登革热预防和控制提供了可扩展的技术策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

DYCAST early warning system for dengue virus risk areas, Ribeirão Preto, Brazil

Authors: Ryan M. Carney
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is currently the fastest-spreading vector-borne disease, with an estimated 100-400 million infections per year and nearly 6 billion people at risk – more than half the world’s population. However, there is no specific antiviral treatment, thus the primary method for preventing infection is through targeted control of the principal mosquito vector, Aedes aegypti . The objective of this retrospective study is to evaluate the efficacy of forecasting dengue risk using human cases and an adaptation of DYCAST: a biologically based, spatiotemporal clustering model that proved operationally successful as a West Nile virus early warning system. Results demonstrate that DYCAST provided accurate and early detection of high-risk areas for dengue in Ribeirão Preto, Brazil, including a cryptic interepidemic focus of transmission preceding a severe epidemic. Using suspected cases, DYCAST predicted 90.3% (4,237/4,690) of confirmed cases, with a mean lead time of 66.3 days prior to illness onset. High-risk cells had >10× higher relative risk of containing a confirmed case, and sensitivity and specificity were 83.8% and 72.3%. These findings suggest that DYCAST could be utilized in real time to prospectively identify areas at high risk of dengue, for rapid and precise targeting of mosquito mitigation and public education. Implementation using free, open-source software offers a scalable technological strategy for the prevention and control of dengue around the world. This article is part of the theme issue ‘The Dengue Prevention Toolbox: Integrating Biological, Social and Technological Strategies for Sustainable Control’.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is currently the fastest-spreading vector-borne disease, with an estimated 100-400 million infections per year and nearly 6 billion people at risk – more than half the world’s population.
Design and population
The objective of this retrospective study is to evaluate the efficacy of forecasting dengue risk using human cases and an adaptation of DYCAST: a biologically based, spatiotemporal clustering model that proved operationally successful as a West Nile virus early warning system.
Core methods
The study used suspected cases to evaluate the performance of DYCAST, a biologically based, spatiotemporal clustering model, in forecasting dengue risk.
Main results

The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

越南父母为儿童和青少年接种 登革热 疫苗的意愿:使用计划行为理论和选定的健康信念模型构建进行的分析。

Parental intention to vaccinate children and adolescents against dengue fever in Vietnam: An analysis using the theory of planned behavior and selected health belief model constructs.
作者： Giao Huynh, Dung Quoc Phan, Quynh Ho Ngoc Huynh, Trinh Thi Diem Le, Lam Thi Phuong Nguyen, Dac Huy Le, Hau Viet Nguyen, Giao Huỳnh, Đắc Huy Lê
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解越南家长为孩子接种 登革热 疫苗的意图,解决没有特定 抗病毒药物 抗病毒药物 治疗 的公共卫生挑战,并确定相关因素。
设计与对象
2025 年 6 月至 10 月,对越南胡志明市五所学校的 1,242 名 4 至 18 岁儿童和青少年家长进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用基于计划行为理论和选定的健康信念模型构建的自填问卷收集数据,并使用多变量逻辑回归进行分析。
主要结果
在 1,242 名参与者中,68.7% 的人表示有意为自己的孩子接种 登革热 疫苗。与较低 疫苗接种 意向相关的因素包括 7-11 岁儿童和较低的自我感知家庭收入,而较高的感知蚊子密度、对 疫苗 的有利态度、更强的主观规范、更强的感知行为控制和更好的登革热预防实践与较高的意向相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,经济、卫生系统和社会因素显着影响父母为孩子接种 登革热 疫苗的意愿,凸显了有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为解决负担能力、社会支持、服务可及性和可信健康沟通问题的策略提供参考,特别是对于 7-11 岁儿童的父母和蚊子密度高的地区的父母。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了建立因果关系的能力,并且样本仅限于越南的特定地区,这可能会影响普遍性。

Parental intention to vaccinate children and adolescents against dengue fever in Vietnam: An analysis using the theory of planned behavior and selected health belief model constructs.

Authors: Giao Huynh, Dung Quoc Phan, Quynh Ho Ngoc Huynh, Trinh Thi Diem Le, Lam Thi Phuong Nguyen, Dac Huy Le, Hau Viet Nguyen, Giao Huỳnh, Đắc Huy Lê
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue fever (DF) is a major public health challenge for which no specific antiviral treatment is currently approved; therefore, prevention is the primary means of control. This study examined parents' intention to vaccinate children and adolescents with the first dengue vaccine approved in Vietnam and the factors associated with this intention. METHODOLOGY/PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: A cross-sectional study was conducted from June to October 2025 among parents of children and adolescents aged 4-18 years in five schools in Ho Chi Minh City, Vietnam. Data were collected using a self-administered questionnaire based on the Theory of Planned Behavior and selected Health Belief Model constructs and analyzed using multivariable logistic regression. Among 1,242 participants, 68.7% reported an intention to vaccinate their children against DF. Compared with parents of children aged 4-6 years, those with children aged 7-11 years had lower odds of vaccination intention (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 0.27, 95% confidence interval [CI] 0.10-0.70). Lower self-perceived household income was associated with reduced intention (aOR = 0.53, 95% CI 0.32-0.85), whereas higher perceived mosquito density around the house (aOR = 1.22, 95% CI 1.05-1.43), favorable attitudes toward the vaccine (aOR = 2.05, 95% CI 1.53-2.75), stronger subjective norms (aOR = 1.32, 95% CI 1.17-1.49), greater perceived behavioral control (aOR = 1.36, 95% CI 1.26-1.47), and better DF prevention practices (aOR = 1.44, 95% CI 1.15-1.80) were independently associated with greater intention to vaccinate. CONCLUSIONS/SIGNIFICANCE: Parents' intention to vaccinate their children against DF was associated with financial, health-system, and social factors. These findings may inform strategies that address affordability, social support, service accessibility, and trusted health communication, particularly for parents of children aged 7-11 years and those in areas with high mosquito density.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand parents' intention to vaccinate their children against dengue fever in Vietnam, addressing a public health challenge where no specific antiviral treatment is available, and to identify associated factors.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from June to October 2025 among 1,242 parents of children and adolescents aged 4-18 years in five schools in Ho Chi Minh City, Vietnam.
Core methods
Data were collected using a self-administered questionnaire based on the Theory of Planned Behavior and selected Health Belief Model constructs and analyzed using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Among 1,242 participants, 68.7% reported an intention to vaccinate their children against dengue fever. Factors associated with lower vaccination intention included children aged 7-11 years and lower self-perceived household income, while higher perceived mosquito density, favorable attitudes toward the vaccine, stronger subjective norms, greater perceived behavioral control, and better dengue prevention practices were associated with higher intention.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that financial, health-system, and social factors significantly influence parents' intention to vaccinate their children against dengue fever, highlighting the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform strategies to address affordability, social support, service accessibility, and trusted health communication, particularly for parents of children aged 7-11 years and those in areas with high mosquito density.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits the ability to establish causality, and the sample was limited to a specific region in Vietnam, which may affect generalizability.

环境颗粒化学成分与登革热 发病率:泰国一项为期 10 年的全国性研究。

Ambient particulate chemical components and dengue incidence: A 10-year nationwide study in Thailand.
作者： Radhakrishnan Muthukumar, Isaraporn Thepwongsa, Udomlack Peansukwech, Kamonwan Jenwitheesuk, Bangonsri Jindawong
摘要中文翻译登革热仍然是热带地区的一个主要公共卫生问题,人们越来越认识到环境决定因素是其 传播 动态的影响因素。泰国经历了反复发作的登革热 暴发 和间歇性空气污染;然而,颗粒化学成分在登革热 流行病学 中的作用仍不清楚。这项研究调查了 2012 年至 2021 年泰国 77 个省份的特定气溶胶成分与登革热 监测 之间的关联。登革热 监测 数据来自公共卫生部,每月气溶胶浓度来自 NASA 的 MERRA-2 系统。采用集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似 (INLA) 的贝叶斯空间建模方法来估计颗粒物成分(包括灰尘 PM2.5、有机碳、黑碳和硫酸盐)与登革热 发病率 之间的关联,并根据人口和时间因素进行调整。研究期间总共记录了 837,616 例登革热病例。较高水平的粉尘 PM2.5 与登革热 发病率 呈正相关,而较高水平的有机碳、黑碳和硫酸盐则与登革热 发病率 呈负相关。这些发现表明,颗粒化学成分可能导致登革热 发病率 的空间变异,尽管潜在机制仍不确定。将颗粒物成分纳入环境 监测 系统可以改善登革热的早期预警模型,特别是在快速城市化的东南亚地区。

Ambient particulate chemical components and dengue incidence: A 10-year nationwide study in Thailand.

Authors: Radhakrishnan Muthukumar, Isaraporn Thepwongsa, Udomlack Peansukwech, Kamonwan Jenwitheesuk, Bangonsri Jindawong
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, and environmental determinants are increasingly recognized as contributors to its transmission dynamics. Thailand experiences both recurrent dengue outbreaks and episodic air pollution; however, the role of particulate chemical components in dengue epidemiology remains unclear. This study examined the association between specific aerosol components and dengue incidence across 77 provinces in Thailand from 2012 to 2021. Dengue surveillance data were obtained from the Ministry of Public Health, and monthly aerosol concentrations were derived from NASA's MERRA-2 system. A Bayesian spatial modelling approach using integrated nested Laplace approximation (INLA) was applied to estimate associations between particulate components, including dust-PM2.5, organic carbon, black carbon, and sulfate, and dengue incidence, adjusting for demographic and temporal factors. A total of 837,616 dengue cases were recorded over the study period. Higher levels of dust-PM2.5 were positively associated with dengue incidence, whereas higher levels of organic carbon, black carbon, and sulfate were inversely associated with dengue incidence. These findings suggest that particulate chemical composition may contribute to spatial variation in dengue incidence, although the underlying mechanisms remain uncertain. Incorporating particulate composition into environmental surveillance systems may improve early warning models for dengue, particularly in rapidly urbanizing regions of Southeast Asia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, and environmental determinants are increasingly recognized as contributors to its transmission dynamics.
Design and population
This study examined the association between specific aerosol components and dengue incidence across 77 provinces in Thailand from 2012 to 2021.
Core methods

Main results

Scientific and public-health significance
Incorporating particulate composition into environmental surveillance systems may improve early warning models for dengue, particularly in rapidly urbanizing regions of Southeast Asia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

泰国免疫功能低下成年人的 重症登革热 风险:一项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究。

Risk of severe dengue in immunocompromised adults in Thailand: A multi-centre retrospective cohort study.
作者： Juthaporn Cowan, Viravarn Luvira, Sivaporn Gatechompol, Pinyo Rattanaumpawan, Punnee Pitisuttithum
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病例报告 表明免疫功能低下患者的严重后果,但缺乏大规模的 队列研究 比较。我们比较了免疫功能低下和非免疫功能低下患者的登革热结果。
设计与对象
对 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 6 月期间实验室确诊的登革热感染患者进行了一项回顾性多中心 队列研究 研究。免疫功能低下患者年龄较大(中位[四分位距]年龄 47.0 [30.0-59.0] vs 33.5 [26.0-49.0] 岁)并且合并症负担更大(查尔森指数 1 [0-3] vs 0 [0-1])。
核心方法
CD4<350个细胞/μL的HIV感染者(PLWH)、接受实体器官移植、2年内造血干细胞移植、活动性恶性肿瘤、脾切除或无脾以及需要免疫抑制剂的情况被归类为免疫功能低下。重症登革热 基于 WHO 2009 年标准,住院情况、住院时间和 30 天 死亡率 是主要结局。
主要结果
总共纳入了 894 名登革热患者,其中 59 名免疫功能低下。临床表现相似,但不发烧更为常见(10.2% vs 1.1%)。实验室检查结果在就诊时具有可比性,但免疫功能低下患者的血小板计数最低点较低且血小板减少时间较长。重症登革热 在免疫功能低下患者中更为常见(22% vs 4.9%),住院率更高,住院时间更长,死亡率 也更高。
解释与创新
这项研究补充了目前免疫功能低下患者 重症登革热 结果风险较高的证据,强调需要改进该患者群体的预防和 重症登革热 策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进免疫功能低下患者的预防策略和 重症登革热 的 重症登革热,这可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施和临床管理方案提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于现有的医疗记录,这可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Risk of severe dengue in immunocompromised adults in Thailand: A multi-centre retrospective cohort study.

Authors: Juthaporn Cowan, Viravarn Luvira, Sivaporn Gatechompol, Pinyo Rattanaumpawan, Punnee Pitisuttithum
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Case reports suggest severe outcomes in immunocompromised patients, but large cohort comparisons are lacking. We compared dengue outcomes between immunocompromised and non-immunocompromised patients. METHODS: A retrospective multicenter cohort study of patients with laboratory-confirmed dengue infection between January 2023 and June 2024 was conducted. People living with HIV (PLWH) with CD4 < 350 cells/μL, recipients of solid organ transplantation, hematopoietic stem cell transplant within 2 years, active malignancy, splenectomy or asplenia, and conditions requiring immunosuppressive agents were classified as immunocompromised. Severe dengue based on WHO 2009 criteria, hospitalization, length of stay, and 30-day mortality were primary outcomes. FINDINGS: A total of 894 dengue patients were included, including 59 were immunocompromised. Immunocompromised patients were older (median [interquartile range] age 47.0 [30.0-59.0] vs 33.5 [26.0-49.0] years) and had greater comorbidity burden (Charlson index 1 [0-3] vs 0 [0-1]). Clinical presentation was similar, although absence of fever was more common (10.2% vs 1.1%). Laboratory findings were comparable at presentation, but immunocompromised patients had lower nadir platelet counts and prolonged thrombocytopenia. Severe dengue was more frequent in immunocompromised patients (22% vs 4.9%), with higher hospitalization rates, longer stays, and mortality. In multivariable analysis, immunocompromised status (OR 3.43, 95% CI 1.61-7.31) and comorbidity index (OR 1.33, 95% CI 1.16-1.52) were independently associated with severe dengue. INTERPRETATION: This study adds to the current evidence of higher risk of severe dengue outcome in immunocompromised highlighting the need for improved strategies for prevention and treatment in this patient population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Case reports suggest severe outcomes in immunocompromised patients, but large cohort comparisons are lacking. We compared dengue outcomes between immunocompromised and non-immunocompromised patients.
Design and population
A retrospective multicenter cohort study of patients with laboratory-confirmed dengue infection between January 2023 and June 2024 was conducted. Immunocompromised patients were older (median [interquartile range] age 47.0 [30.0-59.0] vs 33.5 [26.0-49.0] years) and had greater comorbidity burden (Charlson index 1 [0-3] vs 0 [0-1]).
Core methods
People living with HIV (PLWH) with CD4 < 350 cells/μL, recipients of solid organ transplantation, hematopoietic stem cell transplant within 2 years, active malignancy, splenectomy or asplenia, and conditions requiring immunosuppressive agents were classified as immunocompromised. Severe dengue based on WHO 2009 criteria, hospitalization, length of stay, and 30-day mortality were primary outcomes.
Main results
A total of 894 dengue patients were included, including 59 were immunocompromised. Clinical presentation was similar, although absence of fever was more common (10.2% vs 1.1%). Laboratory findings were comparable at presentation, but immunocompromised patients had lower nadir platelet counts and prolonged thrombocytopenia. Severe dengue was more frequent in immunocompromised patients (22% vs 4.9%), with higher hospitalization rates, longer stays, and mortality.
Interpretation and novelty
This study adds to the current evidence of higher risk of severe dengue outcome in immunocompromised highlighting the need for improved strategies for prevention and treatment in this patient population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved strategies for prevention and treatment of severe dengue in immunocompromised patients, which could inform targeted public health interventions and clinical management protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on existing medical records, which may introduce bias and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

具有 疫苗接种 和饱和 发病率 速率的多阶段登革热模型的随机动力学。

Stochastic dynamics of a multi-stage dengue model with vaccination and saturated incidence rates.
作者： S P Rajasekar, M Manju Priya
摘要中文翻译复杂的人口流动和固有的破坏性环境因素使得分析致病性 登革病毒 更具挑战性。流行病数学框架的研究除了预测登革热疫情的发展模式外,还可能有助于保护经济和公共卫生。受此影响,我们讨论六维S h V h I h R h -S v I v登革热随机流行病模型。对于这个随机系统,我们首先验证全局正解的存在性和唯一性。接下来,我们构造合适的李亚普诺夫函数,用哈斯明斯基方法分析具有遍历特征的平稳分布的存在性和唯一性。使用概率密度函数研究现有平稳分布的统计报告。由此得出,对数正态分布特性的准地方性平衡附近的概率密度函数的表达式可以通过福克-普朗克方程求解。此外,还获得了根除和控制登革热传播的充分条件。随后,进行数值模拟以验证数学预测,并考虑环境干扰和关键参数对登革热传播的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决复杂的人口流动和环境破坏在分析致病性 登革病毒 时带来的挑战。该研究的重点是开发一个数学模型来理解登革热 传播 动力学。
设计与对象
该研究采用六维随机流行病模型(S h V h I h R h -S v I v)来模拟登革热传播。该模型的设计考虑了环境干扰和影响登革热传播的关键参数。
核心方法
这些方法涉及验证随机系统的全局正解的存在性和唯一性。使用 Khasminskii 的方法构建了合适的 Lyapunov 函数来分析平稳分布。进行数值模拟以验证数学预测。
主要结果
该研究证明了具有遍历特征的平稳分布的存在。使用福克-普朗克方程求解接近准地方性平衡的概率密度函数,揭示了对数正态特性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为为理解随机条件下登革热 传播 动力学提供了一个框架。新颖之处在于用 疫苗接种 和饱和 发病率 比率来模拟登革热传播的数学方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的登革热 监测 数学模型和预测提供信息。它们还可能有助于制定在动态环境中控制登革热 传播 的更好策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于数学模拟,不提供经验数据。由于结果基于理论模型和数值验证,因此证据强度中等。

Stochastic dynamics of a multi-stage dengue model with vaccination and saturated incidence rates.

Authors: S P Rajasekar, M Manju Priya
Original abstract (English)
Complex population movements and inherent disruptive environmental factors render it more challenging to analyse the pathogenic dengue virus. Research on epidemic mathematical frameworks may assist in protecting the economy and public health in addition to predicting the dengue epidemic's development pattern. Influenced by this, we discuss the six-dimensional S h V h I h R h -S v I v dengue stochastic epidemic model. For this stochastic system, we first verify the existence and uniqueness of a positive global solution. Next, we construct the suitable Lyapunov function to analyse the existence and uniqueness of a stationary distribution with ergodic features by Khasminskii's method. To investigate the statistical report for the existing stationary distribution by using a probability density function. This yields, the expression of the probability density function near quasi-endemic equilibrium of log-normal properties is solved by the Fokker-Planck equation. Furthermore, sufficient conditions are derived to eradicate and control the dengue transmission. Subsequently, numerical simulations are executed to validate the mathematical predictions, and the impact of environmental disturbances and key parameters on dengue transmission is taken into account as well.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges posed by complex population movements and environmental disruptions in analysing the pathogenic dengue virus. The research focused on developing a mathematical model to understand dengue transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The study employed a six-dimensional stochastic epidemic model (S h V h I h R h -S v I v) to simulate dengue transmission. The model was designed to incorporate environmental disturbances and key parameters influencing dengue spread.
Core methods
The methods involved verifying the existence and uniqueness of a positive global solution for the stochastic system. A suitable Lyapunov function was constructed using Khasminskii's method to analyse the stationary distribution. Numerical simulations were executed to validate the mathematical predictions.
Main results
The study demonstrated the existence of a stationary distribution with ergodic features. The probability density function near quasi-endemic equilibrium was solved using the Fokker-Planck equation, revealing log-normal properties.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as providing a framework for understanding dengue transmission dynamics under stochastic conditions. The novelty lies in the mathematical approach to model dengue spread with vaccination and saturated incidence rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate mathematical models for dengue surveillance and prediction. They may also contribute to better strategies for controlling dengue transmission in dynamic environments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mathematical simulations and does not provide empirical data. The evidence strength is moderate as the results are based on theoretical models and numerical validations.

通过 CRISPR 工具和药理抑制来靶向 CLK1 可调节先天的 免疫应答 和 登革病毒 复制。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.
作者： Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
摘要中文翻译选择性剪接是已知在病毒感染时发生改变的关键调节机制。这些改变可能来自病毒对剪接机制的直接干扰,也可能来自细胞反应,例如先天免疫的激活。在这里,我们研究了培养细胞中 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和干扰素 (IFN) 治疗 之间共享的剪接变化,后者作为病毒依赖性先天免疫激活的模型。在常见事件中,我们发现 CLK1 基因非编码 mRNA 亚型的产量增加,从而产生一种磷酸化剪接因子(包括 SR 蛋白和剪接体成分)的激酶。与这一发现一致,IFN 治疗 导致 CLK1 蛋白水平降低。使用具有 CRISPR/dCas9 介导的 CLK1 表达调节的稳定细胞系,我们发现沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过度表达则减弱了这种诱导。用药理学抑制剂 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性,进一步增强 IFN 诱导的基因表达并减少 DENV 复制。总而言之,这些结果确定 CLK1 是 IFN 刺激的基因表达的前病毒负调节因子,并表明其抑制可以增强 抗病毒药物 防御并成为治疗策略的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨病毒感染和干扰素 治疗 如何影响剪接变化,因为选择性剪接是病毒感染过程中被改变的关键调控机制。
设计与对象
本研究使用培养的细胞来研究 登革病毒(登革病毒)感染和干扰素(IFN) 治疗 对剪接变化的影响,其中 IFN 治疗 作为病毒独立的先天免疫激活模型。
核心方法
研究人员使用 CRISPR/dCas9 工具调节 CLK1 表达,并使用药物抑制剂评估 CLK1 激酶活性对 免疫应答 基因和登革病毒复制的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,在登革病毒感染和 IFN 治疗 激活过程中,CLK1 基因表达均增加,但 IFN 治疗 导致 CLK1 蛋白水平下降。沉默 CLK1 增强了 免疫应答 基因的诱导,而其过表达则抑制了该诱导。使用 TG003 抑制 CLK1 激酶活性进一步增强了干扰素诱导的基因表达,并减少了登革病毒的复制。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 CLK1 在干扰素刺激基因表达中起负调控作用,其抑制可能有助于增强 抗病毒药物 防御能力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为针对 CLK1 的潜在治疗策略提供了见解,以增强 抗病毒药物 对登革病毒的防御能力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于培养细胞,可能无法完全代表体内情况,且证据基于实验性调节而非直接观察病毒在人体中的行为。证据强度为中等。

Targeting CLK1 by CRISPR tools and pharmacological inhibition modulates the innate immune response and dengue virus replication.

Authors: Berta Pozzi, Agustina Magalí Lucero, Valeria Buggiano, Miguel Angel Pelaez, Cybele Carina García, Anabella Srebrow
Original abstract (English)
Alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism known to be altered upon viral infection. These alterations can arise both from direct viral interference with the splicing machinery and from cellular responses such as activation of innate immunity. Here, we investigated splicing changes shared between dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment in cultured cells, the latter serving as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation. Among the common events, we identified an increased production of non-coding mRNA isoforms from the CLK1 gene, which gives rise to a kinase that phosphorylates splicing factors including SR proteins and spliceosomal components. Consistent with this finding, IFN treatment led to a reduction in CLK1 protein levels. Using stable cell lines with CRISPR/dCas9-mediated modulation of CLK1 expression, we found that silencing CLK1 enhanced the induction of immune response genes, while its overexpression attenuated it. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with the pharmacological inhibitor TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication. Altogether, these results identify CLK1 as a proviral negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression and suggest that its inhibition could enhance antiviral defenses and become a target for therapeutic strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how viral infection and interferon treatment affect splicing changes, as alternative splicing is a key regulatory mechanism altered during viral infection.
Design and population
The study used cultured cells to investigate splicing changes in dengue virus (DENV) infection and interferon (IFN) treatment, with IFN treatment serving as a model for virus-independent innate immune activation.
Core methods
The researchers employed CRISPR/dCas9 tools to modulate CLK1 expression and pharmacological inhibitors to assess the impact of CLK1 kinase activity on immune response genes and DENV replication.
Main results
The study found that CLK1 gene expression increased during DENV infection and IFN treatment, with IFN treatment leading to a reduction in CLK1 protein levels. Silencing CLK1 enhanced immune response gene induction, while its overexpression attenuated it. Inhibition of CLK1 kinase activity with TG003 further potentiated IFN-induced gene expression and reduced DENV replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that CLK1 acts as a negative regulator of IFN-stimulated gene expression and that its inhibition could enhance antiviral defenses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into potential therapeutic strategies targeting CLK1 to improve antiviral defenses against dengue virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to cultured cells, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the evidence is based on experimental modulation rather than direct observation of viral behavior in humans. The evidence strength is moderate.

具有饱和非线性 发病率 和双时滞的 登革热 模型的分岔分析和最优控制

Bifurcation analysis and optimal control of a Dengue fever model with saturated nonlinear incidence and dual time delays
作者： Akeem O. Yunus, John O. Akanni, Oludolapo A. Olanrewaju, Akeem Yunus, John Olajide Akanni, Oludolapo Akanni Olanrewaju
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是一个严峻的公共卫生挑战,数学模型为理解其动态和指导控制工作提供了宝贵的视角。本研究开发并分析了一个七室确定性模型,该模型通过耦合的 传播 途径将人类和蚊子种群联系起来。该模型融合了以前在登革热文献中未结合的几个特征:饱和的 发病率 函数,可捕获 传播 两个方向上的拥挤效应,两个生物学上不同的孵化延迟分别代表病毒在人类和蚊子中度过的时间,恢复后暂时丧失免疫力,以及三部分最优控制框架。在基线条件下,导出并估计控制 暴发 潜力的阈值数量约为 2.37。当该阈值降至或低于 1 时,无病平衡显示为全局渐近稳定;当它超过一时,就会出现一种独特的地方性平衡;对于完整的 DDE 模型,分析地建立了其局部渐近稳定性,并在极限 ODE 情况下证明了全局稳定性(无延迟、无饱和、无免疫减弱)。一个值得注意的发现是,即使阈值低于 1,系统也可以维持地方性 传播,前提是饱和常数满足 α > α † 和逐渐减弱的免疫率 δ h > 0 共同作用,这一结果对根除策略有直接影响。对潜伏延迟的分析表明,超过大约 9.4 天的临界值后,系统就会转变为持续振荡,这为东南亚登革热数据中常见的季节性周期提供了机械解释。敏感性分析强调蚊子 死亡率 是最有影响力的参数。通过分析得出的最佳控制解决方案表明,组合干预策略比单一措施方法更有效。数值模拟验证了所有理论结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是一个严峻的公共卫生挑战,数学模型为理解其动态和指导控制工作提供了宝贵的视角。
设计与对象
本研究开发并分析了一个七室确定性模型,该模型通过耦合的 传播 途径将人类和蚊子种群联系起来。
核心方法
该模型融合了以前在登革热文献中未结合的几个特征:饱和的 发病率 函数,可捕获 传播 两个方向上的拥挤效应,两个生物学上不同的孵化延迟分别代表病毒在人类和蚊子中度过的时间,恢复后暂时丧失免疫力,以及三部分最优控制框架。
主要结果
在基线条件下,导出并估计控制 暴发 潜力的阈值数量约为 2.37。当该阈值降至或低于 1 时,无病平衡显示为全局渐近稳定;当它超过一时,就会出现一种独特的地方性平衡;对于完整的 DDE 模型,分析地建立了其局部渐近稳定性,并在极限 ODE 情况下证明了全局稳定性(无延迟、无饱和、无免疫减弱)。
解释与创新
一个值得注意的发现是,即使阈值低于 1,系统也可以维持地方性 传播,前提是饱和常数满足 α > α † 和逐渐减弱的免疫率 δ h > 0 共同作用,这一结果对根除策略有直接影响。
科研与公卫意义
对潜伏延迟的分析表明,超过大约 9.4 天的临界值后,系统就会转变为持续振荡,这为东南亚登革热数据中常见的季节性周期提供了机械解释。

Bifurcation analysis and optimal control of a Dengue fever model with saturated nonlinear incidence and dual time delays

Authors: Akeem O. Yunus, John O. Akanni, Oludolapo A. Olanrewaju, Akeem Yunus, John Olajide Akanni, Oludolapo Akanni Olanrewaju
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a serious public health challenge, and mathematical models offer a valuable lens for understanding its dynamics and guiding control efforts. This study develops and analyses a seven-compartment deterministic model that links human and mosquito populations through coupled transmission pathways. The model incorporates several features not previously combined in the dengue literature: a saturated incidence function that captures crowding effects in both transmission directions, two biologically distinct incubation delays representing the time the virus spends in humans and in mosquitoes separately, gradual loss of temporary immunity after recovery, and a three-component optimal control framework. The threshold quantity governing outbreak potential is derived and estimated at approximately 2.37 under baseline conditions. When this threshold falls to or below one, the disease-free equilibrium is shown to be globally asymptotically stable; when it exceeds one, a unique endemic equilibrium emerges; its local asymptotic stability is established analytically for the full DDE model, and global stability is proved for the limiting ODE case (no delays, no saturation, no waning immunity). A notable finding is that the system can sustain endemic transmission even when the threshold is driven below one, provided that the saturation constant satisfies α > α † and the waning immunity rate δ h > 0 act together—a result with direct implications for eradication strategies. Analysis of the incubation delay reveals that beyond a critical value of roughly 9.4 days, the system transitions into sustained oscillations, offering a mechanistic explanation for the seasonal cycles commonly observed in Southeast Asian dengue data. Sensitivity analysis highlights mosquito mortality as the most influential parameter. Optimal control solutions, derived analytically, point to combined intervention strategies as substantially more effective than single-measure approaches. Numerical simulations validate all theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever remains a serious public health challenge, and mathematical models offer a valuable lens for understanding its dynamics and guiding control efforts.
Design and population

Journal: Biology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-20  |  DOI: 10.3390/biology15222143  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15, 22, 2143
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

丙型肝炎和 抗病毒药物 感染界面的 Reticulon-3:细胞外囊泡介导的转移和新出现的 抗病毒药物 漏洞

Reticulon-3 at the Interface of Hepatitis C and Dengue Virus Infection: Extracellular Vesicle-Mediated Transfer and Emerging Antiviral Vulnerabilities
作者： Tatiana Noumi, Razieh Bitazar, Clinton Njinju Asaba, Labonte Patrick, Terence Ndonyi Bukong, Patrick Labonté
摘要中文翻译正义RNA病毒通常按基因组组织、进入途径、组织向性和临床综合征进行分类,但这些分类并不能解释病毒如何构建受保护的复制生态位或在细胞之间传递感染物质。内质网(ER)为病毒翻译、复制细胞器形成、膜曲率、脂质重塑、先天免疫调节和囊泡运输提供了一个共享平台。在此框架内,reticulon-3 (RTN3) 已成为将基于内质网的 RNA 病毒复制与感染性细胞外囊泡货物的生成联系起来的候选调节因子。丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 为评估该模型提供了互补的实验设置,尽管它们具有不同的 传播 途径、组织向性、疾病过程和免疫病理生理学。HCV 最清楚地表明,富含外泌体的组分可以转移具有定义的 宿主 辅因子的具有复制能力的病毒 RNA,而 DENV 提供了用于检查相关转移表型的急性感染模型。然而,这两种病毒都在重塑的内质网膜内复制,并且可以以囊泡相关的形式转移具有感染能力的物质。功能扰动研究表明 RTN3 参与感染相关囊泡制剂的输出,但尚未建立对病毒 RNA 或其掺入内体腔内囊泡的直接识别。RTN3 还可以通过 NS4B 相互作用限制 HCV 复制,这表明其作用因终点而异,并且可能反映感染不同阶段的不同功能。寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒、肠道病毒、SARS-CoV-2和甲型肝炎病毒中相关的膜重塑和囊泡相关过程说明了 宿主 膜组织的更广泛利用,但这些系统中的复制膜表型和囊泡相关传播并没有建立依赖于 RTN3 的传染性 EV 途径。在选定的模型中,富含EV的制剂含有受保护的病毒货物并将生物活性转移至受体细胞;单独的颗粒富集和感染性都无法解析载体的身份。分离和表征方面的进步,包括密度梯度或尺寸排阻分离、免疫亲和选择、颗粒和标记物分析、膜保护测定和受体细胞感染性测试,现在允许更严格地鉴定确定的囊泡群体、受保护的货物和生物活性。然而,RTN3 不应被视为普遍的 RNA 病毒依赖性,也不应假设细胞外囊泡释放会产生一致的感染群体。相反,HCV 和 DENV 支持 RTN3 在囊泡相关输出中的功能参与,而 ER 组织和选择性货物外壳之间的分子联系仍未解决。定义这些共享的和病毒特异性的依赖性可能会揭示适合广谱或耐药性干预的宿主-病毒界面,前提是可以证明感染选择性依赖性、可测量的治疗窗以及基本宿主-细胞功能的保留。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索病毒如何构建受保护的复制生态位并在细胞之间转移感染物质,强调当前基于 基因组 组织、进入途径、组织向性和临床综合征的分类系统的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究利用丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 作为补充模型来评估 reticulon-3 (RTN3) 在病毒复制和细胞外囊泡介导的转移中的作用,尽管它们具有不同的 传播 途径、组织向性和疾病过程。
核心方法
该研究采用了先进的分离和表征技术,包括密度梯度或尺寸排阻分离、免疫亲和选择、颗粒和标记物分析、膜保护测定和受体细胞感染性测试,以鉴定确定的囊泡群体、受保护的货物和生物活性。
主要结果
HCV和DENV都在重塑的内质网膜内复制,并且可以以囊泡相关形式转移具有感染能力的物质,但内质网组织和选择性货物外壳之间的分子联系仍未解决。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RTN3 在 HCV 和 DENV 的囊泡相关输出中发挥功能性作用,但它的参与并不普遍存在于所有 RNA 病毒中,并且货物选择的精确机制尚未建立。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,了解RTN3在病毒复制和传播中的作用可以为针对宿主-病毒相互作用的广谱抗病毒药物干预措施的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认RTN3不是一种普遍的RNA病毒依赖性,并且细胞外囊泡的释放并不能保证均匀的感染群体,这表明需要进一步的研究来证实这些机制。

Reticulon-3 at the Interface of Hepatitis C and Dengue Virus Infection: Extracellular Vesicle-Mediated Transfer and Emerging Antiviral Vulnerabilities

Authors: Tatiana Noumi, Razieh Bitazar, Clinton Njinju Asaba, Labonte Patrick, Terence Ndonyi Bukong, Patrick Labonté
Original abstract (English)
Positive-sense RNA viruses are commonly classified by genome organization, entry route, tissue tropism, and clinical syndrome, but these categories do not explain how viruses construct protected replication niches or route infectious material between cells. The endoplasmic reticulum (ER) provides a shared platform for viral translation, replication-organelle formation, membrane curvature, lipid remodeling, innate immune regulation, and vesicular trafficking. Within this framework, reticulon-3 (RTN3) has emerged as a candidate regulator linking ER-based RNA-virus replication to the generation of infectious extracellular-vesicle cargo. Hepatitis C virus (HCV) and dengue virus (DENV) provide complementary experimental settings for evaluating this model despite their distinct transmission routes, tissue tropisms, disease courses, and immune pathophysiology. HCV most clearly demonstrates that exosome-enriched fractions can transfer replication-competent viral RNA with defined host cofactors, whereas DENV provides an acute infection model for examining related transfer phenotypes. Both viruses nevertheless replicate within remodeled ER membranes and can transfer infection-competent material in a vesicle-associated form. Functional perturbation studies implicate RTN3 in the output of infection-associated vesicle preparations, but do not yet establish direct recognition of viral RNA or its incorporation into endosomal intraluminal vesicles. RTN3 can also restrict HCV replication through NS4B interactions, suggesting that its effects vary by endpoint and may reflect distinct functions at different stages of infection. Related membrane-remodeling and vesicle-associated processes in Zika virus, West Nile virus, enteroviruses, SARS-CoV-2, and hepatitis A virus illustrate broader exploitation of host membrane organization, but replication-membrane phenotypes and vesicle-associated dissemination in these systems do not establish an RTN3-dependent infectious EV pathway. In selected models, EV-enriched preparations contain protected viral cargo and transfer biological activity to recipient cells; neither particle enrichment nor infectivity alone resolves the carrier’s identity. Advances in isolation and characterization, including density-gradient or size-exclusion separation, immunoaffinity selection, particle and marker profiling, membrane-protection assays, and recipient-cell infectivity testing, now permit more rigorous identification of defined vesicle populations, protected cargo, and biological activity. Nevertheless, RTN3 should not be considered a universal RNA-virus dependency, nor should extracellular-vesicle release be assumed to generate uniformly infectious populations. Instead, HCV and DENV support functional involvement of RTN3 in vesicle-associated output, while the molecular connection between ER organization and selective cargo enclosure remains unresolved. Defining these shared and virus-specific dependencies may reveal host–virus interfaces suitable for broad-spectrum or resistance-resilient intervention, provided that infection-selective reliance, a measurable therapeutic window, and preservation of essential host-cell functions can be demonstrated.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how viruses construct protected replication niches and transfer infectious material between cells, highlighting the limitations of current classification systems based on genome organization, entry route, tissue tropism, and clinical syndrome.
Design and population
The study utilized Hepatitis C virus (HCV) and dengue virus (DENV) as complementary models to evaluate the role of reticulon-3 (RTN3) in viral replication and extracellular-vesicle-mediated transfer, despite their distinct transmission routes, tissue tropisms, and disease courses.
Core methods
The research employed advanced isolation and characterization techniques, including density-gradient or size-exclusion separation, immunoaffinity selection, particle and marker profiling, membrane-protection assays, and recipient-cell infectivity testing to identify defined vesicle populations, protected cargo, and biological activity.
Main results
HCV and DENV both replicate within remodeled ER membranes and can transfer infection-competent material in a vesicle-associated form, but the molecular connection between ER organization and selective cargo enclosure remains unresolved.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that RTN3 plays a functional role in vesicle-associated output for HCV and DENV, but its involvement is not universal across all RNA viruses, and the precise mechanism of cargo selection is not yet established.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that understanding RTN3's role in viral replication and dissemination could inform the development of broad-spectrum antiviral interventions targeting host-virus interactions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that RTN3 is not a universal RNA-virus dependency, and extracellular-vesicle release does not guarantee uniformly infectious populations, indicating the need for further research to confirm these mechanisms.

在新喀里多尼亚部署五年后,沃尔巴克氏体介导的埃及伊蚊 登革病毒 持续阻断

Persistent Wolbachia-mediated dengue virus blocking in Aedes aegypti five years after deployment in New Caledonia
作者： Benjamin Dupuis, Anna‐Bella Failloux, Nicolas Pocquet
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 沃尔巴克氏体感染可减少埃及伊蚊的 登革病毒 (DENV) 传播,但这种效应背后的时间动态在现场相关蚊子种群中仍未完全表征。在这里,我们比较了沃尔巴克氏体感染的伊蚊之间的 DENV 媒介 能力和病毒复制动力学。来自努美阿的埃及伊蚊和来自新喀里多尼亚乌韦亚的无沃尔巴克氏体的种群。方法 雌性蚊子经口感染 DENV-2,并在感染后 3、7、14、21、28 和 35 天(dpi)采样。对感染、传播和 传播 进行了评估,并对腹部、头胸部和唾液中的病毒滴度进行了定量。使用逻辑回归分析 媒介 能力终点,而使用线性模型分析病毒滴度。根据 传播 动力学估计外源潜伏期 (EIP)。结果 在所有时间点上,与乌韦亚无沃尔巴克氏体蚊群相比,努美阿感染沃尔巴克氏体的蚊子中的 DENV 感染、传播和 传播 均显着减少,检测到感染的蚊子分别为 10.1% 和 86.7%,传播分别为 3.4% 和 75.8%,传播 分别为 1.7% 和 31.5%。调整 dpi 后,努美阿的感染、传播和 传播 的比值比大幅降低。人群对感染的影响随时间显着变化,而没有检测到传播或 传播 的人群 × dpi 交互作用。与乌韦阿相比,努美阿的估计 EIP 25 和 EIP 50 延迟,分别达到 23.9 和 24.5 天,而乌韦阿则为 14.4 和 17.6 天。努美阿的病毒滴度也较低,腹部和头胸部存在显着的群体× dpi 相互作用。尽管比较受到几个时间点病毒阳性努美阿蚊子数量极少的限制,但在唾液中没有检测到显着的相互作用。结论 沃尔巴克氏体感染在野外释放五年后,强烈降低了努美阿种群中的 DENV 媒介 能力,媒介 能力降低,病毒滴度降低。这些发现表明,即使在野外条件下长时间使用后,仍能持续发挥功能性病原体阻断作用,并支持基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热控制的持续有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解新喀里多尼亚野外释放五年后,沃尔巴克氏体介导的 登革病毒 (DENV) 阻断埃及伊蚊种群的时间动态,解决沃尔巴克氏体对 DENV 传播 的不完全表征的影响。
设计与对象
该研究比较了沃尔巴克氏体感染的伊蚊之间的 DENV 媒介 能力和病毒复制动力学。来自努美阿的埃及伊蚊和来自新喀里多尼亚乌韦亚的无沃尔巴克氏体种群,在野外释放后的五年内。
核心方法
雌性蚊子经口感染 DENV-2,并在感染后的不同时间点取样。对感染、传播和 传播 进行了评估,并使用逻辑回归和线性模型对不同身体部位的病毒滴度进行了定量。根据 传播 动力学估计外源潜伏期 (EIP)。
主要结果
在所有时间点上,与来自乌韦亚的无沃尔巴克氏体蚊群相比,努美阿感染沃尔巴克氏体的蚊子中的 DENV 感染、传播和 传播 均显着减少。努美阿的感染、传播和 传播 的比值比大幅降低。努美阿人口的 EIP 也被推迟。努美阿人群的病毒滴度较低,腹部和头胸部存在显着的人群× dpi 相互作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在野外释放五年后,努美阿种群中的登革热病毒持续发挥功能性病原体阻断作用,支持基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热控制的持续有效性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热控制策略的长期有效性具有影响,表明 DENV 传播 的减少在野外条件下持续较长时间。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括潜在的混杂因素以及需要更大的样本量来确认人口对感染随时间的影响。由于该研究的观察性质以及努美阿种群在几个时间点病毒呈阳性的蚊子数量有限,证据强度中等。

Persistent Wolbachia-mediated dengue virus blocking in Aedes aegypti five years after deployment in New Caledonia

Authors: Benjamin Dupuis, Anna‐Bella Failloux, Nicolas Pocquet
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Wolbachia infection reduces dengue virus (DENV) transmission by Aedes aegypti , but the temporal dynamics underlying this effect remain incompletely characterized in field-relevant mosquito populations. Here, we compare DENV vector competence and viral replication kinetics between Wolbachia -infected Ae. aegypti from Noumea and a Wolbachia -free population from Ouvea, New Caledonia. Methods Female mosquitoes were orally infected with DENV-2 and sampled at 3, 7, 14, 21, 28, and 35 days post-infection (dpi). Infection, dissemination, and transmission were assessed, and viral titers were quantified in the abdomen, head-thorax, and saliva. Vector competence endpoints were analyzed using logistic regression, while viral titers were analyzed using linear models. The extrinsic incubation period (EIP) was estimated from transmission kinetics. Results Across all time points, DENV infection, dissemination, and transmission were markedly reduced in Wolbachia -infected mosquitoes from Noumea compared with Wolbachia -free population from Ouvea, with infection detected in 10.1% versus 86.7% of mosquitoes, dissemination in 3.4% versus 75.8%, and transmission in 1.7% versus 31.5%, respectively. After adjustment for dpi, the odds ratio of infection, dissemination, and transmission were strongly reduced in Noumea. The population effect on infection varied significantly over time, whereas no population × dpi interaction was detected for dissemination or transmission. The estimated EIP 25 and EIP 50 were delayed in Noumea compared with Ouvea, reaching 23.9 and 24.5 days, respectively, versus 14.4 and 17.6 days in Ouvea. Viral titers were also lower in Noumea, with significant population × dpi interactions in the abdomen and head-thorax. No significant interaction was detected in saliva, although comparisons were limited by the very low number of virus-positive Noumea mosquitoes at several time points. Conclusions Wolbachia infection strongly reduced DENV vector competence in the Noumea population five years after field release, with reduced vector competence and reduced viral titers. These findings demonstrate sustained functional pathogen blocking even after long period in field conditions and support the continued effectiveness of Wolbachia -based dengue control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the temporal dynamics of Wolbachia-mediated dengue virus (DENV) blocking in Aedes aegypti populations five years after field release in New Caledonia, addressing the incompletely characterized effect of Wolbachia on DENV transmission.
Design and population
The study compared DENV vector competence and viral replication kinetics between Wolbachia-infected Ae. aegypti from Noumea and a Wolbachia-free population from Ouvea, New Caledonia, over a period of five years post-field release.
Core methods
Female mosquitoes were orally infected with DENV-2 and sampled at various time points post-infection. Infection, dissemination, and transmission were assessed, and viral titers were quantified in different body parts using logistic regression and linear models. The extrinsic incubation period (EIP) was estimated from transmission kinetics.
Main results
Across all time points, DENV infection, dissemination, and transmission were significantly reduced in Wolbachia-infected mosquitoes from Noumea compared to the Wolbachia-free population from Ouvea. The odds ratio of infection, dissemination, and transmission were strongly reduced in Noumea. The EIP was also delayed in the Noumea population. Viral titers were lower in the Noumea population, with significant population × dpi interactions in the abdomen and head-thorax.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate sustained functional pathogen blocking of DENV in the Noumea population five years after field release, supporting the continued effectiveness of Wolbachia-based dengue control.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for the long-term effectiveness of Wolbachia-based dengue control strategies, indicating that the reduction in DENV transmission persists over extended periods in field conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the potential for confounding factors and the need for larger sample sizes to confirm the population effect on infection over time. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the limited number of virus-positive mosquitoes in the Noumea population at several time points.

抗病毒药物 暹罗花乙醇提取物对 登革病毒 血清型 2 感染的活性

Antiviral activity of Clinacanthus siamensis Bremek ethanolic extract against dengue virus serotype 2 infection
作者： Somluethai Fungfueang, Natthanich Boonsatit, Saruda Thongyim, Angkhana Inta, George S. Baillie, Thawornchai Limjindaporn, Pa-thai Yenchitsomanus, Aussara Panya
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 感染会导致 登革热,并可能发展为严重的危及生命的疾病。目前,尚无特定的 抗病毒药物 疗法可用,尽管 疫苗 存在,但其在 DENV 血清型中的不同疗效引起了安全性担忧。因此,鉴定有效的 抗病毒药物 药物对于降低疾病严重程度和限制病毒 抗病毒药物 仍然至关重要。本研究评估了泰国药草 Clinacanthus siamensis 对抗 DENV 感染的 抗病毒药物 活性。使用体外 DENV 感染测定评估 C. siamensis 的乙醇提取物,并显示出显着的 抗病毒药物 活性,IC50 值为 19.32 μg/mL。使用添加时间实验在病毒生命周期的多个阶段评估提取物的抑制机制,包括预治疗、共治疗和感染后条件,其中病毒分别在病毒进入之前、期间和之后接触提取物。治疗 前条件表现出最高水平的抑制,提取物在浓度为 15.625、31.25 和 62.5 μg/mL 时,将病毒蛋白水平分别降低至 92.79 ± 7.74%、60.30 ± 9.79% 和 12.52 ± 10.18%。这些发现表明,提取物主要在感染的早期阶段发挥其 抗病毒药物 活性。这一观察结果得到了病毒结合测定的进一步支持,病毒结合测定表明,用提取物预处理病毒颗粒会损害其随后与 宿主 细胞结合的能力,这表明对病毒颗粒的直接影响可能有助于降低病毒感染性。此外,使用 LC-MS/MS 分析对提取物的生物活性成分进行了表征,确定了两种具有报道的 抗病毒药物 活性的化合物,包括异阿魏酸和木犀草素,这可能有助于观察到的抗 DENV 作用。这些研究结果表明,暹罗念珠菌通过降低感染早期的病毒感染性而具有有效的抗登革热病毒活性,并强调了暹罗念珠菌作为一种负担得起的针对登革热病毒感染的治疗候选药物的未来发展潜力。

问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 感染会导致 登革热,并可能发展为严重的危及生命的疾病。目前,尚无特定的 抗病毒药物 疗法可用,尽管 疫苗 存在,但其在 DENV 血清型中的不同疗效引起了安全性担忧。
设计与对象
本研究评估了泰国药草 Clinacanthus siamensis 对抗 DENV 感染的 抗病毒药物 活性。使用体外 DENV 感染测定法对暹罗棒虫的乙醇提取物进行了评估。
核心方法
使用添加时间实验在病毒生命周期的多个阶段评估提取物的抑制机制,包括预治疗、共治疗和感染后条件,其中病毒分别在病毒进入之前、期间和之后接触提取物。使用 LC-MS/MS 分析对提取物的生物活性成分进行了表征。
主要结果
治疗 前条件表现出最高水平的抑制,提取物在浓度为 15.625、31.25 和 62.5 μg/mL 时,将病毒蛋白水平分别降低至 92.79 ± 7.74%、60.30 ± 9.79% 和 12.52 ± 10.18%。该提取物表现出显着的 抗病毒药物 活性,IC50 值为 19.32 μg/mL。这一观察结果得到了病毒结合测定的进一步支持,病毒结合测定表明,用提取物预处理病毒颗粒会损害其随后与 宿主 细胞结合的能力。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,提取物主要在感染的早期阶段发挥其 抗病毒药物 活性。该研究强调了暹罗念珠菌作为一种经济实惠的登革热病毒感染治疗候选药物的未来发展潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,暹罗念珠菌通过在感染早期降低病毒感染性而具有有效的抗 DENV 活性,并强调了念珠菌的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Antiviral activity of Clinacanthus siamensis Bremek ethanolic extract against dengue virus serotype 2 infection

Authors: Somluethai Fungfueang, Natthanich Boonsatit, Saruda Thongyim, Angkhana Inta, George S. Baillie, Thawornchai Limjindaporn, Pa-thai Yenchitsomanus, Aussara Panya
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) infection causes dengue fever and can progress to severe, life-threatening diseases. Currently, no specific antiviral therapy is available, and although vaccines exist, their variable efficacy across DENV serotypes raises safety concerns. Therefore, the identification of effective antiviral agents remains critical for reducing disease severity and limiting viral transmission. This study evaluated the antiviral activity of the Thai medicinal herb Clinacanthus siamensis against DENV infection. An ethanolic extract of C. siamensis was evaluated using in vitro DENV infection assays and demonstrated significant antiviral activity, with an IC50 value of 19.32 μg/mL. The inhibitory mechanism of the extract was evaluated across multiple stages of the viral life cycle using time-of-addition experiments, including pre-treatment, co-treatment, and post-infection conditions, in which the virus was exposed to the extract before, during, and after viral entry, respectively. The pre-treatment condition demonstrated the highest level of inhibition, with the extract reducing viral protein levels to 92.79 ± 7.74%, 60.30 ± 9.79%, and 12.52 ± 10.18% at concentrations of 15.625, 31.25, and 62.5 μg/mL, respectively. These findings suggest that the extract exerts its antiviral activity predominantly at an early stage of infection. This observation was further supported by virus-binding assays, which demonstrated that pretreatment of viral particles with the extract impaired their subsequent ability to bind to host cells, suggesting a direct effect on viral particles that may contribute to reduced viral infectivity. In addition, the bioactive constituents of the extract were characterized using LC–MS/MS analysis, which identified two compounds with reported antiviral activity, including isoferulic acid and luteolin, that may contribute to the observed anti-DENV effects. These findings demonstrate that C. siamensis possesses potent anti-DENV activity by reducing viral infectivity at an early stage of infection and highlights the potential of C. siamensis for future development as an affordable therapeutic candidate against DENV​ infection.

1980年至2021年墨西哥四种登革病毒血清型的进化史和遗传多样性

Evolutionary History and Genetic Diversity of the Four Dengue Virus Serotypes in Mexico from 1980 to 2021
作者： Mariana Moran-Rodríguez, José Israel Torres-Avendaño, Marcos Bucio-Pacheco, Edith Hilario Torres-Montoya, Hipólito Castillo-Ureta, Juan José Ríos‐Tostado, Edgar Rosalío Barraza-Soto, Luz Isela Peinado-Guevara, Samuel Campista-León, José Marcial Zazueta-Moreno
摘要中文翻译登革热是世界上最重要的虫媒病毒疾病,由 登革病毒 (DENV) 引起,其中包括四种抗原不同的血清型 (DENV-1–4),进一步分为多个基因型和谱系。墨西哥是一个高流行地区,所有四种血清型的共同循环导致 暴发 疫情反复流行。本研究旨在描述墨西哥 DENV 的进化动态、遗传多样性和地理分布。从 GenBank 检索代表四种血清型的序列,并使用系统发育和群体遗传学方法进行分析,以确定循环基因型和谱系、谱系替换模式和遗传分化。人们发现,随着时间的推移,多个登革热病毒谱系在墨西哥传播,有反复引入、持续存在和谱系更替的证据。分析序列的地理分布表明,东南部和海湾加勒比地区,特别是塔巴斯科州、尤卡坦州、韦拉克鲁斯州和金塔纳罗奥州,在登革热病毒向墨西哥​​中部、太平洋和北部的引入和随后扩散中发挥着重要作用。这些发现突出了墨西哥登革热病毒的动态进化历史,并支持将系统性 基因组监测 与流行病学监测相结合,以改进对循环病毒谱系变化的检测,并加强区域登革热防范和控制策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是世界上最重要的虫媒病毒疾病,由 登革病毒 (DENV) 引起,其中包括四种抗原不同的血清型 (DENV-1–4),进一步分为多个基因型和谱系。墨西哥是一个高流行地区,所有四种血清型的共同循环导致 暴发 疫情反复流行。
设计与对象
本研究旨在描述墨西哥 DENV 的进化动态、遗传多样性和地理分布。
核心方法
从 GenBank 检索代表四种血清型的序列,并使用系统发育和群体遗传学方法进行分析,以确定循环基因型和谱系、谱系替换模式和遗传分化。
主要结果
人们发现,随着时间的推移,多个登革热病毒谱系在墨西哥传播,有反复引入、持续存在和谱系更替的证据。分析序列的地理分布表明,东南部和海湾加勒比地区,特别是塔巴斯科州、尤卡坦州、韦拉克鲁斯州和金塔纳罗奥州,在登革热病毒向墨西哥​​中部、太平洋和北部的引入和随后扩散中发挥着重要作用。
解释与创新
这些发现突出了墨西哥登革热病毒的动态进化历史,并支持将系统性 基因组监测 与流行病学监测相结合,以改进对循环病毒谱系变化的检测,并加强区域登革热防范和控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要持续的 监测 和控制策略来解决墨西哥 DENV 循环的动态性质。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对 GenBank 中可用序列的依赖以及可能丢失数据的限制。然而,由于对多个 DENV 谱系的综合分析,这些发现被认为具有很高的证据强度。

Evolutionary History and Genetic Diversity of the Four Dengue Virus Serotypes in Mexico from 1980 to 2021

Authors: Mariana Moran-Rodríguez, José Israel Torres-Avendaño, Marcos Bucio-Pacheco, Edith Hilario Torres-Montoya, Hipólito Castillo-Ureta, Juan José Ríos‐Tostado, Edgar Rosalío Barraza-Soto, Luz Isela Peinado-Guevara, Samuel Campista-León, José Marcial Zazueta-Moreno
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is the most significant arboviral disease worldwide and is caused by dengue virus (DENV), which comprises four antigenically distinct serotypes (DENV-1–4) further classified into multiple genotypes and lineages. Mexico is a hyperendemic region where the co-circulation of all four serotypes contributes to recurrent epidemic outbreaks. This study aimed to characterize the evolutionary dynamics, genetic diversity, and geographic distribution of DENV in Mexico. Sequences representing the four serotypes were retrieved from GenBank and analyzed using phylogenetic and population genetic approaches to identify circulating genotypes and lineages, lineage replacement patterns, and genetic differentiation. Multiple DENV lineages were found to have circulated in Mexico over time, with evidence of repeated introductions, persistence, and lineage replacement. The geographic distribution of the analyzed sequences suggests an important role for the southeastern and Gulf–Caribbean region, particularly Tabasco, Yucatán, Veracruz, and Quintana Roo, in the introduction and subsequent dispersal of DENV toward central, Pacific, and northern Mexico. These findings highlight the dynamic evolutionary history of DENV in Mexico and support the integration of systematic genomic surveillance with epidemiological monitoring to improve the detection of changes in circulating viral lineages and strengthen regional dengue preparedness and control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is the most significant arboviral disease worldwide and is caused by dengue virus (DENV), which comprises four antigenically distinct serotypes (DENV-1–4) further classified into multiple genotypes and lineages. Mexico is a hyperendemic region where the co-circulation of all four serotypes contributes to recurrent epidemic outbreaks.
Design and population
This study aimed to characterize the evolutionary dynamics, genetic diversity, and geographic distribution of DENV in Mexico.
Core methods
Sequences representing the four serotypes were retrieved from GenBank and analyzed using phylogenetic and population genetic approaches to identify circulating genotypes and lineages, lineage replacement patterns, and genetic differentiation.
Main results
Multiple DENV lineages were found to have circulated in Mexico over time, with evidence of repeated introductions, persistence, and lineage replacement. The geographic distribution of the analyzed sequences suggests an important role for the southeastern and Gulf–Caribbean region, particularly Tabasco, Yucatán, Veracruz, and Quintana Roo, in the introduction and subsequent dispersal of DENV toward central, Pacific, and northern Mexico.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings highlight the dynamic evolutionary history of DENV in Mexico and support the integration of systematic genomic surveillance with epidemiological monitoring to improve the detection of changes in circulating viral lineages and strengthen regional dengue preparedness and control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for ongoing surveillance and control strategies to address the dynamic nature of DENV circulation in Mexico.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on sequences available in GenBank and the potential for missing data. However, the findings are considered high in evidence strength due to the comprehensive analysis of multiple DENV lineages.

输入性 DENV-1 感染的 RT-PCR 检测不一致:诊断挑战和临床意义

Discordant RT-PCR Detection of Imported DENV-1 Infection: Diagnostic Challenges and Clinical Implications
作者： Anna-Lisa Capria, Fabio Filippini, Erika Plicanti, Maria Agnese Manuguerra, Mario Cesaretti, Valerio Corsini, Flavia Favilli, Francesca Fantolini, Paola Mazzetti, Giovanna Antonella Moscato
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:登革病毒 (DENV) 急性期的诊断主要依靠核酸扩增检测,其敏感性可能受到病毒序列变异、标本时间和病毒载量的影响。病例介绍:我们描述了一名 30 岁女性,她从印度尼西亚返回意大利,出现急性发烧、头痛、肌痛、眼眶后疼痛、先兆晕厥和呕吐。DENV 的初步分子和血清学检测结果呈阴性。由于临床怀疑仍然很高,因此第二天使用针对不同基因组区域的商业测定法重复了 RT-PCR。第二次检测呈阳性,而第一次检测仍呈阴性;重复 血清学 后来证明了 DENV IgM 和 IgG 血清转换。独立于序列的扩增和 Illumina 测序产生了近乎完整的 DENV-1 基因组。Nextclade 将序列置于主要为亚洲菌株的谱系 1I_K.3 内。由于阴性商业检测的寡核苷酸序列是专有的,因此无法证明特定的引物/探针不匹配。然而,在序列确认的病毒血症存在的情况下重复测定的不一致支持了分析灵敏度的靶标依赖性损失。结论:这一观察强调需要根据发病时间和流行病学背景来解释阴性 DENV RT-PCR 结果,并且当怀疑持续存在时,需要使用补充或正交诊断方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决急性期 登革病毒 (DENV) 检测的诊断挑战,特别是核酸扩增测试敏感性的变异性。
设计与对象
案例研究是一名从印度尼西亚返回意大利的 30 岁女性,她出现急性发烧和头痛症状。该研究涉及登革病毒的分子和血清学测试。
核心方法
使用针对不同基因组区域的不同商业化验进行两次 RT-PCR。使用独立于序列的扩增和随后的 Illumina 测序来确认 DENV-1 基因组。
主要结果
第一次 RT-PCR 检测呈阴性,但第二次呈阳性。重复 血清学 确认 DENV IgM 和 IgG 血清转换。该序列位于主要亚洲菌株中的谱系 1I_K.3 内。
解释与创新
不一致的结果凸显了需要根据发病时间和流行病学背景来解释阴性 DENV RT-PCR 结果,并在怀疑持续存在时使用补充或正交诊断方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了考虑标本采集时机和使用多种诊断方法以提高 DENV 诊断 准确性的重要性。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括使用专有的商业化验,无法直接比较不匹配情况。由于样本量较小且集中于单个案例,证据强度中等。

Discordant RT-PCR Detection of Imported DENV-1 Infection: Diagnostic Challenges and Clinical Implications

Authors: Anna-Lisa Capria, Fabio Filippini, Erika Plicanti, Maria Agnese Manuguerra, Mario Cesaretti, Valerio Corsini, Flavia Favilli, Francesca Fantolini, Paola Mazzetti, Giovanna Antonella Moscato
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: Dengue virus (DENV) diagnosis during the acute phase relies mainly on nucleic-acid amplification tests, whose sensitivity can be affected by viral sequence variation, specimen timing, and viral load. Case Presentation: We describe a 30-year-old woman who returned to Italy from Indonesia with acute fever, headache, myalgia, retro-orbital pain, presyncope, and vomiting. Initial molecular and serological tests for DENV were negative. Because clinical suspicion remained high, RT-PCR was repeated the following day using a commercial assay targeting a different genomic region. The second assay was positive, whereas the first remained negative; repeat serology later demonstrated DENV IgM and IgG seroconversion. Sequence-independent amplification followed by Illumina sequencing yielded a near-complete DENV-1 genome. Nextclade placed the sequence within lineage 1I_K.3 among predominantly Asian strains. Because the oligonucleotide sequences of the negative commercial assay are proprietary, a specific primer/probe mismatch could not be demonstrated. Nevertheless, repeated assay discordance in the presence of sequence-confirmed viremia supports target-dependent loss of analytical sensitivity. Conclusions: This observation emphasizes the need to interpret negative DENV RT-PCR results in relation to illness timing and epidemiological context and, when suspicion persists, to use complementary or orthogonal diagnostic approaches.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the diagnostic challenges of dengue virus (DENV) detection during the acute phase, particularly the variability in sensitivity of nucleic-acid amplification tests.
Design and population
A 30-year-old woman returning to Italy from Indonesia with symptoms of acute fever and headache was the case study. The study involved molecular and serological tests for DENV.
Core methods
RT-PCR was performed twice using different commercial assays targeting different genomic regions. Sequence-independent amplification followed by Illumina sequencing was used to confirm the DENV-1 genome.
Main results
The first RT-PCR assay was negative, but the second was positive. Repeat serology confirmed DENV IgM and IgG seroconversion. The sequence was placed within lineage 1I_K.3 among predominantly Asian strains.
Interpretation and novelty
The discordant results highlight the need to interpret negative DENV RT-PCR results in relation to illness timing and epidemiological context, and to use complementary or orthogonal diagnostic approaches when suspicion persists.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of considering the timing of specimen collection and the use of multiple diagnostic methods to improve the accuracy of DENV diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the use of proprietary commercial assays, which could not be directly compared for mismatches. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and the focus on a single case.

儿童登革热严重程度的预测:血清皮质醇和脂质谱作为预测因子的前瞻性观察性研究

Serum Cortisol and Lipid Profile as Predictors of Dengue Severity in Children: A Prospective Observational Study
作者： R. Abirami, Subash Sundar, R. Suresh
摘要中文翻译引言:即使有世界卫生组织(WHO)的警告信号,儿童登革热严重程度的早期预测仍然困难。压力激素和脂质代谢的变化与登革热严重程度有关,但前瞻性儿科数据有限。血清皮质醇和脂质谱是常规的实验室参数,可能提供有关疾病严重程度的额外信息。目的:评估血清皮质醇和脂质谱在预测儿童发展为登革热严重程度中的意义。材料与方法:本前瞻性观察性研究在印度泰米尔纳德邦金奈卡坦库拉特的SRM医学院医院和研究中心儿科部门进行,为期两年(2024年7月至2026年7月)。在获得知情同意后,共纳入了90名登革热NS1或IgM ELISA阳性、年龄在0-14岁之间、出现警告信号且在发病第3至第7天就诊的儿童。在入组时测量了血清皮质醇和空腹脂质谱,并记录了采样时的发病天数。根据后续发展为登革热严重程度对儿童进行分类。分类变量采用卡方检验,连续变量采用独立样本t检验;p值<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。结果:在本研究中,90名儿童中有15名(16.67%)发展为登革热严重程度。发展为登革热严重程度的儿童中,男孩占57/90(63.33%),最大年龄组为1-5岁(32/90;35.56%)。液体积聚、黏膜出血、嗜睡和显著肝脏肿大与发展为登革热严重程度显著相关。登革热严重程度还与较高的总白细胞计数和血清尿素、较低的血清钠和碳酸氢盐水平以及延长的凝血酶原时间有关。发展为登革热严重程度的儿童其血清皮质醇水平平均较高(18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL;p值=0.017)。甘油三酯水平也较高(220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL;p值=0.014),而总胆固醇水平较低(99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL;p值=0.003)。极低密度脂蛋白(VLDL)、低密度脂蛋白胆固醇(LDL-C)和高密度脂蛋白(HDL)水平在两组之间没有显著差异。结论:入院时皮质醇和甘油三酯水平高而总胆固醇水平低,与登革热严重程度的发展有强烈关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
即使有世界卫生组织(WHO)的警告信号,儿童登革热严重程度的早期预测仍然困难。血清皮质醇和脂质谱是常规的实验室参数,可能提供有关疾病严重程度的额外信息。
设计与对象
本前瞻性观察性研究在印度泰米尔纳德邦金奈卡坦库拉特的SRM医学院医院和研究中心儿科部门进行,为期两年(2024年7月至2026年7月)。在获得知情同意后,共纳入了90名登革热NS1或IgM ELISA阳性、年龄在0-14岁之间、出现警告信号且在发病第3至第7天就诊的儿童。
核心方法
在入组时测量了血清皮质醇和空腹脂质谱,并记录了采样时的发病天数。分类变量采用卡方检验,连续变量采用独立样本t检验;p值<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。
主要结果
在本研究中,90 名儿童中有 15 名 (16.67%) 进展为 重症登革热。液体积聚、粘膜出血、嗜睡和肝脏明显肿大与 重症登革热 的进展显着相关。重症登革热 还与较高的总白细胞计数和血清尿素、较低的血清钠和碳酸氢盐以及延长的凝血酶原时间相关。进展为 重症登革热 的儿童的平均血清皮质醇高于未进展为 重症登革热 的儿童(18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL;p 值=0.017)。平均甘油三酯水平也较高(220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL;p 值=0.014),而平均总胆固醇水平较低(99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL;p 值=0.003)。
解释与创新
入院时皮质醇和甘油三酯水平高而总胆固醇水平低,与登革热严重程度的发展有强烈关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为非侵入性生物标志物的开发提供信息,以早期识别有 重症登革热 风险的儿童,从而有助于有针对性的临床管理和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于使用了统计检验和定义的 队列研究,证据强度适中。

Serum Cortisol and Lipid Profile as Predictors of Dengue Severity in Children: A Prospective Observational Study

Authors: R. Abirami, Subash Sundar, R. Suresh
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Early prediction of severe dengue in children continues to be difficult, even with World Health Organisation (WHO) warning signs. Alterations in stress hormones and lipid metabolism have been associated with dengue severity; however, prospective paediatric data are limited. Serum cortisol and lipid profile are routinely available laboratory parameters and may provide additional information regarding disease severity. Aim: To evaluate the significance of serum cortisol and lipid profile in forecasting the advancement to severe dengue in children. Materials and Methods: This prospective observational study was conducted in the Department of Paediatrics, SRM Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Kattankulathur, Chennai, Tamil Nadu, India, over two years (July 2024-July 2026). A total of 90 dengue NS1 or IgM ELISA-positive children aged between 0-14 years with warning signs and presenting between days 3 and 7 of illness were enrolled after informed consent. Serum cortisol and fasting lipid profile were measured at enrolment, and the day of illness at sampling was recorded. Children were classified according to subsequent progression to severe dengue. Categorical variables were compared using the Chisquare test and continuous variables using the independentsamples t-test; p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant. Results: In the present study, 15 out of 90 (16.67%) children progressed to severe dengue. Boys constituted 57/90 (63.33%) of the cohort, and the largest age group was 1-5 years (32/90; 35.56%). Fluid accumulation, mucosal bleeding, lethargy, and significant liver enlargement were significantly associated with progression to severe dengue. Severe dengue was also associated with higher total leucocyte count and serum urea, lower serum sodium and bicarbonate, and prolonged prothrombin time. Mean serum cortisol was higher in children who progressed to severe dengue than in those who did not (18.74±6.14 vs 15.46±4.48 μg/dL; p-value =0.017). Mean triglyceride levels were also higher (220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL; p-value =0.014), whereas mean total cholesterol levels were lower (99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL; p-value =0.003). Very Low-Density Lipoprotein (VLDL), Low-Density Lipoprotein Cholesterol (LDL-C), and High-Density Lipoprotein (HDL) levels did not differ significantly between groups. Conclusion: High levels of cortisol and triglycerides with low levels of total cholesterol at the time of admission are strongly linked to the development of severe dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Early prediction of severe dengue in children continues to be difficult, even with World Health Organisation (WHO) warning signs. Serum cortisol and lipid profile are routinely available laboratory parameters and may provide additional information regarding disease severity.
Design and population
This prospective observational study was conducted in the Department of Paediatrics, SRM Medical College Hospital and Research Centre, Kattankulathur, Chennai, Tamil Nadu, India, over two years (July 2024-July 2026). A total of 90 dengue NS1 or IgM ELISA-positive children aged between 0-14 years with warning signs and presenting between days 3 and 7 of illness were enrolled after informed consent.
Core methods
Serum cortisol and fasting lipid profile were measured at enrolment, and the day of illness at sampling was recorded. Categorical variables were compared using the Chisquare test and continuous variables using the independent-samples t-test; p-value <0.05 was considered statistically significant.
Main results
In the present study, 15 out of 90 (16.67%) children progressed to severe dengue. Fluid accumulation, mucosal bleeding, lethargy, and significant liver enlargement were significantly associated with progression to severe dengue. Severe dengue was also associated with higher total leucocyte count and serum urea, lower serum sodium and bicarbonate, and prolonged prothrombin time. Mean triglyceride levels were also higher (220.07±141.71 vs 162.97±62.69 mg/dL; p-value =0.014), whereas mean total cholesterol levels were lower (99.67±23.70 vs 122.01±26.71 mg/dL; p-value =0.003).
Interpretation and novelty
High levels of cortisol and triglycerides with low levels of total cholesterol at the time of admission are strongly linked to the development of severe dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of non-invasive biomarkers for early identification of children at risk of severe dengue, aiding in targeted clinical management and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of statistical tests and a defined cohort.
