2023年1月至2024年12月期间RSUP Prof. Dr. R. D. Kandou医院儿童登革热出血病例患者特征分析

Profil Pasien Anak Dengan Demam Berdarah Dengue di RSUP Prof. Dr. R. D. Kandou Periode Januari 2023–Desember 2024
作者： Kayla N. Terrene, Novie Homenta Rampengan, Praevilia Margareth Salendu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间关藤教授综合医院登革出血热 (DHF) 儿科患者的临床和实验室概况。
设计与对象
这是一项回顾性和描述性研究,使用了 2023 年至 2024 年患者的病历。大多数患者来自万鸦老市,营养状况良好。
核心方法
本研究使用患者病历作为数据源。这是一项回顾性和描述性研究设计。
主要结果
结果显示,大多数病例发生在2024年,最大的年龄组为6-12岁,且以男性为主。实验室检查结果显示白细胞减少(45%的患者)、血细胞比容增加(53.9%)和严重的血小板减少(66.3%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,儿童登革出血热病例最常见于学龄儿童,最常见的实验室检查结果是严重的血小板减少,继发感染的病例较多。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在儿童登革出血热关键阶段早期发现和最佳管理的重要性,特别是在学龄群体中。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于医疗记录,这可能会引入偏差或缺失数据。尽管回收率很高,但 2.9% 的 死亡率 比率凸显了进一步研究和改进管理策略的必要性。

Profil Pasien Anak Dengan Demam Berdarah Dengue di RSUP Prof. Dr. R. D. Kandou Periode Januari 2023–Desember 2024

Authors: Kayla N. Terrene, Novie Homenta Rampengan, Praevilia Margareth Salendu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract: Dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) is a major health problem among children in Indonesia due to its high incidence and mortality rates. This study aimed to describe the clinical and laboratory profiles of pediatric patients with DHF at Prof. Kandou General Hospital from January 2023 to December 2024. This was a retrospective and descriptive study using patient medical records from 2023 to 2024. The results showed that most cases occurred in 2024, with the largest age group being 6–12 years old and predominantly male. The majority of patients were from Manado City and in good nutritional status. Common clinical manifestations were fever, abdominal pain, fluid accumulation, and shock. Laboratory findings showed leukopenia (45% of patients), increased hematocrit (53.9%), and severe thrombocytopenia (66.3%). Grade 3 DHF was the most common category (64.1%). Most patients were declared recovered, but mortality still reached 2.9%. In conclusion, dengue hemorrhagic fever cases in children most commonly occur in school-aged children. Manifestations of fever, gastrointestinal symptoms, plasma leakage, and shock dominate the clinical findings. Severe thrombocytopenia is the most common laboratory finding, and there are more cases of secondary infection. Despite the high recovery rate, the mortality rate that is still found emphasizes the urgency of early detection and optimal management during the critical phase. Keywords: dengue hemorrhagic fever; children; patient profile Abstrak: Demam berdarah dengue (DBD) merupakan masalah kesehatan utama pada anak di Indonesia karena tingginya angka kejadian dan mortalitas. Penelitian ini bertujuan menggambarkan profil klinis dan laboratorium pasien anak dengan DBD di RSUP Prof. Kandou periode Januari 2023–Desember 2024. Jenis penelitian ialah deskriptif retrospektif menggunakan data sekunder rekam medik pasien tahun 2023–2024. Hasil penelitian mendapatkan sebagian besar kasus terjadi pada tahun 2024, dengan kelompok usia terbanyak 6–12 tahun dan dominasi jenis kelamin laki-laki. Mayoritas pasien berasal dari Kota Manado dan memiliki status gizi baik. Manifestasi klinis yang umum dijumpai ialah demam, nyeri perut, akumulasi cairan, serta syok. Secara laboratorium, leukopenia ditemukan pada 45%, peningkatan hematokrit pada 53,9%, dan trombositopenia berat pada 66,3% pasien. Kategori terbanyak ialah DBD derajat 3 merupakan kategori terbanyak (64,1%). Sebagian besar pasien dinyatakan sembuh, namun mortalitas masih mencapai 2,9%. Simpulan penelitian ini kasus DBD pada anak paling banyak terjadi pada anak usia sekolah. Manifestasi demam, gastrointestinal, kebocoran plasma, dan syok mendominasi temuan klinis. Trombositopenia berat merupakan temuan laboratorium tersering dan lebih banyak kasus infeksi sekunder. Meskipun kesembuhan tinggi, mortalitas yang masih ditemukan menegaskan urgensi deteksi dini dan penanganan optimal pada fase kritis. Kata kunci: demam berdarah dengue; anak; profil pasien
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to describe the clinical and laboratory profiles of pediatric patients with dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) at Prof. Kandou General Hospital during the period January 2023–December 2024.
Design and population
This was a retrospective and descriptive study using patient medical records from 2023 to 2024. The majority of patients were from Manado City and in good nutritional status.
Core methods
This study used patient medical records as data sources. It was a retrospective and descriptive study design.
Main results
The results showed that most cases occurred in 2024, with the largest age group being 6–12 years old and predominantly male. Laboratory findings showed leukopenia (45% of patients), increased hematocrit (53.9%), and severe thrombocytopenia (66.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that dengue hemorrhagic fever cases in children most commonly occur in school-aged children, and severe thrombocytopenia is the most common laboratory finding, with more cases of secondary infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of early detection and optimal management during the critical phase of dengue hemorrhagic fever in children, particularly in school-aged groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on medical records, which may introduce bias or missing data. Despite the high recovery rate, the mortality rate of 2.9% highlights the need for further research and improved management strategies.

将社区参与与加权登革热风险分层框架相结合,以实现越南巴地头顿省社区层面的优先排序

Integrating community participation with a weighted dengue risk-stratification framework for commune-level prioritization in Ba Ria-Vung Tau Province, Vietnam
作者： Dang Anh Tuan, Đặng Anh Tuấn
摘要中文翻译当流行病学、昆虫​​学和操作信息必须结合起来时,登革热预防计划需要透明的社区层面的优先顺序。我们在越南巴地头顿省的 72 个公社、行政区和城镇实施了利益相关者知情的加权登革热风险分层框架。社区参与行动研究 (CPAR) 提供了参与式实施方法,而定量程序是在 MS Access 中实施的确定性加性评分。2022 年 1 月的分数基于 2021 年可用的信息,根据观察到的 2022 年 发病率 进行临时评估。经过源数据质量控制并应用前五年平均值作为历史基线后,2022 年 1 月的分类包括 12 个高优先级单位和 60 个中优先级单位;2023 年 1 月的分类包括 34 个中优先级单位和 38 个低优先级单位。2022 年分数与 2022 年绝对 发病率 呈负相关(Spearman rho = -0.304,p = 0.009),对上四分位数绝对 发病率 的辨别力较差(AUC = 0.335)。高优先级单位相对于 2016-2020 年平均值的相对增幅高于中优先级单位(中位数 5.36 倍与 2.23 倍;p = 0.001),上四分位相对反弹的探索性 AUC 为 0.711。分类对有序输入中的截止值和一级不确定性很敏感。该框架应被解释为透明的本地优先级工具,而不是概率预测模型。在更广泛使用之前,需要进行前瞻性的多年和外部验证、评估者可靠性评估和针对登革热的重新校准。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
当流行病学、昆虫​​学和操作信息必须结合起来时,登革热预防计划需要透明的社区层面的优先顺序。
设计与对象
我们在越南巴地头顿省的 72 个公社、行政区和城镇实施了利益相关者知情的加权登革热风险分层框架。
核心方法
社区参与行动研究 (CPAR) 提供了参与式实施方法,而定量程序是在 MS Access 中实施的确定性加性评分。
主要结果
2022 年分数与 2022 年绝对 发病率 呈负相关(Spearman rho = -0.304,p = 0.009),对上四分位数绝对 发病率 的辨别力较差(AUC = 0.335)。
解释与创新
高优先级单位相对于 2016-2020 年平均值的相对增幅高于中优先级单位(中位数 5.36 倍与 2.23 倍;p = 0.001),上四分位相对反弹的探索性 AUC 为 0.711。
科研与公卫意义
该框架应被解释为透明的本地优先级工具,而不是概率预测模型。
局限与证据强度
分类对有序输入中的截止值和一级不确定性很敏感。在更广泛使用之前,需要进行前瞻性的多年和外部验证、评估者可靠性评估和针对登革热的重新校准。

Integrating community participation with a weighted dengue risk-stratification framework for commune-level prioritization in Ba Ria-Vung Tau Province, Vietnam

Authors: Dang Anh Tuan, Đặng Anh Tuấn
Original abstract (English)
Dengue prevention programs require transparent commune-level prioritization when epidemiological, entomological, and operational information must be combined. We implemented a stakeholder-informed weighted dengue risk-stratification framework in 72 communes, wards, and towns in Ba Ria-Vung Tau Province, Vietnam. Community participatory action research (CPAR) provided the participatory implementation approach, whereas the quantitative procedure was a deterministic additive score implemented in MS Access. The January 2022 score, based on information available through 2021, was temporally evaluated against observed 2022 incidence. After source-data quality control and application of the preceding five-year mean as the historical baseline, the January 2022 classification comprised 12 high-priority and 60 intermediate-priority units; the January 2023 classification comprised 34 intermediate-priority and 38 low-priority units. The 2022 score was inversely associated with absolute 2022 incidence (Spearman rho = -0.304, p = 0.009), with poor discrimination for upper-quartile absolute incidence (AUC = 0.335). High-priority units showed a larger relative increase from their 2016–2020 mean than intermediate-priority units (median 5.36-fold vs. 2.23-fold; p = 0.001), with an exploratory AUC of 0.711 for upper-quartile relative rebound. Classifications were sensitive to cut-offs and one-level uncertainty in ordinal inputs. The framework should be interpreted as a transparent local prioritization tool rather than a probabilistic prediction model. Prospective multi-year and external validation, assessor-reliability evaluation, and dengue-specific recalibration are required before broader use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue prevention programs require transparent commune-level prioritization when epidemiological, entomological, and operational information must be combined.
Design and population
We implemented a stakeholder-informed weighted dengue risk-stratification framework in 72 communes, wards, and towns in Ba Ria-Vung Tau Province, Vietnam.
Core methods
Community participatory action research (CPAR) provided the participatory implementation approach, whereas the quantitative procedure was a deterministic additive score implemented in MS Access.
Main results
The 2022 score was inversely associated with absolute 2022 incidence (Spearman rho = -0.304, p = 0.009), with poor discrimination for upper-quartile absolute incidence (AUC = 0.335).
Interpretation and novelty
High-priority units showed a larger relative increase from their 2016–2020 mean than intermediate-priority units (median 5.36-fold vs. 2.23-fold; p = 0.001), with an exploratory AUC of 0.711 for upper-quartile relative rebound.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework should be interpreted as a transparent local prioritization tool rather than a probabilistic prediction model.
Limitations and evidence strength
Classifications were sensitive to cut-offs and one-level uncertainty in ordinal inputs. Prospective multi-year and external validation, assessor-reliability evaluation, and dengue-specific recalibration are required before broader use.

重症登革热 发热恢复期自发性脾破裂:紧急脾切除术的围手术期麻醉管理

Spontaneous Splenic Rupture During the Recovery Phase of Severe Dengue Fever: Perioperative Anaesthetic Management of an Emergency Splenectomy
作者： Janson Darrel, P. Panduwawalla, C. N. Widanapathirana, P. Abiharan, J. Senavirathna, Darrel Janson
摘要中文翻译自发性脾破裂是 登革热 一种罕见但危及生命的并发症,最常见于关键期。我们描述了一名 35 岁的先前健康的 重症登革热 男性在恢复阶段发生的一个不寻常的病例,他最初随着血小板计数的上升而有所改善,然后出现突然的左上腹疼痛、低血压、心动过速和贫血。增强计算机断层扫描证实脾破裂伴腹腔积血,并在全身麻醉下进行了紧急脾切除术。旋转血栓弹力图引导输血支持靶向血液制品治疗。组织病理学证实脾破裂并伴有广泛的实质内出血和包膜破裂。患者康复良好,病情稳定已出院。该病例强调了即使在 登革热 的恢复阶段也需要考虑脾破裂,以及早期 诊断、及时手术和细致的围手术期管理的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自发性脾破裂是 登革热 一种罕见但危及生命的并发症,最常见于关键期。这个案例突显了恢复阶段发生的不寻常现象。
设计与对象
这篇 病例报告 描述了对一名 35 岁、先前健康、患有 重症登革热 发烧的男性进行的紧急脾切除术,该男性在恢复阶段出现自发性脾破裂。
核心方法
对比增强计算机断层扫描用于确认脾破裂伴腹腔积血,旋转血栓弹力图引导输血支持围手术期管理期间的靶向血液制品治疗。
主要结果
患者突然出现左上腹疼痛、低血压、心动过速和贫血,这些症状与脾破裂相符。组织病理学证实存在广泛的实质内出血和包膜破裂。
解释与创新
作者将此病例解释为 登革热 恢复期发生的自发性脾破裂的一个例子,这在文献中并不常见。
科研与公卫意义
该病例为了解 登革热 恢复期脾破裂的可能性提供了重要见解,强调了临床管理中保持警惕的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是单一的病例报告,这限制了其普遍性。然而,这些发现得到了直接临床和组织病理学证据的支持,提供了中等程度的可信度。

Spontaneous Splenic Rupture During the Recovery Phase of Severe Dengue Fever: Perioperative Anaesthetic Management of an Emergency Splenectomy

Authors: Janson Darrel, P. Panduwawalla, C. N. Widanapathirana, P. Abiharan, J. Senavirathna, Darrel Janson
Original abstract (English)
Spontaneous splenic rupture is a rare but life-threatening complication of dengue fever and is most commonly reported during the critical phase. We describe an unusual case occurring during the recovery phase in a 35-year-old previously healthy man with severe dengue who initially improved with rising platelet counts before developing sudden left upper quadrant pain, hypotension, tachycardia, and anaemia. Contrast-enhanced computed tomography confirmed splenic rupture with haemoperitoneum, and emergency splenectomy was performed under general anaesthesia. Rotational thromboelastometry-guided transfusion supported targeted blood product therapy. Histopathology confirmed splenic rupture with extensive intraparenchymal haemorrhage and capsular disruption. The patient recovered well and was discharged in a stable condition. This case highlights the need to consider splenic rupture even during the recovery phase of dengue fever and the importance of early diagnosis, prompt surgery, and meticulous perioperative management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Spontaneous splenic rupture is a rare but life-threatening complication of dengue fever, most commonly reported during the critical phase. This case highlights an unusual occurrence during the recovery phase.
Design and population
This case report describes an emergency splenectomy performed on a 35-year-old previously healthy man with severe dengue fever who developed spontaneous splenic rupture during the recovery phase.
Core methods
Contrast-enhanced computed tomography was used to confirm splenic rupture with haemoperitoneum, and rotational thromboelastometry-guided transfusion supported targeted blood product therapy during the perioperative management.
Main results
The patient developed sudden left upper quadrant pain, hypotension, tachycardia, and anaemia, which were consistent with splenic rupture. Histopathology confirmed extensive intraparenchymal haemorrhage and capsular disruption.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this case as an example of spontaneous splenic rupture occurring during the recovery phase of dengue fever, which is not typically reported in the literature.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case provides important insights into the potential for splenic rupture during the recovery phase of dengue fever, emphasizing the need for vigilance in clinical management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a single case report, which limits its generalizability. However, the findings are supported by direct clinical and histopathological evidence, providing a moderate level of confidence.

具有空间定义的咬合区域的标准化蚊对小鼠 传播 测定

A standardized mosquito-to-mouse transmission assay with spatially defined bite areas
作者： Elliott F. Miot, Hacène Medkour, Lauryne Pruvost, Louise Belleville, Mihra Tavadia, Félix Rey-Cadilhac, Carole Ginibre, Bethsabée Scheid, Pascal Boutinaud, Julien Pompon
摘要中文翻译蚊媒病毒引发皮肤感染,病毒颗粒、宿主 细胞和蚊子唾液之间的相互作用可以影响早期感染和随后的病毒传播。调查这些事件需要将蚊子暴露与精确的接种部位联系起来的方法。然而,在随后的组织收集过程中识别单个叮咬部位仍然是蚊子到小鼠 传播 模型中的一个实际挑战。在这里,我们描述了一种标准化的蚊子到小鼠 传播 测定,该测定有助于蚊子叮咬部位的定位和目标组织采样。该方法结合了定制的 3D 打印喂食装置、个体蚊子暴露和穿孔胶带来定义叮咬区域并标记已识别的叮咬部位以供后续收集。我们分别使用西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) 和 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染的埃及伊蚊与 C57BL/6J 小鼠和 A129 Ifnar1-/- 小鼠演示了该协议的应用。所有受感染的蚊子在接触后 20 分钟内都成功吸血。在 3 次被感染的蚊虫叮咬后,小鼠从叮咬后大约第 6 天开始出现临床症状,对于 WNV,死亡率 出现在第 8 天到第 10 天之间,而对于 DENV,疾病发作发生在叮咬后 4 天,大多数小鼠在第 10 天康复。为了研究皮肤中的早期 WNV 病毒动力学,对在标记咬伤部位和引流腹股沟淋巴结收集的活检中的 WNV 基因组 RNA 进行了定量。早在被咬后 1 小时,两种组织中就检测到病毒 RNA,并且在被咬后 24 小时,RNA 水平显着增加。该协议提供了一种实用方法,用于识别蚊子叮咬部位并调查 媒介 介导的 传播 后的早期组织水平事件。通过适当的验证,该协议可以适应其他 媒介 病原体 宿主 系统和下游分析。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种方法来研究 媒介 介导的 登革病毒 的 传播 后的早期组织水平事件,因为蚊媒病毒引发皮肤感染,病毒颗粒、宿主 细胞和蚊子唾液之间的相互作用可以影响早期感染和随后的病毒传播。
设计与对象
该研究描述了使用感染 登革病毒 (DENV) 的埃及伊蚊和 C57BL/6J 小鼠进行的标准化蚊对小鼠 传播 测定,并在单独的实验中使用 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 演示了该方案。
核心方法
该方法结合了定制的 3D 打印喂食装置、个体蚊子暴露和穿孔胶带来定义叮咬区域并标记已识别的叮咬部位以供后续收集。
主要结果
早在被咬后 1 小时,两种组织中就检测到病毒 RNA,并且在被咬后 24 小时,RNA 水平显着增加。
解释与创新
该协议提供了一种实用方法,用于识别蚊子叮咬部位并调查 媒介 介导的 登革病毒 的 传播 后的早期组织水平事件。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为研究 登革病毒 传播 动力学和早期感染过程提供了方法学上的进步,可以为未来登革热 发病机制 和干预策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究的局限性或证据强度。

A standardized mosquito-to-mouse transmission assay with spatially defined bite areas

Authors: Elliott F. Miot, Hacène Medkour, Lauryne Pruvost, Louise Belleville, Mihra Tavadia, Félix Rey-Cadilhac, Carole Ginibre, Bethsabée Scheid, Pascal Boutinaud, Julien Pompon
Original abstract (English)
Mosquito-borne viruses initiate infection in the skin, where interactions between viral particles, host cells, and mosquito saliva can influence early infection and subsequent viral dissemination. Investigating these events requires methods that link mosquito exposure to the precise site of inoculation. However, identifying individual bite sites during subsequent tissue collection remains a practical challenge in mosquito-to-mouse transmission models. Here, we describe a standardized mosquito-to-mouse transmission assay that facilitates the localization of mosquito bite sites and targeted tissue sampling. The method combines a custom 3D-printed feeding device, individual mosquito exposure, and perforated adhesive tape to define the biting area and mark identified bite sites for subsequent collection. We demonstrate the application of this protocol using West Nile virus (WNV) and Dengue virus (DENV)-infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes with C57BL/6J mice and A129 Ifnar1-/- mice, respectively. All infected mosquitoes successfully blood-fed within 20 min of exposure. After three infected-mosquito bites, mice developed clinical signs from approximately day 6 post-bite, with mortality occurring between days 8 and 10 for WNV, while disease onset occurred at 4 days post bite and most of the mice recovered by day 10 for DENV. To investigate early WNV viral kinetics in skin, WNV genomic RNA was quantified in biopsies collected at marked bite sites and the draining inguinal lymph nodes. Viral RNA was detected in both tissues as early as 1 h post-bite, and RNA levels increased significantly at 24 h post-bite. This protocol provides a practical approach for identifying mosquito bite sites and investigating early tissue-level events following vector-mediated transmission. With appropriate validation, the protocol can be adapted to other vector pathogen host systems and downstream analyses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a method for investigating early tissue-level events following vector-mediated transmission of dengue virus, as mosquito-borne viruses initiate infection in the skin where interactions between viral particles, host cells, and mosquito saliva can influence early infection and subsequent viral dissemination.
Design and population
The study describes a standardized mosquito-to-mouse transmission assay using Aedes aegypti mosquitoes infected with Dengue virus (DENV) and C57BL/6J mice, with the protocol demonstrated using DENV-1 and DENV-2 in separate experiments.
Core methods
The method combines a custom 3D-printed feeding device, individual mosquito exposure, and perforated adhesive tape to define the biting area and mark identified bite sites for subsequent collection.
Main results
Viral RNA was detected in both tissues as early as 1 h post-bite, and RNA levels increased significantly at 24 h post-bite.
Interpretation and novelty
This protocol provides a practical approach for identifying mosquito bite sites and investigating early tissue-level events following vector-mediated transmission of dengue virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a methodological advancement for studying dengue virus transmission dynamics and early infection processes, which can inform future research on dengue pathogenesis and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.

利益相关者对人工智能驱动的登革热 监测 和报告系统的数字化准备情况

Stakeholder digital readiness for AI-driven dengue surveillance and reporting system
作者： Katlego Tefo Mabote, Da-Vaugh Sanderson
摘要中文翻译背景随着公共卫生部门迈向数字健康转型,人工智能为改善疾病 监测、早期疾病检测和加强报告系统提供了机会。然而,这些技术的采用取决于利益相关者的知识、准备情况和实践。因此,本研究评估了牙买加金斯敦的利益相关者对登革热 监测 的认识、预防实践、数字访问和准备情况。方法 该研究采用了横断面定量混合模式调查。该调查问卷由多项选择题和开放式问题组成,共有 397 名参与者和 391 名符合条件的数据注册。参与者是通过在线渠道和在金斯顿的医疗机构进行的现场纸质调查问卷招募的。定量数据在 SPSS 30 版上使用 Pearson 卡方检验进行描述性分析,p 值≤ 0.05,同时对定性成分进行主题分析。结果显示,72.1% 的参与者表示准备程度较高,10.2% 的参与者表示对数字化准备程度中等,17.6% 的参与者表示准备程度较低。96.4%的研究参与者表示愿意采用人工智能驱动的数字健康工具。然而,只有 31.1% 的回答问题的参与者表示高度自信,而 50.4% 的研究受访者对使用数字健康工具表示中性信心。最想要的人工智能功能是患者参与聊天机器人,占 86.3%。数字化准备程度与教育水平的提高、在医疗保健中的主要作用、年龄组、居住地和之前使用数字健康工具显着相关。经常报道的担忧是信息隐私和对系统的信任。结论 研究参与者表现出强大的数字基础设施准备度和对人工智能驱动的电子医疗解决方案的强烈采用意愿。尽管如此,利益相关者仍对数据隐私和对系统的信任表示担忧。这项研究为 媒介 传播疾病 监测 文献做出了贡献,同时证明了在公共卫生技术设计和采用中整合基于社区的知识和技术准备的重要性。这项研究可能有助于共同设计和开发与金士顿相关的人工智能数字登革热 监测 电子健康系统。未来的研究应侧重于对所有利益相关者的教育和培训,以提高他们驾驭数字健康领域的能力。此外,还可以探索生成式人工智能在数字健康领域的伦理使用。

问题与背景
随着公共卫生部门迈向数字健康转型,人工智能提供了改善疾病 监测、早期疾病检测和加强报告系统的机会。然而,这些技术的采用取决于利益相关者的知识、准备情况和实践。
设计与对象
该研究采用了横断面定量混合模式调查。该调查问卷由多项选择题和开放式问题组成,共有 397 名参与者和 391 名符合条件的数据注册。参与者是通过在线渠道和在金斯顿的医疗机构进行的现场纸质调查问卷招募的。
核心方法
定量数据在 SPSS 30 版上使用 Pearson 卡方检验进行描述性分析,p 值≤ 0.05,同时对定性成分进行主题分析。
主要结果
结果显示,72.1% 的参与者表示准备程度较高,10.2% 的参与者表示对数字化准备程度中等,17.6% 的参与者表示准备程度较低。经常报道的担忧是信息隐私和对系统的信任。
解释与创新
研究参与者表现出强大的数字基础设施准备度和对人工智能驱动的电子医疗解决方案的强烈采用意愿。最想要的人工智能功能是患者参与聊天机器人,占 86.3%。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究为 媒介 传播疾病 监测 文献做出了贡献,同时证明了在公共卫生技术设计和采用中整合基于社区的知识和技术准备的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究设计的具体局限性或证据强度评估。

Stakeholder digital readiness for AI-driven dengue surveillance and reporting system

Authors: Katlego Tefo Mabote, Da-Vaugh Sanderson
Original abstract (English)
Background As the public health sector moves towards digital health transformation, artificial intelligence offers opportunities to improve disease surveillance, early disease detection and strengthen reporting systems. However, the adoption of these technologies depends on stakeholder knowledge, readiness, and practices. Therefore, this study assesses stakeholders’ awareness, preventative practices, digital access, and readiness for dengue surveillance in Kingston, Jamaica. Methods The study employed a cross-sectional quantitative mixed-mode survey. The questionnaire comprised of multiple choice and open-ended questions with 397 participants and 391 eligible data registrations. Participants were recruited through online channels and in-person paper-based questionnaires administered at healthcare facilities across Kingston. Quantitative data was analyzed descriptively on SPSS Version 30 with Pearson Chi-square tests and a p -value of ≤ 0.05, while qualitative components were thematically analyzed. Findings Results reveal that 72.1% of the participants reported having high readiness, 10.2% reported moderate digital readiness and 17.6% reported low readiness. 96.4% of the study participants expressed willingness to adopt AI-driven digital health tools. Yet only 31.1% of the participants who answered the question indicated high confidence, while 50.4% of the study respondents indicated neutral confidence in using digital health tools. The most desired AI feature is patient engagement chatbots at 86.3%. Digital readiness was significantly correlated to elevated level of education, primary role in healthcare, age group, residence and prior use of digital health tools. The frequently reported concerns were privacy of information and trust in the system. Conclusion Study participants exhibited strong digital infrastructure readiness and substantial adoption willingness to AI-driven eHealth solutions. Nonetheless, the stakeholders expressed concerns about data privacy and trust in the systems. This study contributes to vector-borne disease surveillance literature while demonstrating the importance of integrating community-based knowledge and technology readiness in public health technology design and adoption. This study may assist in the co-design and development of an AI-enabled digital dengue surveillance eHealth system relevant to the Kingston context. Future research should focus on education and training of all stakeholders to improve their capability in navigating the digital health landscape. Additionally, the ethical use of generative AI in digital health can be explored.

Design and population
The study employed a cross-sectional quantitative mixed-mode survey. The questionnaire comprised of multiple choice and open-ended questions with 397 participants and 391 eligible data registrations. Participants were recruited through online channels and in-person paper-based questionnaires administered at healthcare facilities across Kingston.
Core methods
Quantitative data was analyzed descriptively on SPSS Version 30 with Pearson Chi-square tests and a p -value of ≤ 0.05, while qualitative components were thematically analyzed.
Main results
Results reveal that 72.1% of the participants reported having high readiness, 10.2% reported moderate digital readiness and 17.6% reported low readiness. The frequently reported concerns were privacy of information and trust in the system.

The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

妊娠期疟疾和登革热合并感染导致严重血小板减少症:病例报告 强调由于临床特征重叠而导致的诊断和管理挑战

Malaria and Dengue Co-infection in Pregnancy Causing Severe Thrombocytopenia: A Case Report Highlighting Diagnostic and Management Challenges Due to Overlapping Clinical Features
作者： Daniel Odongo, David Alfred Younge, Goberdhan Nirvani, Goolcharan Dyal, Nirvanie Singh, Hardeo Ramdeholl, Nirvani Goberdhan
摘要中文翻译背景:疟疾和登革热是蚊媒感染,在怀孕期间构成重大健康风险,并可能对孕产妇和胎儿结局产生不利影响。它们重叠的临床表现可能会使 诊断 复杂化并延迟适当的治疗,特别是当两种感染同时发生时。目的:本 病例报告 旨在强调妊娠期疟疾和登革热合并感染的罕见性,以及广泛有针对性的检测和促进孕产妇优化以尽量减少不良产科结果的重要性。病例介绍:一名 22 岁的初产妇,在妊娠 37 周零 4 天时出现五天头痛、发烧、呕吐和腹泻症状后被转诊。检测发现间日疟原虫疟疾,她的初始血小板计数为18,000/μL。在接收医院,登革热特异性I​​gM和IgG抗体呈阳性,血小板计数为40,000/μL,超声显示羊水过少,羊水指数为4.14 cm。她被送入高度依赖科室进行密切监测、临床稳定和分娩计划。治疗包括静脉注射晶体液、八单位血小板、一单位新鲜冰冻血浆和氯喹。第三天血小板计数改善至 60,000/μL 后,使用米索前列醇和经宫颈弗利球囊引产,并用催产素增强。她阴道生下一名活的男婴,重 2,860 克,没有报告直接孕产妇或新生儿并发症。估计失血量为 225 mL。入院第七天,血小板计数已升至128,000/μL,疟疾涂片呈阴性。第十二天,她出院了。结论:被诊断患有任一感染且表现出严重症状的孕妇应广泛调查是否存在合并感染,并应立即启动 治疗 以优化母婴结局。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疟疾和登革热是蚊媒感染,在怀孕期间构成重大健康风险,并可能对孕产妇和胎儿结局产生不利影响。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一名 22 岁的初产妇,在妊娠 37 周零 4 天时出现头痛、发烧、呕吐和腹泻,并被诊断患有疟疾和登革热合并感染。
核心方法
测试发现间日疟原虫疟疾,登革热特异性 IgM 和 IgG 抗体呈阳性。治疗包括静脉注射晶体液、血小板输注、新鲜冰冻血浆和氯喹。
主要结果
接收医院血小板计数为40,000/μL,超声显示羊水过少,羊水指数为4.14 cm。第三天血小板计数提高到60,000/μL,第七天提高到128,000/μL。第十二天,她出院了。
解释与创新
作者解释说,被诊断患有任一感染且表现出严重症状的孕妇应广泛调查是否存在合并感染,并应立即启动 治疗 以优化母婴结局。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了广泛有针对性的检测的重要性,并提示 治疗 针对孕妇合并感染,以尽量减少不良产科结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究是 病例报告,限制了其普遍性,但由于详细的临床表现和管理,证据有力。

Malaria and Dengue Co-infection in Pregnancy Causing Severe Thrombocytopenia: A Case Report Highlighting Diagnostic and Management Challenges Due to Overlapping Clinical Features

Authors: Daniel Odongo, David Alfred Younge, Goberdhan Nirvani, Goolcharan Dyal, Nirvanie Singh, Hardeo Ramdeholl, Nirvani Goberdhan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Malaria and dengue are mosquito-borne infections that pose significant health risks during pregnancy and may adversely affect maternal and fetal outcomes. Their overlapping clinical manifestations can complicate diagnosis and delay appropriate management, particularly when both infections occur concurrently. Aim: This case report aims to highlight the rarity of malaria and dengue coinfection in pregnancy and the importance of extensive targeted testing and prompt maternal optimisation to minimise adverse obstetric outcomes. Case Presentation: A 22-year-old primigravida at 37 weeks and 4 days of gestation was referred after presenting with five days of headache, fever, vomiting and diarrhoea. Testing identified Plasmodium vivax malaria, and her initial platelet count was 18,000/μL. At the receiving hospital, dengue-specific IgM and IgG antibodies were positive, the platelet count was 40,000/μL, and ultrasound showed oligohydramnios with an amniotic fluid index of 4.14 cm. She was admitted to a high-dependency unit for close monitoring, clinical stabilisation and delivery planning. Management included intravenous crystalloid fluids, eight units of platelets, one unit of fresh frozen plasma and chloroquine. Following improvement in the platelet count to 60,000/μL on the third day, labour was induced using misoprostol and a transcervical Foley balloon and augmented with oxytocin. She delivered a live male infant vaginally, weighing 2,860 g, without reported immediate maternal or neonatal complications. Estimated blood loss was 225 mL. On the seventh day of admission, the platelet count had risen to 128,000/μL and the malaria smear was negative. She was discharged on the twelfth day. Conclusion: Pregnant women diagnosed with either infection who exhibit severe symptoms should be extensively investigated for coinfection, and prompt treatment should be initiated to optimise maternal and fetal outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Malaria and dengue are mosquito-borne infections that pose significant health risks during pregnancy and may adversely affect maternal and fetal outcomes.
Design and population
This case report describes a 22-year-old primigravida at 37 weeks and 4 days of gestation who presented with headache, fever, vomiting and diarrhoea, and was diagnosed with malaria and dengue coinfection.
Core methods
Testing identified Plasmodium vivax malaria, and dengue-specific IgM and IgG antibodies were positive. Management included intravenous crystalloid fluids, platelet transfusions, fresh frozen plasma and chloroquine.
Main results
The platelet count was 40,000/μL at the receiving hospital, and ultrasound showed oligohydramnios with an amniotic fluid index of 4.14 cm. The platelet count improved to 60,000/μL on the third day, and to 128,000/μL on the seventh day. She was discharged on the twelfth day.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that pregnant women diagnosed with either infection who exhibit severe symptoms should be extensively investigated for coinfection, and prompt treatment should be initiated to optimise maternal and fetal outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of extensive targeted testing and prompt treatment for coinfection in pregnant women to minimise adverse obstetric outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a case report, which limits its generalisability, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed clinical presentation and management.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

非流行环境中急诊科的登革热快速诊断测试对登革热诊断级联的影响:前后研究 (RaDDEO)。

Impact of a point-of-care rapid diagnostic test on the dengue diagnostic cascade in an emergency department in a non-endemic setting: A before-after study (RaDDEO).
作者： Lauriane Robin, Vincent Foulongne, Damien Perier, Bruno Lehodey, Xavier Bobbia, Vincent Le Moing, Charlotte Boullé
摘要中文翻译目的:及时识别登革热在非流行地区越来越重要,这些地区输入的病毒血症病例可能引发当地的 传播。我们评估了引入联合护理点快速诊断测试(RDT;NS1/IgM/IgG)是否缩短了急诊科登革热 诊断 的时间;其次,我们(a)评估了其对住院时间、入院和通知的影响,(b)重建了诊断级联以识别过度的时间流逝和错过的机会,以及(c)评估了其诊断性能。方法:在法国南部蒙彼利埃大学医院进行单中心回顾性前后研究。对接受登革热测试的成年人在 RDT 前 (10/2022-07/2024) 和 RDT 后 (08/2024-05/2026) 期间进行比较。主要终点是从到达诊断结果到获得诊断结果的时间。RDT 性能根据临床 诊断 进行评估。结果:在 88 名患者中(36 名 RDT 前,52 名 RDT 后),28 名患者患有登革热,全部都是回国旅行者,57% 的患者在 ED 就诊前曾寻求医疗保健。实施 RDT 后,达到 诊断 的时间从 24.3 小时减少到 2.5 小时(中位增益 19.6 小时,95%CI 13.9-44.8;p < 0.001)。最耗时的步骤是实验室周转(中位 25.6 小时)。虽然在住院时间、住院时间和强制通知方面没有观察到显着差异,但该研究评估这些结果的力度不足。RDT敏感性为100%,特异性为70.3%;所有假阳性均为抗体条带,主要出现在输入性疟疾患者中。结论:RDT 将 诊断 的时间缩短了 20 小时,没有遗漏任何急性登革热病例。然而,它并没有加速下游临床或公共卫生流程,突显出需要提高一线临床医生的意识,以确保及时通知。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在非流行地区,及时识别登革热变得越来越重要,这些地区输入的病毒血症病例可能会引发当地的 传播。我们评估了引入联合护理点快速诊断测试(RDT;NS1/IgM/IgG)是否可以缩短急诊室登革热 诊断 的时间。
设计与对象
法国南部蒙彼利埃大学医院的单中心回顾性前后研究。对接受登革热测试的成年人在 RDT 前 (10/2022-07/2024) 和 RDT 后 (08/2024-05/2026) 期间进行比较。
核心方法
主要终点是从到达诊断结果到获得诊断结果的时间。RDT 性能根据临床 诊断 进行评估。
主要结果
在 88 名患者中(36 名 RDT 前,52 名 RDT 后),28 名患者患有登革热,他们都是回国旅行者,57% 的患者在急诊室就诊之前曾寻求医疗保健。实施 RDT 后,达到 诊断 的时间从 24.3 小时减少到 2.5 小时(中位增益 19.6 小时,95%CI 13.9-44.8;p < 0.001)。最耗时的步骤是实验室周转(中位 25.6 小时)。虽然在住院时间、住院时间和强制通知方面没有观察到显着差异,但该研究评估这些结果的力度不足。RDT敏感性为100%,特异性为70.3%;所有假阳性均为抗体条带,主要出现在输入性疟疾患者中。
解释与创新
RDT 将 诊断 的时间缩短了 20 小时,没有遗漏任何急性登革热病例。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了即时 RDT 在非流行环境中提高诊断效率的潜力,这可能有助于早期干预和预防当地的 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究无法评估住院时间、入院和强制通知等结果,并且由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Impact of a point-of-care rapid diagnostic test on the dengue diagnostic cascade in an emergency department in a non-endemic setting: A before-after study (RaDDEO).

Authors: Lauriane Robin, Vincent Foulongne, Damien Perier, Bruno Lehodey, Xavier Bobbia, Vincent Le Moing, Charlotte Boullé
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Timely recognition of dengue is increasingly important in non-endemic regions, where imported viremic cases may trigger local transmission. We evaluated whether introducing a combined point-of-care rapid diagnostic test (RDT; NS1/IgM/IgG) shortened the time to dengue diagnosis in an emergency department; secondarily, we (a) assessed its impact on length of stay, admission and notification, (b) reconstructed the diagnostic cascade to identify excessive time lapses and missed opportunities, and (c) evaluated its diagnostic performance. METHODS: Single-center retrospective before-after study in Montpellier University Hospital, southern France. Adults undergoing dengue testing were compared between pre-RDT (10/2022-07/2024) and post-RDT (08/2024-05/2026) periods. The primary endpoint was the time from arrival to availability of the diagnostic result. RDT performance was assessed against the clinical diagnosis. RESULTS: Among 88 patients (36 pre-, 52 post-RDT), 28 had dengue, all of them returning travelers, and 57% had sought healthcare before ED presentation. After RDT implementation, time to diagnosis decreased from 24.3 to 2.5 h (median gain 19.6 h, 95%CI 13.9-44.8; p < 0.001). The most time-consuming step was laboratory turnaround (median 25.6 h). While no significant differences were observed in terms of length of stay, hospital admission, and mandatory notification, the study was underpowered to assess these outcomes. RDT sensitivity was 100% and specificity 70.3%; all false positives were antibody bands, primarily in patients with imported malaria. CONCLUSION: The RDT shortened time to diagnosis by 20 h without missing any cases of acute dengue. However, it did not accelerate downstream clinical or public-health processes, highlighting a need to raise frontline clinician awareness in view of ensuring timely notification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Timely recognition of dengue is increasingly important in non-endemic regions, where imported viremic cases may trigger local transmission. We evaluated whether introducing a combined point-of-care rapid diagnostic test (RDT; NS1/IgM/IgG) shortened the time to dengue diagnosis in an emergency department.
Design and population
Single-center retrospective before-after study in Montpellier University Hospital, southern France. Adults undergoing dengue testing were compared between pre-RDT (10/2022-07/2024) and post-RDT (08/2024-05/2026) periods.
Core methods

The findings highlight the potential of point-of-care RDTs to improve diagnostic efficiency in non-endemic settings, which could aid in early intervention and prevention of local transmission.

用于 登革热 检测的特征提取和集成学习

Feature Extraction and Ensemble Learning for the Detection of Dengue Fever
作者： 元 笠原, Tad Gonsalves, Gen Kasahara
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种机器学习模型,通过将医疗记录转换为文本数据并使用无监督学习提取特征来识别 登革热 患者。该研究解决了改进登革热检测方法的需求,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
设计与对象
该研究使用从医疗记录转换为文本数据生成的数据集设计了一个机器学习模型。人群包括 登革热 患者,该研究是使用各种深度神经网络和集成学习技术进行的。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用 OCR 技术将病历转换为文本数据,通过无监督学习生成高维特征,以及构建训练数据集。使用七种深度神经网络、FT-Transformer和ResNet作为基础模型,并使用Support 媒介 Machine、Random Forest、DNN和FT-Transformer作为元模型进行集成学习。
主要结果
研究发现,DNN2 显示了最高的准确率,而 FT-Transformer 显示了最高的召回率。使用精确率、召回率、F1 分数和马修斯相关系数 (MCC) 来评估性能。使用 Optuna 进行超参数优化。
解释与创新
作者解释说,所提出的模型可以改善 登革热 的检测,特别是在召回率方面。他们指出,该模型的性能与现有方法相比具有竞争力,并强调使用集成学习作为一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确、更高效的登革热检测工具提供信息,这在资源有限的环境中特别有用。该模型提高召回率的能力可以帮助识别更多 登革热 案例。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用单一数据集和过度拟合的可能性。由于缺乏外部验证和普遍性问题,证据强度中等。

Feature Extraction and Ensemble Learning for the Detection of Dengue Fever

Authors: 元 笠原, Tad Gonsalves, Gen Kasahara
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In this study, we propose a new machine learning model to identify dengue fever patients. To evaluate the performance of the models, we used precision, recall, F1 score, and Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) as the main indicators, and conducted hyperparameter optimization using Optuna to evaluate and verify the models from multiple perspectives. https://doi.org/10.1016/S0966-842X(03)00206-3 - Jayakody, A., et al.: Predicting dengue fever incidence using machine learning techniques: a case study from Sri Lanka. Specifically, we convert actual medical records into text data using Optical Character Recognition (OCR) technology and extract features from this data. Subsequently, we generate high-dimensional features using unsupervised learning and construct a training dataset. Using this training data, we adopted seven types of Deep Neural Networks (DNN), FT-Transformer, and ResNet as base models. Additionally, we performed ensemble learning (stacking) by using Support Vector Machine, Random Forest, DNN, and FT-Transformer as meta-models, and compared and verified each stacking model. Comparing the performance of the 9 base models, DNN2 shows the highest accuracy, while FT-Transformer shows the highest recall. Question and background
The study aimed to develop a machine learning model to identify dengue fever patients by converting medical records into text data and extracting features using unsupervised learning. The research addressed the need for improved dengue detection methods, particularly in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
The study designed a machine learning model using a dataset generated from medical records converted into text data. The population included dengue fever patients, and the study was conducted using a variety of deep neural networks and ensemble learning techniques.
Core methods
The methods involved converting medical records into text data using OCR technology, generating high-dimensional features with unsupervised learning, and constructing a training dataset. Seven types of deep neural networks, FT-Transformer, and ResNet were used as base models, and ensemble learning was performed using Support Vector Machine, Random Forest, DNN, and FT-Transformer as meta-models.
Main results
The study found that DNN2 showed the highest accuracy, while FT-Transformer showed the highest recall. The performance was evaluated using precision, recall, F1 score, and Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC). Hyperparameter optimization was conducted using Optuna.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the proposed model could improve the detection of dengue fever, particularly in terms of recall. They noted that the model's performance was competitive with existing methods and highlighted the use of ensemble learning as a novel approach.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of more accurate and efficient dengue detection tools, which could be particularly useful in resource-limited settings. The model's ability to improve recall could help in identifying more cases of dengue fever.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations included the use of a single dataset and the potential for overfitting. The evidence strength was moderate due to the absence of external validation and generalizability concerns.

印度尼西亚三宝垄 Pedurungan 公共场所的伊蚊幼虫繁殖地及其与报告的登革热病例的空间重叠

Aedes larval breeding sites in public places and their spatial overlay with reported dengue cases in Pedurungan, Semarang, Indonesia
作者： Ari Udiyono, Bennaya Chantika S, Martini Martini, M. Sakundarno Adi
摘要中文翻译公共场所可能是登革热 传播 场所,但三宝垄的幼虫调查主要针对房屋。这项描述性横断面研究描述了 Pedurungan 分区公共场所的伊蚊幼虫繁殖地,并将其与报告的登革热病例重叠。2024年3月至5月,对690个公共场所中的97个进行了单幼虫法调查;优先考虑靠近报告病例的地点,因此样本不具有代表性。基于房屋的指数适用于公共场所(HI、CI、BI、LFI;密度图、DF)。在幼虫调查之前,2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 2 月报告的 63 例登革热病例被绘制在描述性 GIS 叠加图中。30个公共场所(30.93%)和389个集装箱中的75个(19.28%)幼虫呈阳性。水桶贡献了最多的阳性容器(75 个中的 70 个),而浴缸的阳性率最高(25.00%)。在采样的阳性容器中,92%的幼虫被鉴定为埃及伊蚊,8%为白纹伊蚊。8 个城中村的平均 DF 值较高,2 个为中值,1 个为低值;Penggaron Kidul 未进行评估。14 个月内报告的高、中、低类别的累计 发病率 分别为每 1,000 人 0.34、0.38 和 0.19,没有明确的分级模式。未建立关联;公共场所控制应辅以住宅监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查公共场所伊蚊幼虫繁殖地与印度尼西亚三宝垄 Pedurungan 报告的登革热病例之间的空间关系,以更好地了解城市环境中的登革热 传播 动态。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 5 月,在印度尼西亚 Pedurungan 街道开展了一项描述性横断面研究,重点关注 690 个公共场所。该研究包括了 97 个接受调查的公共场所,优先考虑的是靠近报告登革热病例的地点,导致样本不具有代表性。
核心方法
该研究使用单幼虫方法来调查公共场所,并根据公共环境调整基于房屋的指数(HI、CI、BI、LFI)。采用描述性地理信息系统叠加来绘制报告的登革热病例和幼虫繁殖地点的地图。
主要结果
30个公共场所(30.93%)和389个集装箱中的75个(19.28%)幼虫呈阳性。水桶贡献了最多的阳性容器(75 个中的 70 个),而浴缸的阳性率最高(25.00%)。在采样的阳性容器中,92%的幼虫被鉴定为埃及伊蚊,8%为白纹伊蚊。
解释与创新
该研究强调了公共场所作为伊蚊潜在繁殖地的重要性,即媒介为登革病毒。它提供了与这些地点相关的登革热病例的新颖空间分析。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,公共场所应纳入登革热 监测 工作范围,因为它们可能对蚊子繁殖和 监测 做出重大贡献。
局限与证据强度
由于优先考虑报告病例附近的地点,该研究的样本不具有代表性,并且在跨类别的累积 发病率 中没有观察到明确的分级模式。证据的强度中等。

Aedes larval breeding sites in public places and their spatial overlay with reported dengue cases in Pedurungan, Semarang, Indonesia

Authors: Ari Udiyono, Bennaya Chantika S, Martini Martini, M. Sakundarno Adi
Original abstract (English)
Public places may serve as dengue transmission sites, but larval surveys in Semarang have mostly targeted houses. This descriptive cross-sectional study describes Aedes larval breeding sites in public places in Pedurungan sub-district and overlays them with reported dengue cases. In March–May 2024, 97 of 690 public places were surveyed with the single-larva method; sites near reported cases were prioritised, so the sample is not representative. House-based indices were adapted to public places (HI, CI, BI, LFI; density figure, DF). Sixty-three dengue cases reported from January 2023 to February 2024, before the larval survey, were mapped in a descriptive GIS overlay. Thirty public places (30.93%) and 75 of 389 containers (19.28%) were larva-positive. Buckets contributed most positive containers (70 of 75), whereas bathtubs had the highest positivity (25.00%). Among sampled positive containers, 92% of larvae identified were Aedes aegypti and 8% Aedes albopictus. Eight urban villages had high, two medium and one low mean DF; Penggaron Kidul was not assessed. Reported cumulative incidence over 14 months was 0.34, 0.38 and 0.19 per 1,000 in the high, medium and low categories, with no clear graded pattern. No association is established; control in public places should be complemented by residential surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the spatial relationship between Aedes larval breeding sites in public places and reported dengue cases in Pedurungan, Semarang, Indonesia, to better understand dengue transmission dynamics in urban settings.
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted in Pedurungan sub-district, Indonesia, from March to May 2024, focusing on 690 public places. The study included 97 surveyed public places, with sites near reported dengue cases prioritized, resulting in a non-representative sample.
Core methods
The study used a single-larva method to survey public places and adapted house-based indices (HI, CI, BI, LFI) to public settings. A descriptive GIS overlay was employed to map reported dengue cases with larval breeding sites.
Main results
Thirty public places (30.93%) and 75 of 389 containers (19.28%) were larva-positive. Buckets contributed most positive containers (70 of 75), whereas bathtubs had the highest positivity (25.00%). Among sampled positive containers, 92% of larvae identified were Aedes aegypti and 8% were Aedes albopictus.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the importance of public places as potential breeding sites for Aedes mosquitoes, which are vectors for dengue virus. It provides a novel spatial analysis of dengue cases in relation to these sites.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that public places should be included in dengue surveillance efforts, as they may contribute significantly to mosquito breeding and transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's sample is not representative due to prioritization of sites near reported cases, and no clear graded pattern was observed in cumulative incidence across categories. The evidence is moderate in strength.

登革热疾病传播模型的全局特性及最优控制研究

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model
作者： Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, Idrissa Ibrango, Aboudramane Guiro, Harouna Ouédraogo
摘要中文翻译在这项工作中,我们制定了一个非线性微分方程组,对 登革热 的 传播 动力学进行建模。该模型表现出经典的阈值行为。我们证明,当基本再生数小于 1 时,无病平衡局部和全局渐近稳定;当基本再生数大于 1 时,地方性平衡在一定条件下全局渐近稳定。平衡的稳定性是通过利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论和拉萨尔不变原理推导出来的。此外,我们还制定了一个最优控制问题,涉及预防措施、易感人群疫苗接种的投资以及与杀虫剂治疗相关的投资。提供数值模拟来说明理论结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解登革热疾病 传播 的整体特性,并探索其预防和管理的最佳控制策略。
设计与对象
该研究涉及制定一个数学模型来表示 登革热 的 传播 动态,重点关注疾病的整体特性和最佳控制措施的应用。
核心方法
利用李亚普诺夫稳定性理论和拉萨尔不变原理对模型进行分析,以确定无病平衡和地方病平衡的稳定性,并制定最优控制问题来评估预防措施。
主要结果
该模型表现出经典的阈值行为,当基本再生数小于 1 时,无病平衡局部和全局渐近稳定;当基本再生数在一定条件下超过 1 时,地方性平衡全局渐近稳定。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为理解登革热 传播 动态的理论框架,并提出了涉及 疫苗接种 和杀虫剂 治疗 的最佳控制策略,作为疾病管理的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为制定针对性干预措施(例如 疫苗接种 计划和使用杀虫剂来控制登革热 传播 和减轻疾病负担)提供了理论基础。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,不涉及经验数据,这限制了其对现实场景的适用性。证据是理论上的,来自数学分析。

A study of the global properties and optimal control of dengue disease transmission model

Authors: Harouna Ouedraogo, Dramane Ouedraogo, Idrissa Ibrango, Aboudramane Guiro, Harouna Ouédraogo
Original abstract (English)
In this work, we formulate a nonlinear system of differential equations that models the transmission dynamics of dengue fever. The model exhibits the classical threshold behavior. We prove that when the basic reproduction number is less than one, the disease-free equilibrium is locally and globally asymptotically stable and when the basic reproduction number is greater than one the endemic equilibrium is globally asymptotically stable under certain conditions. The stability of equilibria is derived through the use of Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle. In addition we formulate an optimal control problem involving preventive measures, the investment in vaccination of susceptible humans and the investment related to insecticide treatment. Numerical simulations are provided to illustrate the theoretical results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the global properties of dengue disease transmission and explore optimal control strategies for its prevention and management.
Design and population
The study involved the formulation of a mathematical model to represent the transmission dynamics of dengue fever, focusing on the global properties of the disease and the application of optimal control measures.
Core methods
The model was analyzed using Lyapunov stability theory and LaSalle's invariance principle to determine the stability of disease-free and endemic equilibria, and an optimal control problem was formulated to evaluate preventive measures.
Main results
The model demonstrated classical threshold behavior, with the disease-free equilibrium being locally and globally asymptotically stable when the basic reproduction number is less than one, and the endemic equilibrium being globally asymptotically stable when the basic reproduction number exceeds one under certain conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a theoretical framework for understanding dengue transmission dynamics and proposed optimal control strategies involving vaccination and insecticide treatment as novel approaches to disease management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a theoretical basis for developing targeted interventions such as vaccination programs and insecticide use to control dengue transmission and reduce disease burden.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and does not involve empirical data, which limits its applicability to real-world scenarios. The evidence is theoretical and derived from mathematical analysis.

来自 Oryctes rhinoceros 的半乳糖凝集素 8 的分子表征和计算机分析及其可能的 抗病毒药物 针对 登革病毒 的活性

Molecular characterization and in silico analyses of galectin-8 from Oryctes rhinoceros and its possible antiviral activity against the dengue virus
作者： Chitti Babu Keerthana, Kumbalingam Monica, Ayikkara Peroor Mahi Dev, S. S. Jayaraj, Sundaram Janarthanan, ChittiBabu Keerthana
摘要中文翻译数据可用性 当前研究期间没有生成或分析数据集。https://doi.org/10.1016/j.procbio.2022.12.008 - Bidon N、Brichory F、Bourguet P、Le Pennec JP、Dazord L (2001) Galectin-8:半乳糖凝集素的复杂亚家族(综述)。https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkl124 - Ghazarian H、Idoni B、Oppenheimer SB (2011) 糖生物学综述:碳水化合物、凝集素及其对癌症治疗的影响。https://doi.org/10.1093/oxfordjournals.jbchem.a021192 - Kinene T、Wainaina J、Maina S、Boykin LM (2016) 树生根,方法。摘要 从犀牛幼虫中对半乳糖凝集素 8 在不同器官中的表达及其可能的抗病毒活性进行了计算机分析。Galectin-8 扩增、测序和氨基酸预测以及分子分析揭示了其他几种昆虫物种中半乳糖凝集素特有的功能域。此外,半乳糖凝集素 8 转录物的定量显示,血细胞裂解物中的表达水平显着高于任何其他器官。特异性抑制半乳糖凝集素活性的聚糖乳糖被用作分子对接中的配体,产生-8.5 kcal/mol的结合能,揭示了乳糖对半乳糖凝集素8的特异性。为评估结合稳定性而进行的 MD 模拟揭示了 galectin-8 和乳糖之间的相互作用。galectin-8 与登革热病毒非结构蛋白 NS1 和 NS5 之间的蛋白质-蛋白质对接表明 galectin-8 可能具有 抗病毒药物 活性。参考文献 - Abraham MJ、Murtola T、Schulz R、Páll S、Smith JC、Hess B、Lindahl E (2015) GROMACS:通过从笔记本电脑到超级计算机的多级并行性进行高性能分子模拟。https://doi.org/10.1016/j.softx.2015.06.001 - Arumugam...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征来自 Oryctes rhinoceros 的半乳糖凝集素 8,并探讨其针对 抗病毒药物 的潜在 抗病毒药物 活性。该研究解决了了解 宿主 与病毒相互作用以及登革热感染中潜在的 抗病毒药物 机制的需要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及来自 Oryctes rhinoceros grubs 的半乳糖凝集素 8 的分子表征,重点关注其表达和功能域。没有研究特定的人类或动物群体。
核心方法
Galectin-8 被扩增、测序并分析氨基酸预测和分子结构域。通过分子对接和模拟评估了该蛋白质与乳糖和登革热病毒非结构蛋白的相互作用。
主要结果
与其他器官相比,半乳糖凝集素 8 在血细胞裂解物中的表达显着更高。分子对接显示半乳糖凝集素 8 和乳糖之间的结合能为 -8.5 kcal/mol,表明具有特异性。蛋白质-蛋白质对接表明 抗病毒药物 对登革热病毒非结构蛋白 NS1 和 NS5 具有潜在活性。
解释与创新
该研究为半乳糖凝集素 8 的功能域及其针对 抗病毒药物 的潜在 抗病毒药物 活性提供了新的见解,强调了其对乳糖的特异性及其与登革热病毒蛋白的相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为未来关于半乳糖凝集素 8 在登革热感染中的作用的研究提供信息,并可能导致新的 抗病毒药物 策略。Galectin-8 对乳糖的特异性表明了其潜在的治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括缺乏实验数据和使用计算机方法,这可能无法完全反映体内相互作用。由于依赖计算方法,证据强度中等。

Molecular characterization and in silico analyses of galectin-8 from Oryctes rhinoceros and its possible antiviral activity against the dengue virus

Authors: Chitti Babu Keerthana, Kumbalingam Monica, Ayikkara Peroor Mahi Dev, S. S. Jayaraj, Sundaram Janarthanan, ChittiBabu Keerthana
Original abstract (English)
Data availability No datasets were generated or analysed during the current study. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.procbio.2022.12.008 - Bidon N, Brichory F, Bourguet P, Le Pennec JP, Dazord L (2001) Galectin-8: a complex sub-family of galectins (review). https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkl124 - Ghazarian H, Idoni B, Oppenheimer SB (2011) A glycobiology review: carbohydrates, lectins and implications in cancer therapeutics. https://doi.org/10.1093/oxfordjournals.jbchem.a021192 - Kinene T, Wainaina J, Maina S, Boykin LM (2016) Rooting trees, methods for. Abstract The expression of galectin-8 in various organs and its characterization through in silico analysis with possible anti-viral activity were carried out from the grubs of Oryctes rhinoceros. Galectin-8 amplification, sequencing, and amino acid prediction, along with molecular analysis, revealed functional domains specific to galectins in several other insect species. Further, quantification of galectin-8 transcripts revealed significantly higher expression levels in hemocyte lysate than in any other organ. The glycan lactose, which specifically inhibited galectin activity, was used as a ligand in molecular docking, yielding a binding energy of -8.5 kcal/mol and revealing lactose’s specificity for galectin-8. MD simulations performed to assess binding stability revealed an interaction between galectin-8 and lactose. The protein-protein docking between galectin-8 and the dengue viral non-structural proteins NS1 and NS5 suggested the probable antiviral activity of galectin-8. References - Abraham MJ, Murtola T, Schulz R, Páll S, Smith JC, Hess B, Lindahl E (2015) GROMACS: high-performance molecular simulations through multi-level parallelism from laptops to supercomputers. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.softx.2015.06.001 - Arumugam...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize galectin-8 from Oryctes rhinoceros and explore its potential antiviral activity against dengue virus. The research addressed the need for understanding host-virus interactions and potential antiviral mechanisms in dengue infection.
Design and population
The study involved molecular characterization of galectin-8 from Oryctes rhinoceros grubs, focusing on its expression and functional domains. No specific human or animal population was studied.
Core methods
Galectin-8 was amplified, sequenced, and analyzed for amino acid prediction and molecular domains. The protein’s interaction with lactose and dengue viral non-structural proteins was assessed through molecular docking and simulations.
Main results
Galectin-8 showed significantly higher expression in hemocyte lysate compared to other organs. Molecular docking revealed a binding energy of -8.5 kcal/mol between galectin-8 and lactose, suggesting specificity. Protein-protein docking suggested potential antiviral activity against dengue viral non-structural proteins NS1 and NS5.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provided novel insights into the functional domains of galectin-8 and its potential antiviral activity against dengue virus, highlighting its specificity for lactose and its interaction with dengue viral proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform future studies on galectin-8’s role in dengue infection and potentially lead to new antiviral strategies. The specificity of galectin-8 for lactose suggests a potential therapeutic target.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s limitations include the absence of experimental data and the use of in silico methods, which may not fully reflect in vivo interactions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on computational methods.

登革热多米诺骨牌:在 Unipampa 中开展教育教育战略

Dominó da dengue: jogo educativo como estratégia de educação em saúde no Conhecendo a Unipampa
作者： Valdir Paz Ribeiro Junior, Bruna da Costa Ribeiro, Alexandro Gularte Schäfer, Lisie Alende Prates
摘要中文翻译登革热是公共问题的一个重要组成部分,预防措施包括社区的参与、教育和环境因素的识别,以利于埃及伊蚊的繁殖。就上下文而言,教育策略和游戏与儿童和青少年的儿童教育相关。目标是与基础教育学生的积极性有关的“登革热主宰”教育游戏的详细体验和利用。与潘帕联邦大学 (UNIPAMPA) 学院相关的经验相关课程,包括与 Saúde (PET-Saúde) 气候相关的教育计划,包括“Conhecendo a UNIPAMPA”,以及乌拉圭/RS。参加了 30 名访问学校的学生,符合活动响应的估计,并进行了定量分析。我们精心设计了传统多米诺骨牌动态、驱蚊器、传播、传播原理、登革热和预防药物,包括识别和消除蚊子。在活动期间,学生们会回答提出的问题,并与学术界媒体讨论有关的问题。最后,准备好精心制作的工具,以便注册员可以感知游戏和体验。在学生和中间人之间的互动中,递归地应用情境,以继续讨论登革热论坛中的问题、回复和对话。一个清晰的游戏和教育经验,并促进大学和社区教育的发展。预先准备可能的工具或注册参与者的感知,构成子信息的源头或递归和未来活动的主要信息。Entretanto 是一项登记参与者人数和具体限制的操作,作为游戏教育效果的结论。结论是“登革热多米诺骨牌”构成了在没有实际行动背景的情况下参与的中间递归。为社区提供新的辅助经验,并发送必要的验证系统以帮助预防和预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是公共问题的一个重要组成部分,预防措施包括社区的参与、教育和环境因素的识别,以利于埃及伊蚊的繁殖。
设计与对象
与潘帕联邦大学 (UNIPAMPA) 学院相关的经验相关的培训,包括与 Saúde (PET-Saúde) 气候相关的教育计划,包括“Conhecendo a UNIPAMPA”,以及乌拉圭/RS。

我们精心设计了传统多米诺骨牌动态、驱蚊器、传播、传播原理、登革热和预防药物,包括识别和消除蚊子。
主要结果

一个清晰的游戏和教育经验,并促进大学和社区教育的发展。
局限与证据强度
Entretanto 是一项登记参与者人数和具体限制的操作,作为游戏教育效果的结论。

Dominó da dengue: jogo educativo como estratégia de educação em saúde no Conhecendo a Unipampa

Authors: Valdir Paz Ribeiro Junior, Bruna da Costa Ribeiro, Alexandro Gularte Schäfer, Lisie Alende Prates
Original abstract (English)
A dengue constitui um importante problema de saúde pública, cuja prevenção envolve a participação da comunidade, a Educação em Saúde e a identificação de fatores ambientais que favorecem a proliferação do mosquito Aedes aegypti. Nesse contexto, estratégias educativas e lúdicas podem aproximar os conhecimentos em saúde do cotidiano de crianças e adolescentes. Objetiva-se relatar a experiência de elaboração e utilização do jogo educativo “Dominó da Dengue” em uma atividade com estudantes da educação básica. Trata-se de um relato de experiência acerca de uma ação desenvolvida por acadêmicos da Universidade Federal do Pampa (UNIPAMPA), incluindo discentes vinculados ao Programa de Educação pelo Trabalho para a Saúde (PET-Saúde) Clima, durante o evento “Conhecendo a UNIPAMPA”, em Uruguaiana/RS. Participaram aproximadamente 30 estudantes de escolas visitantes, conforme estimativa dos responsáveis pela atividade, sem registro do quantitativo exato. O jogo foi elaborado com base na dinâmica tradicional do dominó, adaptada para abordar o mosquito vetor, a transmissão, os principais sinais e sintomas da dengue e as medidas de prevenção, incluindo a identificação e a eliminação de criadouros. Durante a atividade, os estudantes responderam às questões apresentadas nas peças e discutiram os conteúdos com os acadêmicos mediadores. Ao término, preencheram um instrumento elaborado pelos autores para registrar suas percepções sobre o jogo e a experiência vivenciada. A aplicação do recurso proporcionou situações de interação entre os estudantes e os mediadores, nas quais os conteúdos sobre dengue foram abordados por meio de perguntas, respostas e diálogo. A experiência articulou ludicidade e Educação em Saúde e promoveu a aproximação entre a universidade e a comunidade escolar. O preenchimento do instrumento possibilitou o registro das percepções dos participantes, constituindo uma fonte de informações para subsidiar o aprimoramento do recurso e de futuras atividades. Entretanto, a ausência de registro exato do número de participantes e de avaliação específica da aprendizagem limita as conclusões sobre os efeitos educativos do jogo. Conclui-se que o “Dominó da Dengue” constituiu um recurso para mediar a abordagem participativa da temática no contexto da atividade realizada. A experiência oferece subsídios para novas ações com a comunidade escolar, sendo necessárias avaliações sistemáticas para verificar sua contribuição para a aprendizagem e para as práticas de prevenção.

结合抗原工程的四价 mRNA-LNP 登革热 疫苗 赋予广泛的保护性免疫力

A tetravalent mRNA-LNP dengue vaccine incorporating antigen engineering confers broad protective immunity
作者： Shih-Han Ko, Monika Kumari, Hsiu-Ting Lin, Ming-Jane Wu, Wan-Yu Chen, Nai-Shu Chen, Hsuan-An Chen, Yu‐Fen Lu, Shih-Chieh Su, Yi‐Ling Lin
摘要中文翻译全球登革热 疫苗 的增加凸显了对安全且具有广泛保护性的 疫苗 的迫切需求。登革病毒 (DENV) 疫苗接种 需要针对四种抗原不同血清型的平衡免疫力,同时最大限度地降低 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的风险。在这里,我们开发了一种四价 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,在 DENV 包膜 (E) 蛋白中加入 N8R 取代,并用新型可电离脂质 AS-CL-28 配制。血清型特异性 mRNA-LNP 组成的优化确定了 4:1:4:4 配方 (DENV1:DENV2:DENV3:DENV4),可降低 DENV2 免疫优势并改善所有四种血清型中和抗体反应的平衡。N8R 替代的结合为被动保护模型中的多种 DENV 血清型提供了额外的保护作用。总之,这些发现为开发下一代四价 DENV mRNA 疫苗 提供了一个框架,可改善免疫平衡和保护功效,同时支持未来的黄病毒 疫苗 设计。

提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。

主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。

A tetravalent mRNA-LNP dengue vaccine incorporating antigen engineering confers broad protective immunity

Authors: Shih-Han Ko, Monika Kumari, Hsiu-Ting Lin, Ming-Jane Wu, Wan-Yu Chen, Nai-Shu Chen, Hsuan-An Chen, Yu‐Fen Lu, Shih-Chieh Su, Yi‐Ling Lin
Original abstract (English)
The increasing global incidence of dengue highlights the urgent need for safe and broadly protective vaccines. Dengue virus (DENV) vaccination requires balanced immunity against four antigenically distinct serotypes while minimizing the risk of antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). Here, we developed a tetravalent mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccine incorporating an N8R substitution in the DENV envelope (E) protein and formulated with a novel ionizable lipid, AS-CL-28. Optimization of serotype-specific mRNA-LNP composition identified a 4:1:4:4 formulation (DENV1:DENV2:DENV3:DENV4) that reduced DENV2 immunodominance and improved the balance of neutralizing antibody responses across all four serotypes. Incorporation of the N8R substitution provided additional protective benefits against multiple DENV serotypes in passive protection models. Together, these findings provide a framework for developing next-generation tetravalent DENV mRNA vaccines that improve immune balance and protective efficacy while supporting future flavivirus vaccine design.

Lévy Jump 波动下的随机登革热流行

Stochastic Dengue Endemicity under Lévy Jump Fluctuations
作者： Ragnimwende Sawadogo, Harouna Ouedraogo, Fourtoua Victorien Konané, Fourtoua Victorien Konane
摘要中文翻译登革热的死灰复燃给公共卫生带来了重大挑战。由于传播受到环境和随机波动的影响,因此需要现实的模型。我们提出了一种随机登革热模型,具有人类易感传染性恢复结构和蚊子易感传染性结构,并结合了白噪声和 Levy 跳跃。我们建立了全局正解的存在性和唯一性,证明了无病平衡的矩稳定性和几乎确定的指数稳定性,导出了均值持续存在的充分条件,并以数值方式说明了结果。该分析阐明了随机波动如何影响登革热 传播 及其长期变异性。

科研与公卫意义
该分析阐明了随机波动如何影响登革热 传播 及其长期变异性。

Stochastic Dengue Endemicity under Lévy Jump Fluctuations

Authors: Ragnimwende Sawadogo, Harouna Ouedraogo, Fourtoua Victorien Konané, Fourtoua Victorien Konane
Original abstract (English)
Dengue resurgence poses major public-health challenges. Because transmission is affected by environmental and random fluctuations, realistic models are needed. We propose a stochastic dengue model, with a susceptible infectious recovered structure for humans and a susceptible infectious structure for mosquitoes, incorporating white noise and Levy jumps. We establish the existence and uniqueness of a global positive solution, prove moment and almost sure exponential stability of the disease-free equilibrium, derive sufficient conditions for persistence in mean, and illustrate the results numerically. The analysis clarifies how random fluctuations affect dengue transmission and its long-term variability.

原发性 登革病毒 感染中血清型依赖性融合环和 pr 抗体谱

Serotype-dependent fusion loop and pr antibody profiles in primary dengue virus infections
作者： Elias M. Duarte, Marco Antonio Chapa, Agamjot Bal, Nharae Eugene Lee, Claudia Sánchez-San Martín, Michael H. Verdolin, Aaron Graber, Reinaldo Mercado-Hernandez, José Víctor Zambrana, Guillermina Kuan
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV1-4) 是全世界虫媒病毒疾病的主要原因。包膜 (E) 和膜前 (prM) 蛋白是主要的抗体靶点;在 E 内,保守的融合环 (FL) 是免疫显性交叉反应表位。pr 和 FL 的抗体已与 抗体依赖性增强 相关,但它们对多克隆血清中交叉反应反应的贡献仍不清楚。在这里,我们使用重组抗原和对尼加拉瓜儿童登革热 队列研究 研究的血清进行多重血清学测定,结果表明,抗 pr 反应的程度依赖于血清型,但在 DENV1-4 之间广泛存在交叉反应,而 ZIKV 识别非常有限。所有原发性 DENV 感染都会产生 FL 反应性抗体,但 E-FL 分数因感染血清型和检测抗原而异,揭示了交叉反应反应的不对称血清型依赖性形状。有趣的是,交叉反应 E 结合的程度越大,FL 敏感结合的比例就越低。这些发现表明,初次感染在登革热和寨卡病毒的交叉反应抗体格局上留下了血清型特异性模式的印记,这可能会影响随后的感染结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解原发性 登革病毒 感染中融合环 (FL) 和 prM 蛋白的抗体反应的血清型依赖性模式,弥补有关这些抗体对交叉反应反应的贡献的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用尼加拉瓜儿童登革热 队列研究 研究的血清,采用重组抗原的多重血清学测定来研究原发性 队列研究 感染中的抗体反应。
核心方法
使用多重血清学测定,使用针对 登革病毒 1-4 的融合环 (FL) 和 prM 蛋白的重组抗原来测量抗体反应。
主要结果
抗 pr 反应的强度取决于血清型,但在 DENV1-4 之间广泛存在交叉反应,对寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 的识别非常有限。原发性 DENV 感染产生 FL 反应性抗体,但 E-FL 部分因感染血清型和检测抗原而异,揭示了交叉反应反应的不对称血清型依赖性形状。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,初次感染会在登革热和寨卡病毒的交叉反应抗体格局上留下血清型特异性模式,这可能会影响随后的感染结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于理解交叉反应抗体在 登革病毒 感染中的作用具有重要意义,并可能为未来 疫苗 开发和血清学诊断的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单一 队列研究 的使用以及对原发感染的关注的限制,这可能无法完全代表更广泛的人群。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Serotype-dependent fusion loop and pr antibody profiles in primary dengue virus infections

Authors: Elias M. Duarte, Marco Antonio Chapa, Agamjot Bal, Nharae Eugene Lee, Claudia Sánchez-San Martín, Michael H. Verdolin, Aaron Graber, Reinaldo Mercado-Hernandez, José Víctor Zambrana, Guillermina Kuan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue viruses (DENV1-4) are a leading cause of arboviral disease worldwide. The envelope (E) and premembrane (prM) proteins are major antibody targets; within E, the conserved fusion loop (FL) is an immunodominant cross-reactive epitope. Antibodies to pr and FL have been linked to antibody-dependent enhancement, yet their contribution to the cross-reactive response in polyclonal serum remains undefined. Here using recombinant antigens and a multiplexed serological assay with sera from the Nicaraguan Pediatric Dengue Cohort Study, we show that anti-pr responses are serotype-dependent in magnitude yet broadly cross-reactive among DENV1–4, with very limited ZIKV recognition. All primary DENV infections generated FL-reactive antibodies, but the E-FL fraction varied by infecting serotype and detection antigen, revealing asymmetric serotype-dependent shaping of cross-reactive responses. Interestingly, the greater the magnitude of cross-reactive E binding, the lower the proportion of FL-sensitive binding. These findings show that primary infection imprints serotype-specific patterns on cross-reactive antibody landscapes to dengue and Zika viruses that may influence subsequent infection outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the serotype-dependent patterns of antibody responses to the fusion loop (FL) and prM proteins in primary dengue virus infections, addressing the gap in knowledge regarding the contribution of these antibodies to the cross-reactive response.
Design and population
The study used sera from the Nicaraguan Pediatric Dengue Cohort Study, employing a multiplexed serological assay with recombinant antigens to investigate antibody responses in primary dengue virus infections.
Core methods
Antibody responses were measured using a multiplexed serological assay with recombinant antigens targeting the fusion loop (FL) and prM proteins from dengue viruses 1–4.
Main results
Anti-pr responses were serotype-dependent in magnitude but broadly cross-reactive among DENV1–4, with very limited Zika virus (ZIKV) recognition. Primary DENV infections generated FL-reactive antibodies, but the E-FL fraction varied by infecting serotype and detection antigen, revealing asymmetric serotype-dependent shaping of cross-reactive responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings show that primary infection imprints serotype-specific patterns on cross-reactive antibody landscapes to dengue and Zika viruses, which may influence subsequent infection outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for understanding the role of cross-reactive antibodies in dengue virus infections and may inform future studies on vaccine development and serological diagnostics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single cohort and the focus on primary infections, which may not fully represent the broader population. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

对城市地区登革热和基孔肯雅热流行病学的影响:系统修订

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática
作者： Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Maité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
摘要中文翻译目的:证明城市流行病学对登革热和基孔肯雅热的影响。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);包括 15 个工作室。结果:1990 年世界登革热事件,拉丁美洲地区市场和货运方面存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区得到扩展,并产生了城市爆炸性的产品,并具有持久的关节表现。城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张以及对传播有利于杀虫剂的抵抗力。Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado。结论:虫媒病毒在热带和亚热带城市中发挥着重要作用,并在预防和控制方面发挥着重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在证明登革热和基孔肯雅热对城市流行病学的影响,并以热带和亚热带城市地区的流行病学为对象。
设计与对象
以 PubMed 和 SciELO 数据为基础,对 2012 年至 2025 年公共研究进行了系统的修订;其中包括 15 个针对不同地区的病毒影响的研究。
核心方法
La revisión sistemática se llevó a cabo en PubMed y SciELO,seleccionando estudios entre entre 2012 y 2025. Se 包括 15 个对登革热和基孔肯雅热流行病影响进行分析的研究。
主要结果
1990 年世界登革热疫情的结果是,地区市场和拉丁美洲的其他地区存在差异。基孔肯雅热在 2013 年美洲地区扩展,导致城市爆炸和关节持续表现。
解释与创新
作者解释了城市化、埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的扩张,以抵抗杀虫剂,并有利于传播安博斯病毒。我们对新的真空需求和矢量集成控制策略进行了修订。
科研与公卫意义
热带和亚热带城市的警戒和控制、登革热和基孔肯雅热的信息通报对流行病学意义重大。
局限与证据强度
证据是对抽象系统的修订,限制了分析的深刻性。禁运,该研究的目的是对文学的综合性进行研究,以确保其公正性和公正性。

Impacto epidemiológico del dengue y del chikungunya en zonas urbanas: revisión sistemática

Authors: Nayeli Mishell Zamora-Villaprado, Thais Maité Silva-Saavedra, Mariam Mustafá Ibrahim-Santiago, Bolívar Fernando Mena-Hidalgo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios. Resultados: la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013 y produjo brotes urbanos explosivos, con manifestaciones articulares persistentes. La urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus y la resistencia a insecticidas favorecieron la transmisión. Destacan como estrategias las nuevas vacunas y el control vectorial integrado. Conclusión: ambas arbovirosis son una carga creciente para las ciudades tropicales y subtropicales y exigen vigilancia y control sostenidos.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
El estudio buscaba sintetizar la evidencia sobre el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya, con el objetivo de abordar la creciente carga de enfermedad en zonas urbanas tropicales y subtropicales.
Design and population
Se realizó una revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en bases de datos como PubMed y SciELO, considerando estudios publicados entre 2012 y 2025; se incluyeron 15 estudios que abordaron el impacto de estos virus en diferentes regiones.
Core methods
La revisión sistemática se llevó a cabo en PubMed y SciELO, seleccionando estudios publicados entre 2012 y 2025. Se incluyeron 15 estudios que analizaron el impacto epidemiológico urbano del dengue y del chikungunya.
Main results
Los resultados mostraron que la incidencia mundial del dengue aumentó desde 1990, con diferencias regionales marcadas y una alta carga en América Latina. El chikungunya se expandió por las Américas desde 2013, causando brotes urbanos explosivos y manifestaciones articulares persistentes.
Interpretation and novelty
Los autores interpretaron que la urbanización, la expansión de Aedes aegypti y Aedes albopictus, así como la resistencia a insecticidas, han favorecido la transmisión de ambos virus. Esta revisión destacó la necesidad de nuevas vacunas y estrategias de control vectorial integrado.
Scientific and public-health significance
Los hallazgos pueden informar estrategias de vigilancia y control sostenido en ciudades tropicales y subtropicales, donde el dengue y el chikungunya representan una carga epidemiológica significativa.
Limitations and evidence strength
La evidencia se basa en una revisión sistemática abstracto, lo que limita la profundidad de los análisis. Sin embargo, el estudio proporciona una síntesis generalizada de la literatura disponible, lo que justifica una confianza moderada en los hallazgos.

O USO DA INTELIGÊNCIA ARTIFICIAL NA PREVISIO DA DENUE E DA INFLUENZA COMO APOIO AO PLANEJAMENTO DE INSUMOS LABORATORIAIS 使用人工智能预测登革热和流感以支持实验室供应规划

O USO DA INTELIGÊNCIA ARTIFICIAL NA PREVISÃO DA DENGUE E DA INFLUENZA COMO APOIO AO PLANEJAMENTO DE INSUMOS LABORATORIAIS THE USE OF ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE IN FORECASTING DENGUE AND INFLUENZA TO SUPPORT LABORATORY SUPPLY PLANNING
作者： Luiz Santos, Carlos Campos
摘要中文翻译本研究探讨了人工智能 (AI) 在预测登革热和流感方面的应用及其对实验室供应管理的贡献。基于科学文章、官方文件和公共数据库,采用混合方法和描述性设计进行了文献综述。预测模型提前表明疾病行为的变化,尽管表现因地区、时期、变量和方法而异。在实验室环境中,供应可用性会影响诊断流程,而需求的突然变化会损害补货。没有证据表明这种整合已经建立。结论是,只要人工智能的预测得到验证,人工智能就有潜力支持实验室管理。

预测模型提前表明疾病行为的变化,尽管表现因地区、时期、变量和方法而异。
设计与对象
本研究探讨了人工智能 (AI) 在预测登革热和流感方面的应用及其对实验室供应管理的贡献。
核心方法
基于科学文章、官方文件和公共数据库,采用混合方法和描述性设计进行了文献综述。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
只要人工智能的预测得到验证,人工智能就有潜力支持实验室管理。
科研与公卫意义
没有证据表明这种整合已经建立。
局限与证据强度
在实验室环境中,供应可用性会影响诊断流程,而需求的突然变化会损害补货。

O USO DA INTELIGÊNCIA ARTIFICIAL NA PREVISÃO DA DENGUE E DA INFLUENZA COMO APOIO AO PLANEJAMENTO DE INSUMOS LABORATORIAIS THE USE OF ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE IN FORECASTING DENGUE AND INFLUENZA TO SUPPORT LABORATORY SUPPLY PLANNING

Authors: Luiz Santos, Carlos Campos
Original abstract (English)
This study examines the use of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in forecasting dengue and influenza and its contribution to laboratory supply management. A literature review was conducted, with a mixed-methods approach and descriptive design, based on scientific articles, official documents and public databases. Predictive models indicate in advance changes in disease behavior, although performance varies by region, period, variables and method. In the laboratory context, supply availability influences the diagnostic flow, and sudden changes in demand impair restocking. There is no evidence that this integration is already established. It is concluded that AI has potential to support laboratory management, provided that its forecasts are validated.

Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

印度尼西亚西加里曼丹省坤甸登革热 暴发 管理中针对埃及伊蚊的多部门干预

Multi-sectoral intervention against Aedes aegypti in Dengue outbreak management in Pontianak, West Kalimantan, Indonesia
作者： Fithriyyah Fithriyyah
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种通过埃及伊蚊传播的病毒感染,在热带和亚热带地区流行。坤甸作为印度尼西亚西加里曼丹省的省会,是登革热的流行地。登革热 已成为公共卫生的重大负担,特别是在印度尼西亚这样的发展中国家。除了受到人口增长和流动性增加的影响外,登革热 现在还受到气候变化和环境退化的影响。一般来说,登革热管理主要集中在消灭媒介:埃及伊蚊,以消灭病毒传播。然而,多部门干预对于补充社区健康干预也至关重要,使利益相关者在解决 登革热 问题上发挥协同作用。因此,阻止登革热 暴发 不仅是卫生部门的责任,也是企业、政策制定者、研究和教育机构以及政府的责任。

问题与背景
登革热 是一种通过埃及伊蚊传播的病毒感染,在热带和亚热带地区流行。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
登革热 已成为公共卫生的重大负担,特别是在印度尼西亚这样的发展中国家。
解释与创新
然而,多部门干预对于补充社区健康干预也至关重要,使利益相关者在解决 登革热 问题上发挥协同作用。
科研与公卫意义
因此,阻止登革热 暴发 不仅是卫生部门的责任,也是企业、政策制定者、研究和教育机构以及政府的责任。
局限与证据强度
一般来说,登革热管理主要集中在消灭媒介:埃及伊蚊,以消灭病毒传播。

Multi-sectoral intervention against Aedes aegypti in Dengue outbreak management in Pontianak, West Kalimantan, Indonesia

Authors: Fithriyyah Fithriyyah
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a viral infection that is transmitted through the Aedes aegypti mosquito and is prevalent in tropical and subtropical areas. Pontianak, as the provincial capital of West Kalimantan, Indonesia, is an endemic place for dengue fever. Dengue fever has become a significant burden on public health, especially in a developing country like Indonesia. Besides being influenced by population growth and increasing mobility, dengue fever is now notably worsened by climate change and environmental degradation. Generally, dengue fever management mainly focuses on eliminating the vector: Aedes aegypti mosquito, to end the virus transmission. However, multi-sectoral intervention is also crucial to complement the health intervention in the community, making synergies among stakeholders in addressing Dengue fever. Hence, the responsibility to stop the Dengue outbreak is not only the role of the health sector, but also for the corporations, policymakers, research and educational institutions, and the government.
