右旋糖酐给药与小儿登革热休克综合征中的急性肾损伤:多中心倾向评分匹配的 队列研究 研究

DEXTRAN ADMINISTRATION AND ACUTE KIDNEY INJURY IN PEDIATRIC DENGUE SHOCK SYNDROME: A MULTICENTER PROPENSITY SCORE-MATCHED COHORT STUDY
作者： Rawara Rangsithadakul, Kamolwish Laoprasopwattana, Kanokporn Rangsitsathian, Chirabat Techato, Sipang Pangprasertkul, Chompunut Na Nakorn, Punnathorn Auaboonkanok, Chompoonut Limratchapong
摘要中文翻译背景:急性肾损伤 (AKI) 使高达 10% 的 登革病毒 感染变得复杂,且风险随着疾病严重程度的增加而增加。泰国国家指南推荐Dextran-40作为登革热休克综合征(DSS)的一线胶体疗法,理论上具有肾毒性潜力;然而,关于其与 DSS 儿童 AKI 相关的证据仍然有限。方法:这项多中心观察性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2012 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间在泰国六家医院收治的 15 岁以下诊断为 DSS 的儿童。排除了在第一小时内需要正性肌力药物的难治性休克、出血并发症或液体复苏前 AKI 的患者。AKI 根据 KDIGO 标准定义。进行倾向评分匹配以平衡 dextran-40 接受者和比较者之间的基线协变量。进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析以确定与 AKI 相关的因素。结果:在接受评估的 650 名患者中,604 名患者被纳入 队列研究,592 名患者被纳入最终倾向评分匹配的 队列研究,开始液体复苏后 60 分钟的匹配后绝对标准化平均差与主要模型和严重程度调整模型中 AKI 几率的增加独立相关,这一发现在专门接受 dextran-40 治疗休克的患者亚组中持续存在。在主要模型中,较高的累积 dextran-40 剂量与 AKI 的几率增加相关,但在调整疾病严重程度后,这种相关性减弱。结论:Dextran-40 给药与 AKI 并不独立相关;相反,延迟给药(液体复苏开始后 >60 分钟)和疾病严重程度,特别是难治性休克和 DHF IV 级,分别是 AKI 风险的关键潜在可改变和不可改变的预测因素。这些发现支持在儿科 DSS 中继续使用 dextran-40 作为胶体疗法,同时强调及时给药以尽量减少 AKI 风险的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性肾损伤 (AKI) 使高达 10% 的 登革病毒 感染变得复杂,且风险随着疾病严重程度的增加而增加。登革病毒 感染,尤其是 重症登革热,由于可能导致 AKI,因此构成了重大的公共卫生问题,需要进一步调查风险因素。
设计与对象
这项多中心观察性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2012 年 1 月至 2024 年 12 月期间被诊断患有登革热休克综合征 (DSS) 并入住泰国六家医院的 15 岁以下儿童。排除了在第一小时内需要正性肌力药物、出血并发症或液体复苏前 AKI 的难治性休克患者。
核心方法
AKI 根据 KDIGO 标准定义。进行倾向评分匹配以平衡 dextran-40 接受者和比较者之间的基线协变量。进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析以确定与 AKI 相关的因素。
主要结果
在评估的 650 名患者中,604 名患者被纳入 队列研究,592 名患者被纳入最终倾向评分匹配的 队列研究。延迟给药(开始液体复苏后> 60 分钟)和疾病严重程度,特别是难治性休克和 DHF IV 级,与 AKI 几率增加独立相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,延迟给予 dextran-40 和疾病严重程度,而不是给药本身,是 AKI 风险的关键预测因素。这一发现凸显了及时干预治疗登革热休克综合征的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为临床实践提供了信息,支持继续使用 dextran-40 作为儿童登革热休克综合征的胶体疗法,同时强调需要及时给药以降低 AKI 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,依赖于倾向评分匹配,这可能无法完全消除混杂因素。由于控制所有潜在混杂因素的局限性,证据是中等的。

DEXTRAN ADMINISTRATION AND ACUTE KIDNEY INJURY IN PEDIATRIC DENGUE SHOCK SYNDROME: A MULTICENTER PROPENSITY SCORE-MATCHED COHORT STUDY

Authors: Rawara Rangsithadakul, Kamolwish Laoprasopwattana, Kanokporn Rangsitsathian, Chirabat Techato, Sipang Pangprasertkul, Chompunut Na Nakorn, Punnathorn Auaboonkanok, Chompoonut Limratchapong
Original abstract (English)
Background: Acute kidney injury (AKI) complicates up to 10% of dengue virus infections, with the risk increasing with disease severity. Dextran-40, recommended as first-line colloid therapy for dengue shock syndrome (DSS) in Thailand's national guidelines, has theoretical nephrotoxic potential; however, evidence regarding its association with AKI in children with DSS remains limited. Methods: This multicenter observational cohort study included children under 15 years diagnosed with DSS and admitted to six hospitals in Thailand between January 2012 and December 2024. Patients with refractory shock requiring inotropes within the first hour, bleeding complications, or AKI prior to fluid resuscitation were excluded. AKI was defined according to the KDIGO criteria. Propensity score matching was performed to balance baseline covariates between dextran-40 recipients and comparators. Univariable and multivariable logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with AKI. Results: Of 650 patients assessed, 604 were included in the cohort, and 592 were included in the final propensity score–matched cohort, with post matching absolute standardized mean differences 60 minutes after initiation of fluid resuscitation was independently associated with increased odds of AKI in both the primary and severity-adjusted models, a finding that persisted in the subgroup of patients receiving dextran-40 specifically for shock. A higher cumulative dextran-40 dose was associated with increased odds of AKI in the primary model, but this association was attenuated after adjustment for disease severity. Conclusion: Dextran-40 administration was not independently associated with AKI; instead, delayed administration (>60 minutes after fluid resuscitation initiation) and disease severity, particularly refractory shock and DHF grade IV, were the key potentially modifiable and non-modifiable predictors of AKI risk, respectively. These findings support continued use of dextran-40 as colloid therapy in pediatric DSS while emphasizing the importance of timely administration to minimize AKI risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute kidney injury (AKI) complicates up to 10% of dengue virus infections, with the risk increasing with disease severity. Dengue virus infections, particularly severe dengue, pose a significant public health problem due to their potential to cause AKI, necessitating further investigation into risk factors.
Design and population
This multicenter observational cohort study included children under 15 years diagnosed with dengue shock syndrome (DSS) and admitted to six hospitals in Thailand between January 2012 and December 2024. Patients with refractory shock requiring inotropes within the first hour, bleeding complications, or AKI prior to fluid resuscitation were excluded.
Core methods
AKI was defined according to the KDIGO criteria. Propensity score matching was performed to balance baseline covariates between dextran-40 recipients and comparators. Univariable and multivariable logistic regression analyses were conducted to identify factors associated with AKI.
Main results
Of 650 patients assessed, 604 were included in the cohort, and 592 were included in the final propensity score–matched cohort. Delayed administration (>60 minutes after fluid resuscitation initiation) and disease severity, particularly refractory shock and DHF grade IV, were independently associated with increased odds of AKI.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that delayed administration of dextran-40 and disease severity, rather than the administration itself, are key predictors of AKI risk. This finding highlights the importance of timely intervention in managing dengue shock syndrome.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform clinical practice by supporting the continued use of dextran-40 as colloid therapy in pediatric dengue shock syndrome while emphasizing the need for timely administration to reduce AKI risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and relies on propensity score matching, which may not fully eliminate confounding. The evidence is moderate due to the limitations in controlling for all potential confounding factors.

登革热伴奏卡:PRÁTICAS INOVADORAS DE AÇÕES EM SAÚDE

CARTÃO DE ACOMPANHAMENTO DA DENGUE: PRÁTICAS INOVADORAS DE AÇÕES EM SAÚDE
作者： Jéssica de Paula Oliveira Reis, Talia Cristine Rodrigues Silva, Thayla Porfirio Damian, Amanda de Castro Poleto, Anaclara Tomé Pina, Selma Cristina Tomé Pina, Elder Francisco Latorraca, Nicole Blanco Bernardes, Kemily Silva Silvestre
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决登革热流行地区城市和郊区管理登革热病例的挑战,特别注重改进诊断和 治疗 实践。
设计与对象
该研究在巴西帕索斯/MG 进行,使用了从初级卫生保健和实施“Projeto Zelar”的 流行病学 部门收集的数据。它涉及确定患者管理中的关键缺陷,以增强 治疗 协议。
核心方法
该研究包括由多名医疗保健专业人员参加的健康活动,其中包括关于强制性登革热 诊断 通知、警告标志以及适当的患者分诊和管理程序的重要性的讲座。还开发了“Cartão de Manejo e Acompanhamento”来指导患者并记录临床和实验室数据。
主要结果
该项目确定了登革热患者管理中需要改进的关键领域,强调了更好的诊断实践和患者分诊的必要性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明医疗保健专业人员必须不断改进他们的做法,以确保有效的患者管理和预防并发症,这是加强该地区登革热护理的一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调标准化诊断和 监测 方案的重要性,为改善登革热 监测 和管理的公共卫生策略提供信息,特别是在初级保健机构中。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于抽象数据,没有提供详细的统计或方法分析,将证据的强度限制为中等。

CARTÃO DE ACOMPANHAMENTO DA DENGUE: PRÁTICAS INOVADORAS DE AÇÕES EM SAÚDE

Authors: Jéssica de Paula Oliveira Reis, Talia Cristine Rodrigues Silva, Thayla Porfirio Damian, Amanda de Castro Poleto, Anaclara Tomé Pina, Selma Cristina Tomé Pina, Elder Francisco Latorraca, Nicole Blanco Bernardes, Kemily Silva Silvestre
Original abstract (English)
A Dengue, doença endêmica nos países que apresentam clima tropical e subtropical, especialmente nas áreas urbanas e suburbanas, é transmitida pela picada da fêmea do mosquito Aedes Aegypti. No Brasil, o vírus dessa patologia febril apresenta quatro sorotipos e no último ano foram notificados quase 1,5 milhão de casos no país. Com isso, o “Projeto Zelar” objetiva o desenvolvimento de medidas que possam garantir um melhor manejo dos pacientes infectados. O presente trabalho foi desenvolvido a partir de dados coletados na Atenção Primária e no Setor de Epidemiologia da cidade de Passos / MG, município em que o projeto foi aplicado, de modo que foi possível elencar os principais pontos deficitários a serem atenuados para que os pacientes possam ser adequadamente tratados. Para que fosse possível atingir este objetivo, foi realizado um evento que contou com diversos ouvintes da área da saúde e nele foram ofertadas palestras sobre a importância da notificação compulsória de um diagnóstico de Dengue, quais os sinais de alerta da doença e como deve ser feita a triagem e o manejo dos pacientes. Além disso, também foi desenvolvido o “Cartão de Manejo e Acompanhamento do Paciente com Suspeita de Dengue”, por meio do qual foram fornecidas orientações gerais aos pacientes como também uma área aonde é possível registros clínicos e laboratoriais do paciente para seu acompanhamento. Com tal projeto, foi possível concluir que os profissionais da saúde devem estar sempre em processo de aperfeiçoamento, para que dessa maneira os pacientes recebam as medidas necessárias de tratamento, evitando agravos.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of managing dengue cases in urban and suburban areas where the disease is endemic, particularly focusing on improving diagnostic and treatment practices.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Passos / MG, Brazil, using data collected from Primary Health Care and the Epidemiology Sector, where the "Projeto Zelar" was implemented. It involved identifying key deficits in patient management to enhance treatment protocols.
Core methods
The study included a health event with multiple healthcare professionals, featuring lectures on the importance of mandatory dengue diagnosis notification, warning signs, and proper patient triage and management procedures. A "Cartão de Manejo e Acompanhamento" was also developed to guide patients and record clinical and laboratory data.
Main results
The project enabled the identification of critical areas needing improvement in the management of dengue patients, highlighting the necessity for better diagnostic practices and patient triage.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that healthcare professionals must continuously improve their practices to ensure effective patient management and prevent complications, which is a novel approach to enhancing dengue care in the region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for improving dengue surveillance and management, particularly in primary healthcare settings, by emphasizing the importance of standardized diagnostic and treatment protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract data and does not provide detailed statistical or methodological analysis, limiting the strength of the evidence to moderate.

用于基于折射率检测登革热相关血液改变的高性能 2D 光子晶体开槽腔生物传感器的数值模拟

Numerical Simulation of a High-Performance 2D Photonic Crystal Slotted Cavity Biosensor for Refractive-Index-Based Detection of Dengue-Associated Blood Alterations
作者： Vipin Kumar, Sanjeev Sharma, Sanjeev Kumar Srivastava, Ravinder Kumar, S. K. Srivastava
摘要中文翻译Similar content being viewed by others 数据可用性 在当前研究中未生成或分析任何数据集。https://doi.org/10.1515/ntrev-2025-0284 - 张燕宁、赵宇、吕瑞强(2015)光子晶体传感器综述。摘要 通过监测分离血液成分的折射率,设计并研究了一种高性能的二维(2D)光子晶体槽腔生物传感器,用于无标记检测登革热相关的生理变化。该生物传感器采用硅板和三角形空气孔阵列,结合纳米级槽腔,实现了强光学约束和增强的光-物质相互作用。有限差分时域(FDTD)模拟显示,该传感器具有约20,000的超高品质因子、1800 nm/RIU的灵敏度、窄的共振线宽(FWHM ≈ 0.096–0.099 nm)以及超过18,000 RIU−1的图示因子。通过分析登革热感染期间分离血红蛋白、血小板和血浆的特征折射率变化,评估了其传感性能,分别获得了显著的共振波长偏移+63.00 nm、−59.40 nm和−23.40 nm。通过一阶电磁扰动理论阐明了线性灵敏度的物理机制,证实了在折射率范围n = 1.330–1.400内具有卓越的线性响应(R2 = 0.9996)。此外,还全面分析了血液分馏程序、近2.0 μm的光学色散、制造公差、温度敏感性和多通道光流体架构的集成。所提出的槽状光子晶体平台为无标记光学监测血细胞变化提供了坚实的计算基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种高性能生物传感器,通过对分离的血液成分进行折射率监测,无标记地检测登革热相关的生理变化。
设计与对象
进行了数值模拟研究,以评估 2D 光子晶体槽腔生物传感器的传感性能,以检测登革热感染期间与分离的血红蛋白、血小板和血浆相关的特征折射率变化。
核心方法
采用时域有限差分(FDTD)模拟来分析生物传感器的性能,包括其灵敏度、品质因数和共振线宽,同时采用一阶电磁摄动理论来阐明控制线性灵敏度的物理机制。
主要结果
该生物传感器表现出 1800 nm/RIU 的灵敏度、窄共振线宽(FWHM ≈ 0.096–0.099 nm)以及超过 18,000 RIU−1 的品质因数。血红蛋白、血小板和血浆分别观察到明显的共振波长偏移+63.00 nm、-59.40 nm 和-23.40 nm。
解释与创新
该研究为与登革热病理学相关的血液学变化的无标记光学监测提供了强大的计算基础,突出了生物传感器卓越的灵敏度和线性响应。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为非侵入性、实时监测与登革热相关的血液变化提供了潜在的工具,这可以提高诊断准确性并支持登革热管理的早期干预策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于数值模拟,不包括实验验证,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于依赖计算模型而不是经验数据,证据并不充分。

Numerical Simulation of a High-Performance 2D Photonic Crystal Slotted Cavity Biosensor for Refractive-Index-Based Detection of Dengue-Associated Blood Alterations

Authors: Vipin Kumar, Sanjeev Sharma, Sanjeev Kumar Srivastava, Ravinder Kumar, S. K. Srivastava
Original abstract (English)
Similar content being viewed by others Data Availability No datasets were generated or analysed during the current study. https://doi.org/10.1515/ntrev-2025-0284 - Zhang YN, Zhao Y, Lv RQ (2015) A review of photonic crystal sensors. Abstract A high-performance two-dimensional (2D) photonic crystal slotted cavity biosensor is numerically designed and investigated for label-free detection of dengue-associated physiological alterations through refractive-index monitoring of separated blood constituents. The biosensor employs a silicon slab with a triangular lattice of air holes and a nanoscale slot cavity, enabling strong optical confinement and enhanced light–matter interaction. Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) simulations demonstrate an ultra-high-quality factor of approximately 20,000, a sensitivity of 1800 nm/RIU, a narrow resonance linewidth (FWHM ≈ 0.096–0.099 nm), and a figure of merit exceeding 18,000 RIU−1. The sensing performance is evaluated for characteristic refractive-index variations associated with separated hemoglobin, platelets, and plasma during dengue infection, yielding pronounced resonance wavelength shifts of + 63.00 nm, − 59.40 nm, and − 23.40 nm, respectively. The physical mechanism governing linear sensitivity is elucidated via first-order electromagnetic perturbation theory, confirming an exceptional linear response (R2 = 0.9996) across the refractive index range n = 1.330–1.400. In addition, blood fractionation procedures, optical dispersion near 2.0 μm, fabrication tolerances, temperature sensitivity, and integration into multi-channel optofluidic architectures are comprehensively analyzed. The proposed slotted photonic crystal platform provides a robust computational foundation for label-free optical monitoring of hematological variations...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a high-performance biosensor for label-free detection of dengue-associated physiological alterations through refractive-index monitoring of separated blood constituents.
Design and population
A numerical simulation study was conducted to evaluate the sensing performance of a 2D photonic crystal slotted cavity biosensor for characteristic refractive-index variations associated with separated hemoglobin, platelets, and plasma during dengue infection.
Core methods
Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) simulations were used to analyze the biosensor's performance, including its sensitivity, quality factor, and resonance linewidth, while first-order electromagnetic perturbation theory was employed to elucidate the physical mechanism governing linear sensitivity.
Main results
The biosensor demonstrated a sensitivity of 1800 nm/RIU, a narrow resonance linewidth (FWHM ≈ 0.096–0.099 nm), and a figure of merit exceeding 18,000 RIU−1. Pronounced resonance wavelength shifts of +63.00 nm, −59.40 nm, and −23.40 nm were observed for hemoglobin, platelets, and plasma, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides a robust computational foundation for label-free optical monitoring of hematological variations associated with dengue pathology, highlighting the biosensor's exceptional sensitivity and linear response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a potential tool for non-invasive, real-time monitoring of dengue-associated blood alterations, which could enhance diagnostic accuracy and support early intervention strategies in dengue management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to numerical simulations and does not include experimental validation, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on computational models rather than empirical data.

登革病毒 NS5 甲基转移酶的比较结构和生物物理分析将 DENV-4 确定为优先 抗病毒药物 靶标

Comparative Structural and Biophysical Profiling of Dengue Virus NS5 Methyltransferase Identifies DENV-4 as a Prioritized Antiviral Target
作者： Afaf Salim Alwabli, Ghadeer Fareed Bukhari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于当前 抗病毒药物 疗法的局限性以及 NS5 在病毒复制和免疫逃避中的重要性,本研究旨在通过检查 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型中 NS5 甲基转移酶 (MTase) 结构域的结构和生物物理特性,将 DENV-4 确定为 抗病毒药物 的优先靶点。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 DENV-1、DENV-2、DENV-3 和 DENV-4 中 NS5 MTase 结构域的比较结构和生物物理分析。使用为 DENV-1-4 核酸检测标准建立的参考菌株从 NCBI 数据库检索这些血清型的全长 基因组 序列。
核心方法
使用蛋白质-蛋白质界面预测算法(例如 InterProSurf 和 SPIDER)对 NS5 MTase 模型进行分析,以预测 Fab-抗原界面残基。此外,生物物理描述符还用于通过 AC-SINS、PSR 和 META 等测定来估计自关联、非特异性相互作用和复合稳定性/可靠性。
主要结果
NS5 N 端 MTase 区域的长度在不同血清型之间略有差异,其中 DENV-1 和 DENV-3 有 262 个氨基酸,DENV-2 和 DENV-4 有 263 个氨基酸。预测残基分为直接接触、表面、近界面和双(链-链)Fab 界面,界面得分表明预测参与的程度。
解释与创新
这些图说明了 NS5 MTase 在 DENV 血清型中的整体保守性,以及可用于有针对性的 抗病毒药物 策略的独特表面和可开发特性,突出了 DENV-4 作为优先目标。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 NS5 MTase 结构域的结构和生物物理特征的深入了解,可以为血清型特异性 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供信息,并通过针对病毒的保守但可修改的区域来改进 疫苗 设计。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于基于序列和结构的模型,这些模型可能无法完全捕捉动态相互作用或功能后果。证据是中等的,因为它基于预测模型,并且不包括 抗病毒药物 靶向的直接实验验证。

Comparative Structural and Biophysical Profiling of Dengue Virus NS5 Methyltransferase Identifies DENV-4 as a Prioritized Antiviral Target

Authors: Afaf Salim Alwabli, Ghadeer Fareed Bukhari
Original abstract (English)
Approximately 390 million infections occur each year, of which about one-quarter result in clinical manifestations (Bhatt et al., 2013), and roughly half of the world's population now lives in at-risk areas. To explain these features, we carried out a comparative structural and biophysical study of the NS5 MTase domain across all four DENV serotypes, building on our recent serotype-resolved analyses of the DENV-1 (Bukhari, 2026b), DENV-2 (Bukhari, 2026a), DENV-3 (Alwabli, 2026b) and DENV-4 (Alwabli, 2026a) NS5 MTase domains. MATERIALS AND METHODS Sequence Retrieval and DENV Serotype Genomes Full-length genome sequences for DENV-1, DENV-2, DENV-3 and DENV-4 were retrieved from the National Center for Biotechnology Information (NCBI) database, using the reference strains established for DENV-1-4 nucleic-acid testing standards (Nunez et al., 2016). 0% READ FULL TEXT INTRODUCTION Dengue is among the most common arthropod-borne viral diseases of humans. This risk is amplified by rapid urbanization, global travel and the expansion of Aedes mosquitoes driven by climate change (eClinicalMedicine, 2024; Lim et al., 2025; Yousuf et al., 2024). No effective antiviral therapy is currently available, and existing vaccines show limited efficacy and serotype-dependent safety concerns. Such limitations underscore the importance of validated viral targets that can support both broad protection and serotype-specific intervention (Goh et al., 2024; Lim et al., 2015). The Dengue Viruses (DENV-1-4) are positive-sense RNA Flaviviruses with a genome of ~10.7 kb encoding a single polyprotein that is processed into three structural (C, prM/M, E) and Seven Non-Structural (NS1-NS5) proteins (El Sahili and Lescar, 2017; Murugesan and Manoharan, 2020). Of these, NS5 is the largest and most conserv...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify DENV-4 as a prioritized antiviral target by examining the structural and biophysical properties of the NS5 methyltransferase (MTase) domain across Dengue Virus (DENV) serotypes, given the limitations of current antiviral therapies and the importance of NS5 in viral replication and immune evasion.
Design and population
The study involved comparative structural and biophysical profiling of the NS5 MTase domain across DENV-1, DENV-2, DENV-3, and DENV-4. Full-length genome sequences for these serotypes were retrieved from the NCBI database using reference strains established for DENV-1-4 nucleic-acid testing standards.
Core methods
The NS5 MTase models were analyzed using protein-protein interface-prediction algorithms such as InterProSurf and SPPIDER to predict Fab-antigen interface residues. Additionally, biophysical descriptors were used to estimate self-association, non-specific interactions, and composite stability/liability through assays like AC-SINS, PSR, and META.
Main results
The N-terminal MTase region of NS5 varied slightly in length among serotypes, with DENV-1 and DENV-3 having 262 amino acids and DENV-2 and DENV-4 having 263. Predicted residues were categorized into direct contact, surface, near-interface, and double (chain-chain) Fab interface, with interface scores indicating the level of predicted involvement.
Interpretation and novelty
The figures illustrate the overall conservation of the NS5 MTase across DENV serotypes, alongside unique surface and developability characteristics that can be leveraged for targeted antiviral strategies, highlighting DENV-4 as a prioritized target.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the structural and biophysical features of the NS5 MTase domain that could inform the development of serotype-specific antiviral therapies and improve vaccine design by targeting conserved yet modifiable regions of the virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on sequence-based and structural models, which may not fully capture dynamic interactions or functional consequences. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on predictive models and does not include direct experimental validation of antiviral targeting.

通过在Gunung Kidul县Baleharjo乡Wukirsari村开展卵形诱捕器制作教育来预防登革热

Upaya Pencegahan Demam Berdarah Dengue Melalui Edukasi Pembuatan Ovitrap di Padukuhan Wukirsari, Desa Baleharjo, Kabupaten Gunung Kidul
作者： Handika Immanuel, IGNASIUS WIDYA PARAHITA PUTRANTO, Cicilia Muis, F. Edit Andre Kumbara, Fransiska Natali Sihombing, Antin Anggraiva, Michael Raharja Gani, Enade Perdana Istyastono, Florentinus Dika Octa Riswanto
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过实施关于使用产卵器控制埃及伊蚊种群的教育计划,解决印度尼西亚持续存在的 登革病毒 (DBD) 公共卫生问题,特别是在人口密度高和卫生条件差的地区。
设计与对象
该研究在 Padukuhan Wukirsari、Desa Baleharjo 和 Kabupaten Gunung Kidul 进行,涉及描述性非实验设计以及测试前和测试后评估。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用教育材料,如测试前和测试后问卷、互动讲座、演示和视频演示,向社区进行有关 DBD 和诱产蛋器使用的教育。
主要结果
结果显示,社区理解程度从测试前的平均分 52.18 分显着提高到测试后的 66.15 分,表明了教育方法的有效性。
解释与创新
该研究将研究结果解释为证明了基于社区的教育计划在提高对 DBD 和产卵器使用的认识和理解方面的有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,此类教育计划可以有效提高社区意识,并有可能减少 DBD 的 发病率。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括非实验设计和缺乏对照组,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Upaya Pencegahan Demam Berdarah Dengue Melalui Edukasi Pembuatan Ovitrap di Padukuhan Wukirsari, Desa Baleharjo, Kabupaten Gunung Kidul

Authors: Handika Immanuel, IGNASIUS WIDYA PARAHITA PUTRANTO, Cicilia Muis, F. Edit Andre Kumbara, Fransiska Natali Sihombing, Antin Anggraiva, Michael Raharja Gani, Enade Perdana Istyastono, Florentinus Dika Octa Riswanto
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) merupakan salah satu penyakit infeksi yang masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat di Indonesia dan negara tropis lainnya. Kepadatan penduduk, rendahnya kesadaran masyarakat terhadap kebersihan lingkungan, serta kondisi sanitasi yang kurang memadai menjadi faktor pendukung berkembangnya habitat nyamuk Aedes aegypti, khususnya di Kabupaten Gunung Kidul. Program pengabdian masyarakat dalam bentuk pelatihan pembuatan dan penggunaan ovitrap merupakan langkah strategis dan relevan. Kegiatan ini diharapkan dapat menumbuhkan kesadaran serta partisipasi aktif masyarakat dalam pencegahan DBD secara berkelanjutan. Pelaksanaan kegiatan pengabdian kepada masyarakat ini dilaksanakan dengan pendekatan observasional dengan rancangan deskriptif non eksperimental. Penelitian ini melibatkan alat ukur berupa kuisioner pre-test dan pos-test yang diberikan sebelum dan sesudah pelaksanaan edukasi. Materi edukasi yang diberikan adalah seputar kejadian DBD dan juga pembuatan ovitrap ditunjang dengan video edukasi yang dikembangkan. Pemaparan materi dan pelatihan dilakukan dalam koordinasi dengan UPT Puskesmas Wonosari II, Gunungkidul, DIY. Hasil pre-test dan post-test dievaluasi secara statistik dengan menggunakan uji T berpasangan. Perangkat lunak yang digunakan adalah R versi 4.4.1 dan RStudio 2024.04.2 Build 764. Hasil menunjukkan rata-rata skor pemahaman masyarakat meningkat dari sekitar 52,18 pada pre-test menjadi 66,15 pada post-test, menunjukkan peningkatan sebesar 13,97 poin. Peningkatan nilai tersebut menggambarkan efektivitas metode penyuluhan yang diterapkan oleh tim pengabdi, yang memadukan ceramah interaktif, demonstrasi langsung, dan penayangan video edukasi dalam menjelaskan konsep DBD serta praktik pembuatan ovitrap. Kegiatan pengabdian kepada masyarakat dalam upaya pencegahan DBD telah berhasil dilakukan melalui edukasi pembuatan ovitrap di Padukuhan Wukirsari, Baleharjo, Wonosari, Gunungkidul, DIY. Kegiatan ini juga berhasil meningkatkan pemahaman masyarakat setelah pemberian edukasi.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the ongoing public health issue of Dengue virus (DBD) in Indonesia, particularly in areas with high population density and poor sanitation, by implementing educational programs on the use of ovitrap to control the Aedes aegypti mosquito population.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Padukuhan Wukirsari, Desa Baleharjo, Kabupaten Gunung Kidul, involving a descriptive non-experimental design with pre-test and post-test evaluations.
Core methods
The methods included the use of educational materials such as pre-test and post-test questionnaires, interactive lectures, demonstrations, and video presentations to educate the community on DBD and the use of ovitrap.
Main results
The results showed a significant increase in community understanding from a pre-test average score of 52.18 to 66.15 on the post-test, indicating the effectiveness of the educational approach.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of a community-based educational program in enhancing awareness and understanding of DBD and ovitrap usage.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that such educational programs can be effective in improving community awareness and potentially reducing the incidence of DBD.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the non-experimental design and the lack of a control group, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

Pematangsiantar Vita Insani 医院住院患者家庭知识水平与登革出血热 发病率 的关系

The Relationship Between the Level of Family Knowledge of Inpatients and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever at Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar
作者： Lidewanty Haloho, Riska Wani Eka Putri Perangin-angin, Norong Perangin-angin
摘要中文翻译登革出血热是由登革热病毒引起的传染病,通过蚊子传播。如果不立即治疗,可能会很危险,因为它可能导致死亡。Vita Insani Hospital Pematangsiantar 的登革出血热 发病率 还是比较高。从医院获得的数据显示,2月至4月,登革出血热发病率病例达到490例。本研究旨在确定 Pematangsiantar Vita Insani 医院住院患者家属的知识水平与登革出血热 发病率 之间的关系。本研究采用相关方法和横截面方法,并采用有目的抽样技术的抽样技术。本研究的样本由 85 名受访者组成。使用的研究工具是根据 Lahagu 等人,2021 年杂志中(印度尼西亚卫生部,2018 年)开发的调查问卷。数据分析技术使用单变量和双变量分析。使用卡方检验的统计研究结果显示,Pematangsiantar Vita Insani 医院住院患者家属的登革热出血热知识水平与 发病率 之间存在相关性,p = 0.003 (p > 0.05)。结果显示,家庭知识水平以51人(60%)为主,约有55人(64%)曾经历过登革出血热。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定 Pematangsiantar Vita Insani 医院住院患者家属的知识水平与登革出血热 发病率 之间的关系。登革出血热是由登革热病毒引起的传染病,通过蚊子传播。
设计与对象
本研究采用横截面方法的相关方法和有目的抽样的抽样技术。本研究的样本由 85 名受访者组成。
核心方法
使用的研究工具是根据 Lahagu 等人,2021 年杂志中(印度尼西亚卫生部,2018 年)开发的调查问卷。数据分析技术使用单变量和双变量分析。
主要结果
使用卡方检验的统计研究结果显示,Pematangsiantar Vita Insani 医院住院患者家属的登革热出血热知识水平与 发病率 之间存在相关性,p = 0.003 (p > 0.05)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明家庭知识水平与登革出血热 发病率 之间的关系,这是在医院家庭意识项目背景下的一项新颖观察。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定有针对性的家庭教育计划提供信息,以减少医院环境中登革出血热的 发病率。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到横截面设计和小样本量的限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

The Relationship Between the Level of Family Knowledge of Inpatients and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever at Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar

Authors: Lidewanty Haloho, Riska Wani Eka Putri Perangin-angin, Norong Perangin-angin
Original abstract (English)
Dengue hemorrhagic fever is an infectious disease caused by the dengue fever virus which is transmitted by mosquitoes. It can be dangerous if not treated immediately because it can cause death. The incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever at Vita Insani Hospital Pematangsiantar is still relatively high. Data obtained from hospitals from February – April, the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever reached 490 cases. This study aims to determine the relationship between the level of knowledge of inpatients' families and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever at the Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar. This research uses a correlational method with a cross- sectional approach with a sampling technique using purposive sampling technique. The sample in this study consisted of 85 respondents. The research instrument used was a questionnaire developed from (Indonesian Ministry of Health, 2018) in the journal Lahagu et al., 2021. Data analysis techniques use univariate and bivariate analysis. Statistical research results using the chi-square test showed that there was a relationship between the level of knowledge of inpatients' families and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever at Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar with a value of p = 0.003 (p > 0.05). And the results show that the level of family knowledge is dominated by 51 people (60%) and around 55 people (64%) have experienced dengue hemorrhagic fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to determine the relationship between the level of knowledge of inpatients' families and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever at Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar. Dengue hemorrhagic fever is an infectious disease caused by the dengue fever virus which is transmitted by mosquitoes.
Design and population
This research uses a correlational method with a cross-sectional approach and a sampling technique using purposive sampling. The sample in this study consisted of 85 respondents.
Core methods
The research instrument used was a questionnaire developed from (Indonesian Ministry of Health, 2018) in the journal Lahagu et al., 2021. Data analysis techniques use univariate and bivariate analysis.
Main results
Statistical research results using the chi-square test showed that there was a relationship between the level of knowledge of inpatients' families and the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever at Vita Insani Hospital, Pematangsiantar with a value of p = 0.003 (p > 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a relationship between family knowledge levels and dengue hemorrhagic fever incidence, which is a novel observation in the context of hospital-based family awareness programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted family education programs to reduce the incidence of dengue hemorrhagic fever in hospital settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its cross-sectional design and small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate.

预防登革热

Prevenção da dengue em jogo
作者： João Pedro Marchesi Tavares, Sofia Delatorre Fonseca, Bruna da Costa Ribeiro, Samuel Salvi Romero
摘要中文翻译预防登革热需要公众参与,以识别和消除有利于埃及伊蚊、龙卷风繁殖的人群,并开展与虫媒病毒环境相关策略的教育。回顾游戏,为互动游戏提供信息,支持公众参与,并与预防措施和实践相关的关系。旨在预防登革热的教育活动和预防登革热的教育活动,以及在“联合大学”活动期间与医学基础相关的活动,将于 2026 年 11 月在潘帕联邦大学实现(UNIPAMPA),乌拉圭校园。作为 Grupo 5 活动的一部分 – 教育计划的通信和创新 – Trabalho para a Saúde (PET-Saúde) – Clima。乌拉圭基础教育和中小学教育的最终参与者,在长期的时间内分布着多种形式,并且与参与者总数保持一致。为了采取行动,利用“登革热多米诺骨牌”,建立与传播疾病相关的关系和预防疾病的相关联,以预防为主。在长此以往,参与者将确认为回复所呈现情况的通讯员并估计了合理的学习机会,可以就先前的情况进行讨论并进一步讨论。论坛有关各种情况的相关情况,包括水的展示和 com pneus、garrafas、calhas、caixas-d’água、vasos de plantas 和 quintais、além da importantância da hidratação 和参与社区的活动登革热。观察蚊子传播者识别的困难:学生们响应 apenas“蚊子”或 escolhiam uma resposta ao acaso。尽管遇到了一些困难,但总体来说,还是要了解有关游戏的便利性。作为 pontuais 论坛的贡献,包括与登革热相关的 pessoas próximas 和 garrafas 的演示,以 quintais 为例。一种可能的预防措施,包括具体情况和评估,以及与无法控制埃及伊蚊的责任的核对。在活动期间,协会有利于参与和可能的对话时刻,允许在个人和集体关系的讨论中发挥作用。结论是,利用预防登革热的疫苗策略和推广措施有利于预防登革热的发生,预防实践中的相关内容不会对民众产生任何影响。

预防登革热需要公众参与,以识别和消除有利于埃及伊蚊、龙卷风繁殖的人群,并开展与虫媒病毒环境相关策略的教育。

旨在预防登革热的教育活动和预防登革热的教育活动,以及在“联合大学”活动期间与医学基础相关的活动,将于 2026 年 11 月在潘帕联邦大学实现(UNIPAMPA),乌拉圭校园。
核心方法
为了采取行动,利用“登革热多米诺骨牌”,建立与传播疾病相关的关系和预防疾病的相关联,以预防为主。
主要结果
观察蚊子传播者识别的困难:学生们响应 apenas“蚊子”或 escolhiam uma resposta ao acaso。尽管遇到了一些困难,但总体来说,还是要了解有关游戏的便利性。
解释与创新
在活动期间,协会有利于参与和可能的对话时刻,允许在个人和集体关系的讨论中发挥作用。

作为 Grupo 5 活动的一部分 – 教育计划的通信和创新 – Trabalho para a Saúde (PET-Saúde) – Clima。

Prevenção da dengue em jogo

Authors: João Pedro Marchesi Tavares, Sofia Delatorre Fonseca, Bruna da Costa Ribeiro, Samuel Salvi Romero
Original abstract (English)
A prevenção da dengue requer a participação da população na identificação e eliminação de situações que favorecem a reprodução do Aedes aegypti, tornando a educação em saúde um componente relevante das estratégias de enfrentamento das arboviroses. Recursos lúdicos podem contribuir para abordar essas informações de maneira interativa, favorecendo a participação do público e a relação entre conhecimentos sobre a doença e práticas cotidianas de prevenção. O objetivo deste trabalho é relatar uma experiência educativa voltada à prevenção da dengue, desenvolvida por meio de uma dinâmica baseada em jogo de associação durante o evento “Conhecendo a UNIPAMPA”, realizado em 11 de setembro de 2026, na Universidade Federal do Pampa (UNIPAMPA), Campus Uruguaiana. A ação integrou as atividades do Grupo 5 – Comunicação e Inovação do Programa de Educação pelo Trabalho para a Saúde (PET-Saúde) – Clima. Participaram estudantes dos anos finais do ensino fundamental e do ensino médio de escolas de Uruguaiana, distribuídos em diversas turmas ao longo do dia, sem contabilização do número total de participantes. Para a atividade, utilizou-se o “Dominó da Dengue”, composto por peças que estabelecem associações entre perguntas e respostas relacionadas à transmissão da doença, ao reconhecimento de sinais e sintomas e, principalmente, às medidas de prevenção. Ao longo da dinâmica, os participantes identificavam as respostas correspondentes às situações apresentadas e eram estimulados a justificar suas escolhas, possibilitando a discussão de conhecimentos prévios e o esclarecimento de dúvidas. Foram abordadas situações do cotidiano relacionadas à presença de água parada e aos cuidados com pneus, garrafas, calhas, caixas-d’água, vasos de plantas e quintais, além da importância da hidratação e da participação comunitária no enfrentamento da dengue. Observou-se dificuldade recorrente na identificação do mosquito transmissor: alguns estudantes respondiam apenas “mosquito” ou escolhiam uma resposta ao acaso. Apesar dessa dificuldade, de modo geral, os alunos conseguiam concluir o jogo com relativa facilidade. As contribuições espontâneas foram pontuais e incluíram relatos de pessoas próximas que tiveram dengue e da presença de garrafas com água parada em quintais. A experiência possibilitou trabalhar medidas preventivas a partir de situações concretas e estimular o reconhecimento da responsabilidade compartilhada no controle do Aedes aegypti. A dinâmica de associação favoreceu a participação e possibilitou momentos de diálogo durante a atividade, permitindo que o jogo funcionasse como mediador da discussão sobre comportamentos individuais e coletivos relacionados à prevenção. Conclui-se que a utilização de estratégias lúdicas em ações extensionistas pode favorecer a abordagem participativa da dengue, aproximando conhecimentos em saúde de práticas preventivas presentes no cotidiano da população.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
A prevenção da dengue requer a participação da população na identificação e eliminação de situações que favorecem a reprodução do Aedes aegypti, tornando a educação em saúde um componente relevante das estratégias de enfrentamento das arboviroses.
Design and population
O objetivo deste trabalho é relatar uma experiência educativa voltada à prevenção da dengue, desenvolvida por meio de uma dinâmica baseada em jogo de associação durante o evento “Conhecendo a UNIPAMPA”, realizado em 11 de setembro de 2026, na Universidade Federal do Pampa (UNIPAMPA), Campus Uruguaiana.
Core methods
Para a atividade, utilizou-se o “Dominó da Dengue”, composto por peças que estabelecem associações entre perguntas e respostas relacionadas à transmissão da doença, ao reconhecimento de sinais e sintomas e, principalmente, às medidas de prevenção.
Main results
Observou-se dificuldade recorrente na identificação do mosquito transmissor: alguns estudantes respondiam apenas “mosquito” ou escolhiam uma resposta ao acaso. Apesar dessa dificuldade, de modo geral, os alunos conseguiam concluir o jogo com relativa facilidade.
Interpretation and novelty
A dinâmica de associação favoreceu a participação e possibilitou momentos de diálogo durante a atividade, permitindo que o jogo funcionasse como mediador da discussão sobre comportamentos individuais e coletivos relacionados à prevenção.
Scientific and public-health significance
A experiência possibilitou trabalhar medidas preventivas a partir de situações concretas e estimular o reconhecimento da responsabilidade compartilhada no controle do Aedes aegypti.
Limitations and evidence strength
A ação integrou as atividades do Grupo 5 – Comunicação e Inovação do Programa de Educação pelo Trabalho para a Saúde (PET-Saúde) – Clima.

弥合登革热中的 监测 差距:集成混合数据源的分层模型,用于 传播 估计和 疫苗 定位

Bridging surveillance gaps in dengue: a hierarchical model integrating mixed data sources for transmission estimation and vaccine targeting
作者： Djaafara, B. A., Elyazar, I. R., Yosephine, P., Surya, A., Silalahi, F. S., Handito, A., Thohir, B., Aryani, D., Gunawan, D., Nisa, A. K.
摘要中文翻译估计登革热感染力 (FOI) 对于了解 传播 动态和制定干预计划至关重要,但估计所需的流行环境中的 监测 数据通常不完整,且格式各异。我们开发了一个贝叶斯分层催化模型,该模型在单一框架内联合拟合年龄分层病例数据、汇总病例数据和 血清流行率 调查,并结合外部协变量以提高参数可识别性。综合验证表明,即使大多数地区仅提供汇总数据,仅协变量也能恢复准确的 FOI 点估计,但这样做的不确定性校准不佳;有必要通过一次 血清流行率 调查来锚定模型,以使可信的区间覆盖范围接近名义值。该模型应用于印度尼西亚爪哇岛和巴厘岛的 128 个地区(2016-2024 年),揭示了 FOI 和报告率的显着空间异质性。爪哇的许多地区超出了世界卫生组织建议的 疫苗 引入阈值,但在使用报告的 发病率 作为优先标准时被归类为低优先级,特别是在 监测 较弱的地区。基于模型的 血清流行率 估计集成了多个数据源,为识别 疫苗 引入的高优先级地区提供了更一致的基础,并且比报告的 发病率 更不易受到 监测 偏差的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
提供的证据没有明确说明研究问题或其基本原理。
设计与对象
我们开发了一个贝叶斯分层催化模型,该模型在单一框架内联合拟合年龄分层病例数据、汇总病例数据和 血清流行率 调查,并结合外部协变量以提高参数可识别性。
核心方法
估计登革热感染力 (FOI) 对于了解 传播 动态和制定干预计划至关重要,但估计所需的流行环境中的 监测 数据通常不完整,且格式各异。
主要结果
该模型应用于印度尼西亚爪哇岛和巴厘岛的 128 个地区(2016-2024 年),揭示了 FOI 和报告率的显着空间异质性。
解释与创新
爪哇的许多地区超出了世界卫生组织建议的 疫苗 引入阈值,但在使用报告的 发病率 作为优先标准时被归类为低优先级,特别是在 监测 较弱的地区。
科研与公卫意义
基于模型的 血清流行率 估计集成了多个数据源,为识别 疫苗 引入的高优先级地区提供了更一致的基础,并且比报告的 发病率 更不易受到 监测 偏差的影响。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Bridging surveillance gaps in dengue: a hierarchical model integrating mixed data sources for transmission estimation and vaccine targeting

Authors: Djaafara, B. A., Elyazar, I. R., Yosephine, P., Surya, A., Silalahi, F. S., Handito, A., Thohir, B., Aryani, D., Gunawan, D., Nisa, A. K.
Original abstract (English)
Estimating dengue force of infection (FOI) is essential for understanding transmission dynamics and targeting intervention programmes, yet surveillance data in endemic settings required for estimations are often incomplete, with varying formats. We developed a Bayesian hierarchical catalytic model that jointly fits age-stratified case data, aggregate case data, and seroprevalence surveys within a single framework, incorporating external covariates to improve parameter identifiability. Synthetic validation showed that covariates alone recovered accurate FOI point estimates even when most districts contributed only aggregate data, but did so with poorly calibrated uncertainty; anchoring the model with a single seroprevalence survey was necessary to bring credible interval coverage close to nominal. Applied to 128 districts across Java and Bali, Indonesia (2016-2024), the model revealed substantial spatial heterogeneity in FOI and reporting rates. Many districts in Java exceeded the WHO-suggested seroprevalence threshold for vaccine introduction, yet were classified as low-priority when using reported incidence as prioritisation criterion, particularly in areas with weak surveillance. Model-based seroprevalence estimation, integrating multiple data sources, offers a more consistent basis for identifying high-priority districts for vaccine introduction, and is less susceptible to surveillance bias than reported incidence.

Question and background

Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Applied to 128 districts across Java and Bali, Indonesia (2016-2024), the model revealed substantial spatial heterogeneity in FOI and reporting rates.
Interpretation and novelty
Many districts in Java exceeded the WHO-suggested seroprevalence threshold for vaccine introduction, yet were classified as low-priority when using reported incidence as prioritisation criterion, particularly in areas with weak surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

1995 年至 2025 年旅行者中的致命登革热病例:有针对性的回顾。

Fatal dengue cases in travellers from 1995 to 2025: A targeted review.
作者： Carolin Stephanie Dinjer, Martin P Grobusch, Marta Díaz-Menéndez, Jonas Schmidt-Chanasit, Tino F. Schwarz, Ralph M. H. G. Huits, Callum S Bainbridge, Caroline Mächler, Robert J. Steffen, Marta Díaz‐Menéndez
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,在 136 个国家和地区流行,仅 2024 年全球报告病例就超过 1,400 万例,死亡人数超过 10,000 人。国际旅行者在前往流行地区时面临感染风险。虽然旅行者中的登革热已得到充分描述,但该人群中与登革热相关的 死亡率 尚未得到全面综合。我们整理并严格评估了 30 年来国际旅行者中致命登革热病例报告的现有数据。方法:进行了一项有针对性的审查,以确定 1995 年至 2025 年 11 月期间来自非流行国家的旅行者在流行地区感染登革热相关的死亡情况。使用与登革热、死亡率 和旅行相关的术语进行 PubMed 搜索,并辅以手动搜索会议记录、国家和国际 监测 报告、可公开访问的国家传染病数据库、媒体报告和轶事通知。根据诊断方法和/或 监测 系统中的包含情况,将病例分类为确诊病例或疑似病例。提取数据,交叉引用以避免重复,并进行描述性分析。结果:确定了 67 例与旅行相关的致命登革热病例,其中 55 例被归类为确诊/疑似病例,12 例信息有限。在 55 例确诊/疑似病例中,有 22 例报告了居住国和感染源,跨越 14 个流行目的地。确诊/可能死亡病例发生在年龄<1-79岁的患者中;25.5% 为女性,21.8% 为男性,52.7% 的人没有记录性别。在可获得感染状态的情况下,大多数是原发感染 (9/16)。报告的致命症状包括神经、心脏、出血和高炎症并发症。诊断和报告异质性很大,监测 差距也很明显。结论:旅行者中与登革热相关的 死亡率 很少见,但可能未被充分认识和报告。加强诊断能力和提高临床意识可能有助于更早识别和管理旅行者中的重症病例,而统一的国际报告系统可以改善病例查明并有助于未来的预防战略。

范围与问题
Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease endemic in 136 countries and territories, with over 14 million cases and more than 10,000 deaths reported globally in 2024 alone. International travellers are at risk of infection when visiting endemic regions. While dengue in travellers is well described, dengue-related mortality in this population has not been comprehensively synthesised.
证据基础与方法
进行了一项有针对性的审查,以确定 1995 年至 2025 年 11 月期间来自非流行国家的旅行者在流行地区感染登革热相关的死亡情况。使用与登革热、死亡率 和旅行相关的术语进行的 PubMed 搜索得到了手动搜索会议记录、国家和国际 监测 报告、可公开访问的国家传染病数据库、媒体报道和轶事通知的补充。提取数据,交叉引用以避免重复,并进行描述性分析。
共识与结论
已确定 67 例与旅行相关的致命登革热病例,其中 55 例被归类为确诊/疑似病例,12 例信息有限。在 55 例确诊/疑似病例中,有 22 例报告了居住国和感染源,跨越 14 个流行目的地。确诊/可能死亡病例发生在年龄<1-79岁的患者中;25.5% 为女性,21.8% 为男性,52.7% 的人没有记录性别。在可获得感染状态的情况下,大多数是原发感染 (9/16)。报告的致命症状包括神经、心脏、出血和高炎症并发症。旅行者中与登革热相关的 死亡率 很少见,但可能未被充分认识和报告。
争议与缺口
诊断和报告异质性很大,监测 差距也很明显。
科研与实践启示
加强诊断能力和提高临床意识可能有助于更早识别和管理旅行者中的重症病例,而统一的国际报告系统可以改善病例查明并有助于未来的预防战略。

Fatal dengue cases in travellers from 1995 to 2025: A targeted review.

Authors: Carolin Stephanie Dinjer, Martin P Grobusch, Marta Díaz-Menéndez, Jonas Schmidt-Chanasit, Tino F. Schwarz, Ralph M. H. G. Huits, Callum S Bainbridge, Caroline Mächler, Robert J. Steffen, Marta Díaz‐Menéndez
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue is a mosquito-borne viral disease endemic in 136 countries and territories, with over 14 million cases and more than 10,000 deaths reported globally in 2024 alone. International travellers are at risk of infection when visiting endemic regions. While dengue in travellers is well described, dengue-related mortality in this population has not been comprehensively synthesised. We compiled and critically assessed available data on reports of fatal dengue cases among international travellers over a 30-year period. METHODS: A targeted review was conducted to identify dengue-related fatalities in travellers from non-endemic countries acquiring infection in endemic regions between 1995 and November 2025. PubMed searches using terms related to dengue, mortality, and travel were complemented by hand-searching conference proceedings, national and international surveillance reports, publicly accessible national infectious disease databases, media reports, and anecdotal notifications. Cases were classified as confirmed or probable based on diagnostic method and/or inclusion in surveillance systems. Data were extracted, cross-referenced to avoid duplication, and analysed descriptively. RESULTS: Sixty-seven fatal travel-related dengue cases were identified, of which 55 were classified as confirmed/probable and 12 cases had limited information. Of the 55 confirmed/probable cases, 22 reported both country of residence and acquisition, spanning 14 endemic destinations. Confirmed/probable fatal cases occurred among patients aged <1-79 years; 25.5% were female, 21.8% were male, and in 52.7% sex was not documented. Where infection status was available, most were primary infections (9/16). Reported fatal manifestations included neurological, cardiac, haemorrhagic, and hyperinflammatory complications. Diagnostic and reporting heterogeneity was substantial, and surveillance gaps were evident. CONCLUSIONS: Dengue-related mortality among travellers is rare but likely under-recognised and underreported. Strengthened diagnostic capacity and heightened clinical awareness may support earlier recognition and management of severe cases in travellers, while harmonised international reporting systems could improve case ascertainment and contribute to future prevention strategies.

International travellers are at risk of infection when visiting endemic regions. Data were extracted, cross-referenced to avoid duplication, and analysed descriptively.

Of the 55 confirmed/probable cases, 22 reported both country of residence and acquisition, spanning 14 endemic destinations. Confirmed/probable fatal cases occurred among patients aged <1-79 years; 25.5% were female, 21.8% were male, and in 52.7% sex was not documented. Where infection status was available, most were primary infections (9/16). Reported fatal manifestations included neurological, cardiac, haemorrhagic, and hyperinflammatory complications. 登革热 疫苗 在东南亚的实施:对计划设计、采用和安全性 监测 的范围审查。

Dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia: a scoping review of program design, uptake, and safety surveillance.
作者： Mary Grace L. Enriquez, Lucille M. Villanueva-Uy, Miguel Enrico S. Acuin
摘要中文翻译背景:东南亚地区的登革热疫苗接种计划在目标人群、实施方式、资金来源、资格条件和安全监测方面存在差异,相关证据分散在同行评审文献和灰色文献中。目标:绘制东南亚地区登革热疫苗接种计划实施的证据,重点关注计划设计、接种率和安全性,并识别未来该地区疫苗接种政策、实施框架和临床指南的实用见解。设计:综述研究。地点:东南亚。人群:符合或接受登革热疫苗接种的人群。方法:本综述遵循JBI指南和PRISMA-ScR。我们搜索了PubMed、Scopus、Cochrane、Google Scholar、WPRIM、WHO IRIS、Google以及政府和监管网站,收集了2015年至2026年间关于计划设计、接种率或安全性方面的资料。两名评审员独立筛选记录。证据按国家和概念进行映射,并通过叙述性综合进行总结。结果:数据库搜索于2026年9月8日更新,灰色文献和补充搜索于2026年11月1日进行。共筛选出366条数据库记录和220条灰色文献链接,最终纳入33个来源;补充搜索增加了12个(总计45个)。证据涉及六个国家,而文莱、柬埔寨、老挝、缅甸和东帝汶则没有相关证据。菲律宾实施了一个大规模、公共资助的基于学校的CYD-TDV疫苗接种计划;印度尼西亚则结合了私人渠道与省级TAK-003试点计划;马来西亚、新加坡、泰国和越南主要依赖私人渠道进行疫苗接种。安全性方面的证据在菲律宾、马来西亚和新加坡最为充分。接种率数据较为稀少:在菲律宾超过830,000名儿童中接受CYD-TDV疫苗接种的儿童中,不到一半完成了三剂接种,且该计划在暂停前未完成;印尼试点计划报告了约90%的覆盖率。未发现私人渠道接种率的相关数据。九项来源为建模或经济研究,这些研究有助于规划,但并未记录实施情况。结论:东南亚地区登革热疫苗接种计划在实施方式、资金来源和安全监测方面存在差异,且记录不均衡,尤其是在接种率方面。需要对实际疫苗接种计划进行标准化的监测和报告。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
登革病毒 1-4 和登革热病;其他黄病毒属于近邻排除项。生物相关的动物、分类学、模型、差异-诊断 和比较实体仅是补充,除非还存在材料目标特定证据;只有显式的词汇或不相关的硬排除才是最终的。该审查的重点是登革热 疫苗 在东南亚的实施,目标是计划设计、吸收和安全性 监测。
证据基础与方法
该审查遵循 JBI 指南和 PRISMA-ScR。它搜索了 PubMed、Scopus、Cochrane、Google Scholar、WPRIM、WHO IRIS、Google 以及政府和监管网站,并检查了参考文献列表,查找 2015 年至 2026 年发表的有关计划设计、吸收或安全性 监测 的来源。两名评审员独立筛选记录,并按国家和概念绘制证据并进行叙述性综合。
共识与结论
登革热 疫苗 在东南亚的实施在交付、融资和安全监测方面各不相同,并且记录不均匀,尤其是在采用方面。安全 监测 在菲律宾、马来西亚和新加坡得到了最好的记录。
争议与缺口
吸收数据很少,没有发现私营部门交付的数据。审查确定了九项模型或经济研究,这些研究为规划提供了信息,但没有记录实施情况。需要对现实世界的 疫苗接种 程序进行标准化监控和报告。
科研与实践启示
提供了对该地区未来 疫苗接种 政策、实施框架和临床指南的实用见解。

Dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia: a scoping review of program design, uptake, and safety surveillance.

Authors: Mary Grace L. Enriquez, Lucille M. Villanueva-Uy, Miguel Enrico S. Acuin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue vaccine programs in Southeast Asia differ in target groups, delivery, financing, eligibility, and safety monitoring, and the evidence is scattered across peer-reviewed and grey literature. OBJECTIVES: To map evidence on dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia, focusing on program design, uptake, and safety surveillance, and to identify practical insights for future vaccination policies, implementation frameworks, and clinical guidelines in the region. DESIGN: Scoping review. SETTING: Southeast Asia. Population Populations eligible for or receiving dengue vaccination. METHODS: The review followed JBI guidance and PRISMA-ScR. We searched PubMed, Scopus, Cochrane, Google Scholar, WPRIM, WHO IRIS, Google, and government and regulatory websites, and checked reference lists, for sources published from 2015 to 2026 on program design, uptake, or safety surveillance. Two reviewers screened records independently. Evidence was mapped by country and concept and synthesized narratively. RESULTS: Database searches were last updated on September 8, 2026, and grey-literature and supplementary searches on November 1, 2026. Of 366 database records and 220 grey-literature links, 33 sources were included; a supplementary search added 12 (total 45). Evidence was found for six countries and none for Brunei, Cambodia, Lao PDR, Myanmar, or Timor-Leste. The Philippines ran a large, publicly funded, school-based CYD-TDV program; Indonesia combined private access with a provincial TAK-003 pilot; Malaysia, Singapore, Thailand, and Vietnam relied mainly on private delivery. Safety surveillance was best documented in the Philippines, Malaysia, and Singapore. Uptake data were sparse: fewer than half of the >830,000 Filipino children given CYD-TDV completed three doses before the program was suspended, and the Indonesian pilot reported about 90% coverage. No uptake data were found for private-sector delivery. Nine sources were modeling or economic studies, which inform planning but do not document implementation. CONCLUSION: Dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia varies in delivery, financing, and safety monitoring, and is unevenly documented, especially for uptake. Standardized monitoring and reporting of real-world vaccination programs are needed.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease; other flaviviruses are near-neighbour exclusions. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal. The review focuses on dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia, targeting program design, uptake, and safety surveillance.
Evidence base and method
The review followed JBI guidance and PRISMA-ScR. It searched PubMed, Scopus, Cochrane, Google Scholar, WPRIM, WHO IRIS, Google, and government and regulatory websites, and checked reference lists, for sources published from 2015 to 2026 on program design, uptake, or safety surveillance. Two reviewers screened records independently, and evidence was mapped by country and concept and synthesized narratively.
Consensus and conclusions
Dengue vaccine implementation in Southeast Asia varies in delivery, financing, and safety monitoring, and is unevenly documented, especially for uptake. Safety surveillance was best documented in the Philippines, Malaysia, and Singapore.
Controversies and gaps
Uptake data were sparse, and no data were found for private-sector delivery. The review identified nine modeling or economic studies, which inform planning but do not document implementation. Standardized monitoring and reporting of real-world vaccination programs are needed.
Research and practice implications
Practical insights for future vaccination policies, implementation frameworks, and clinical guidelines in the region are provided.

TAK-003 登革热 疫苗接种 的成人免疫原性和描述性安全性:系统评价和荟萃分析

Adult Immunogenicity and Descriptive Safety of TAK-003 Dengue Vaccination: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis
作者： I Wayan Bagus Abiyoga Suputra, Komang Andjani Putri, ‪Cokorda Agung Wahyu Purnamasidhi
摘要中文翻译背景:成人 TAK-003 数据因配方、基线登革热状态、共同给药和结果定义而异。目的:合成获得许可的 TAK-003 两剂量最终制剂的成人免疫原性和安全性。方法:我们检索了 PubMed、Scopus、CENTRAL 衍生记录、注册、监管来源和最终检索更新的引用。筛选、提取、评估和分析使用可重复的计算工作流程,无需独立的重复验证。使用二项正态 Logit 模型对血清阳性率和 270 天严重不良事件 (SAE) 风险进行建模;比较反应原性使用随机效应风险比和修正的 Hartung-Knapp 区间。确定性按结果分级,敏感性分析添加了单臂 DEN307 队列研究。结果:在 1,090 条记录中,筛选了 606 条独特记录(553 条出版物和 53 条登记记录);排除 473 篇出版物,检索 80 篇。五项试验提供了定量结果。在 620 名基线血清阴性成人中,第 2 剂给药后约 30 天,DENV-1 的汇总血清阳性率为 98.7%(95% CI 95.8-99.6),DENV-2 为 99.0%(97.9-99.6),DENV-3 为 96.9%(95.2-98.0),DENV-4 为 98.4% (97.0–99.1)。添加 DEN307 后,估计值为 98.8%、99.2%、97.1% 和 98.5%(每种血清型 752 名参与者)。在四个 队列研究 中,29/1,468 名参与者在第 270 天出现 SAE(合并风险 2.0%,95% CI 1.4–2.8)。两项研究对疼痛和头痛的估计都非常不精确。结论:最终制剂 TAK-003 在选定的成人试验人群中显示出较高的短期血清阳性率。SAE 风险不受控制,无法建立相对安全性。确定性很低或非常低,并且推断受到研究规模、替代结果和不受控制的安全性数据的限制。

成人 TAK-003 数据因配方、基线登革热状态、共同给药和结果定义而异。合成获得许可的 TAK-003 两剂量最终制剂的成人免疫原性和安全性。
证据基础与方法
我们检索了 PubMed、Scopus、CENTRAL 衍生记录、注册、监管来源和最终检索更新的引用。筛选、提取、评估和分析使用可重复的计算工作流程,无需独立的重复验证。使用二项正态 Logit 模型对血清阳性率和 270 天严重不良事件 (SAE) 风险进行建模;比较反应原性使用随机效应风险比和修正的 Hartung-Knapp 区间。
共识与结论
在 620 名基线血清阴性成人中,第 2 剂给药后约 30 天,DENV-1 的汇总血清阳性率为 98.7%(95% CI 95.8-99.6),DENV-2 为 99.0%(97.9-99.6),DENV-3 为 96.9%(95.2-98.0),DENV-4 为 98.4% (97.0–99.1)。添加 DEN307 后,估计值为 98.8%、99.2%、97.1% 和 98.5%(每种血清型 752 名参与者)。最终制剂 TAK-003 在选定的成人试验人群中显示出较高的短期血清阳性率。
争议与缺口
确定性很低或非常低,并且推断受到研究规模、替代结果和不受控制的安全性数据的限制。在四个 队列研究 中,29/1,468 名参与者在第 270 天出现 SAE(合并风险 2.0%,95% CI 1.4–2.8)。两项研究对疼痛和头痛的估计都非常不精确。
科研与实践启示
SAE 风险不受控制,无法建立相对安全性。

Adult Immunogenicity and Descriptive Safety of TAK-003 Dengue Vaccination: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis

Authors: I Wayan Bagus Abiyoga Suputra, Komang Andjani Putri, ‪Cokorda Agung Wahyu Purnamasidhi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Adult TAK-003 data differ by formulation, baseline dengue status, coadministration, and outcome definition. Objective: To synthesize adult immunogenicity and safety of the licensed two-dose final formulation of TAK-003. Methods: We searched PubMed, Scopus, CENTRAL-derived records, registries, regulatory sources, and citations to the final search update. Screening, extraction, appraisal, and analysis used a reproducible computational workflow without independent duplicate verification. Seropositivity and 270-day serious adverse event (SAE) risk were modeled with binomial-normal logit models; comparative reactogenicity used random-effects risk ratios with modified Hartung-Knapp intervals. Certainty was graded by outcome, and a sensitivity analysis added the single-arm DEN307 cohort. Results: Of 1,090 records, 606 unique records (553 publications and 53 registry records) were screened; 473 publications were excluded and 80 were retrieved. Five trials supplied quantitative outcomes. Among 620 baseline-seronegative adults, pooled seropositivity about 30 days after dose 2 was 98.7% for DENV-1 (95% CI 95.8–99.6), 99.0% for DENV-2 (97.9–99.6), 96.9% for DENV-3 (95.2–98.0), and 98.4% for DENV-4 (97.0–99.1). With DEN307 added, estimates were 98.8%, 99.2%, 97.1%, and 98.5% (752 participants per serotype). Across four cohorts, 29/1,468 participants had an SAE through day 270 (pooled risk 2.0%, 95% CI 1.4–2.8). Pain and headache estimates, each from two studies, were highly imprecise. Conclusion: Final-formulation TAK-003 gave high short-term seropositivity in selected adult trial populations. The SAE risk was uncontrolled and does not establish comparative safety. Certainty was low or very low, and inference is limited by study size, surrogate outcomes, and uncontrolled safety data.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Adult TAK-003 data differ by formulation, baseline dengue status, coadministration, and outcome definition. To synthesize adult immunogenicity and safety of the licensed two-dose final formulation of TAK-003.
Evidence base and method
We searched PubMed, Scopus, CENTRAL-derived records, registries, regulatory sources, and citations to the final search update. Screening, extraction, appraisal, and analysis used a reproducible computational workflow without independent duplicate verification. Seropositivity and 270-day serious adverse event (SAE) risk were modeled with binomial-normal logit models; comparative reactogenicity used random-effects risk ratios with modified Hartung-Knapp intervals.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 620 baseline-seronegative adults, pooled seropositivity about 30 days after dose 2 was 98.7% for DENV-1 (95% CI 95.8–99.6), 99.0% for DENV-2 (97.9–99.6), 96.9% for DENV-3 (95.2–98.0), and 98.4% for DENV-4 (97.0–99.1). With DEN307 added, estimates were 98.8%, 99.2%, 97.1%, and 98.5% (752 participants per serotype). Final-formulation TAK-003 gave high short-term seropositivity in selected adult trial populations.
Controversies and gaps
Certainty was low or very low, and inference is limited by study size, surrogate outcomes, and uncontrolled safety data. Across four cohorts, 29/1,468 participants had an SAE through day 270 (pooled risk 2.0%, 95% CI 1.4–2.8). Pain and headache estimates, each from two studies, were highly imprecise.
Research and practice implications
The SAE risk was uncontrolled and does not establish comparative safety.

AST 与血小板比率指数 (APRI) 对严重或复杂登革热的诊断准确性:系统评价和荟萃分析

Diagnostic accuracy of the AST-to-Platelet Ratio Index (APRI) for severe or complicated dengue: a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Bibek Raj Shrestha, Manish Acharya, Bindira Adhikari, Shreeram Paudel, Sabin Thapaliya
摘要中文翻译背景 本系统回顾和荟萃分析旨在根据纳入研究中使用的目标条件定义,评估 APRI 识别严重或复杂登革热的诊断准确性。单独的敏感性分析仅限于比较严重与非 重症登革热 的研究。该综述还研究了影响研究结果的解释和潜在临床适用性的方法学局限性。目的评价天冬氨酸转氨酶与血小板比值指数对重症或复杂性登革热的诊断准确性。方法 检索了从成立到 2026 年 3 月 13 日的七个电子数据库。符合条件的研究评估了实验室确诊的登革热患者的指数。该审查遵循诊断测试准确性研究的系统审查和荟萃分析的首选报告项目指南。研究水平的敏感性和特异性是根据提取或重建的两两数据计算的。使用通过限制最大似然估计拟合的频率论双变量随机效应 Reitsma 模型获得汇总估计值。评估了异质性、阈值效应和敏感性分析。结果 定性综合中纳入了 8 项研究,其中 6 项研究(948 名参与者)被定量汇总。汇总敏感性为 0.75(95% 置信区间:0.66–0.82),汇总特异性为 0.80(95% 置信区间:0.76–0.84)。阳性似然比为3.76,阴性似然比为0.32,诊断优势比为12.42。汇总受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.841。留一法分析产生了类似的估计。限制四项比较严重与非 重症登革热 的研究得出的敏感性为 0.73,特异性为 0.79,曲线下面积为 0.821。结论 APRI 对严重或复杂登革热的异质目标病症表现出中等的诊断准确性。这些汇总估计值不应被解释为专门适用于严格定义的 重症登革热。目标条件定义的变化和回顾性选择的 APRI 阈值范围广泛限制了临床适用性,而回顾性阈值选择可能会高估诊断性能。在常规使用之前需要进行前瞻性验证以及临床评估和连续实验室监测。

范围与问题
这项系统评价和荟萃分析旨在根据纳入研究中使用的目标条件定义,评估 APRI 识别严重或复杂登革热的诊断准确性。
证据基础与方法
从开始到 2026 年 3 月 13 日,检索了七个电子数据库。审查遵循诊断测试准确性研究的系统审查和元分析的首选报告项目指南。使用通过限制最大似然估计拟合的频率论双变量随机效应 Reitsma 模型获得汇总估计值。评估了异质性、阈值效应和敏感性分析。单独的敏感性分析仅限于比较严重与非 重症登革热 的研究。
共识与结论
定性综合中纳入了 8 项研究,其中 6 项研究(948 名参与者)被定量汇总。汇总敏感性为 0.75(95% 置信区间:0.66–0.82),汇总特异性为 0.80(95% 置信区间:0.76–0.84)。阳性似然比为3.76,阴性似然比为0.32,诊断优势比为12.42。汇总受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.841。留一法分析产生了类似的估计。该综述还研究了影响研究结果的解释和潜在临床适用性的方法学局限性。
争议与缺口
目标条件定义的变化和回顾性选择的 APRI 阈值范围广泛限制了临床适用性,而回顾性阈值选择可能会高估诊断性能。汇总估计值不应被解释为专门适用于严格定义的 重症登革热。
科研与实践启示
在常规使用之前需要进行前瞻性验证以及临床评估和连续实验室监测。

Diagnostic accuracy of the AST-to-Platelet Ratio Index (APRI) for severe or complicated dengue: a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Bibek Raj Shrestha, Manish Acharya, Bindira Adhikari, Shreeram Paudel, Sabin Thapaliya
Original abstract (English)
Background This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of APRI for identifying severe or complicated dengue, according to the target-condition definitions used in the included studies. A separate sensitivity analysis was restricted to studies comparing severe with non-severe dengue. The review also examined methodological limitations affecting the interpretation and potential clinical applicability of the findings. Objective To evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index for identifying severe or complicated dengue. Methods Seven electronic databases were searched from inception to March 13, 2026. Eligible studies evaluated the index among patients with laboratory-confirmed dengue. The review followed the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses of Diagnostic Test Accuracy Studies guidance. Study-level sensitivity and specificity were calculated from extracted or reconstructed two-by-two data. Pooled estimates were obtained using a frequentist bivariate random-effects Reitsma model fitted by restricted maximum likelihood estimation. Heterogeneity, threshold effects and sensitivity analyses were assessed. Results Eight studies were included in the qualitative synthesis, of which six comprising 948 participants were quantitatively pooled. Pooled sensitivity was 0.75 (95% confidence interval: 0.66–0.82), and pooled specificity was 0.80 (95% confidence interval: 0.76–0.84). The positive likelihood ratio was 3.76, the negative likelihood ratio was 0.32 and the diagnostic odds ratio was 12.42. The area under the summary receiver operating characteristic curve was 0.841. Leave-one-out analyses produced similar estimates. Restriction to four studies comparing severe with non-severe dengue yielded a sensitivity of 0.73, specificity of 0.79 and area under the curve of 0.821. Conclusion APRI demonstrated moderate diagnostic accuracy for the heterogeneous target condition of severe or complicated dengue. These pooled estimates should not be interpreted as applying exclusively to strictly defined severe dengue. Variation in target-condition definitions and the wide range of retrospectively selected APRI thresholds limit clinical applicability, while retrospective threshold selection may overestimate diagnostic performance. Prospective validation is required before routine use alongside clinical assessment and serial laboratory monitoring.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of APRI for identifying severe or complicated dengue, according to the target-condition definitions used in the included studies.
Evidence base and method
Seven electronic databases were searched from inception to March 13, 2026. The review followed the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses of Diagnostic Test Accuracy Studies guidance. Pooled estimates were obtained using a frequentist bivariate random-effects Reitsma model fitted by restricted maximum likelihood estimation. Heterogeneity, threshold effects and sensitivity analyses were assessed. A separate sensitivity analysis was restricted to studies comparing severe with non-severe dengue.

Eight studies were included in the qualitative synthesis, of which six comprising 948 participants were quantitatively pooled. Pooled sensitivity was 0.75 (95% confidence interval: 0.66–0.82), and pooled specificity was 0.80 (95% confidence interval: 0.76–0.84). The positive likelihood ratio was 3.76, the negative likelihood ratio was 0.32 and the diagnostic odds ratio was 12.42. The area under the summary receiver operating characteristic curve was 0.841. Leave-one-out analyses produced similar estimates. The review also examined methodological limitations affecting the interpretation and potential clinical applicability of the findings.
Controversies and gaps
Variation in target-condition definitions and the wide range of retrospectively selected APRI thresholds limit clinical applicability, while retrospective threshold selection may overestimate diagnostic performance. The pooled estimates should not be interpreted as applying exclusively to strictly defined severe dengue.
Research and practice implications
Prospective validation is required before routine use alongside clinical assessment and serial laboratory monitoring.

伊朗的 登革热:流行病学、媒介、诊断和公共卫生挑战:全面系统文献综述(2020-2026 年)

Dengue fever in Iran: epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges: a comprehensive systematic literature review (2020–2026)
作者： Hassan Nasirian
摘要中文翻译这项系统文献综述是根据 PRISMA 2020 指南(承认存在偏差)进行的,分析了 2020 年至 2026 年间发表的 46 项同行评审研究。这种定量综合主要用于说明目的,以可视化地理模式;叙述性综合仍然是本次审查的主要贡献。发表偏倚分析(漏斗图不对称性;Egger 检验:p = 0.028)表明低 患病率 区域的代表性可能不足,但这些研究结果并不可靠,研究很少,应极其谨慎地解释。该审查确定了 监测 基础设施、分子诊断能力和医疗保健系统准备方面的关键差距。数据可用性 当前研究期间没有生成或分析数据集。https://doi.org/10.2139/ssrn.5868061 - Abbasi E (2025b) 气候变化对半干旱地区埃及伊蚊分布和 登革热 患病率 的影响:伊朗德黑兰省的案例研究。https://doi.org/10.1016/j.envres.2025.121441 - Abbasi E,Moemenbellah-Fard MD (2025) 患病率 基于酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 的伊朗基孔肯雅热、登革热和西尼罗河虫媒病毒:系统评价和荟萃分析。摘要 登革热 已从偶尔的输入性感染转变为伊朗日益严重的国内公共卫生问题。对来自可比研究子集的 血清流行率 估计值(来自三项主要研究的五项估计值,其中只有三项是直接观察到的 血清流行率 测量值)进行的有限的探索性荟萃分析得出了 3.96% 的全国汇总估计值 [95% CI: 1.20–6.72],具有显着的异质性(I2 = 98.5%,p < 0.01)。锡斯坦和俾路支斯坦省的关键 暴发...
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
登革热 已从偶尔的输入性感染转变为伊朗日益严重的国内公共卫生问题。该审查重点关注伊朗的 流行病学、媒介、诊断以及与 登革病毒 相关的公共卫生挑战。
证据基础与方法
这项系统文献综述根据 PRISMA 2020 指南(承认存在偏差)进行,分析了 2020 年至 2026 年间发表的 46 项同行评审研究。对来自可比研究子集的 血清流行率 估计值进行有限的探索性荟萃分析(来自三项主要研究的五项估计值,其中只有三项是直接观察到的 血清流行率 测量值)得出的全国汇总估计值为 3.96% [95% CI:1.20–6.72],具有显着的异质性(I2 = 98.5%,p < 0.01)。
共识与结论
2024 年锡斯坦和俾路支斯坦省出现了严重的 暴发,涉及 1,057 例确诊病例(80.7% 为本地传播),这表明存在流行病形成的威胁,而不是已达到流行病。该审查确定了 监测 基础设施、分子诊断能力和医疗保健系统准备方面的关键差距。
争议与缺口
发表偏倚分析(漏斗图不对称性;Egger 检验:p = 0.028)表明低 患病率 区域的代表性可能不足,但这些研究结果并不可靠,研究很少,应极其谨慎地解释。该审查还强调需要有关 登革病毒 传播 动态和 媒介 行为的更全面的数据。
科研与实践启示
主要建议包括实施综合 监测 系统、采用基因组 流行病学 以及加强区域合作。如果不立即采取协调一致的行动,伊朗将面临着转变为地方性登革热 传播 的迫在眉睫的风险。

Dengue fever in Iran: epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges: a comprehensive systematic literature review (2020–2026)

Authors: Hassan Nasirian
Original abstract (English)
This systematic literature review, conducted in accordance with PRISMA 2020 guidelines (with acknowledged deviations), analyzed 46 peer-reviewed studies published between 2020 and 2026. This quantitative synthesis is presented primarily for illustrative purposes to visualize geographic patterns; the narrative synthesis remains the primary contribution of this review. Publication bias analyses (funnel plot asymmetry; Egger’s test: p = 0.028) suggested potential underrepresentation of low-prevalence regions, but these findings are unreliable with few studies and should be interpreted with extreme caution. The review identifies critical gaps in surveillance infrastructure, molecular diagnostic capacity, and healthcare system preparedness. The critical outbreak in Sistan and Baluchestan Provin...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue fever has transitioned from an occasional imported infection to a growing domestic public health concern in Iran. The review focuses on the epidemiology, vectors, diagnostics, and public health challenges associated with dengue virus in Iran.
Evidence base and method
This systematic literature review, conducted in accordance with PRISMA 2020 guidelines (with acknowledged deviations), analyzed 46 peer-reviewed studies published between 2020 and 2026. A limited, exploratory meta-analysis of seroprevalence estimates from a subset of comparable studies (five estimates from three primary studies, of which only three were directly observed seroprevalence measurements) yielded a pooled national estimate of 3.96% [95% CI: 1.20–6.72], with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 98.5%, p < 0.01).
Consensus and conclusions
The critical outbreak in Sistan and Baluchestan Province in 2024, involving 1,057 confirmed cases (80.7% locally transmitted), suggests a threat of endemic establishment rather than having reached endemicity. The review identifies critical gaps in surveillance infrastructure, molecular diagnostic capacity, and healthcare system preparedness.
Controversies and gaps
Publication bias analyses (funnel plot asymmetry; Egger’s test: p = 0.028) suggested potential underrepresentation of low-prevalence regions, but these findings are unreliable with few studies and should be interpreted with extreme caution. The review also highlights the need for more comprehensive data on dengue virus transmission dynamics and vector behavior.
Research and practice implications
Key recommendations include implementing integrated surveillance systems, adopting genomic epidemiology, and enhancing regional collaboration. Without immediate and coordinated action, Iran faces an imminent risk of transitioning to endemic dengue transmission.

番木瓜叶提取物对登革热引起的血小板减少症血小板计数的影响:系统评价和荟萃分析

Effect of Carica papaya Leaf Extract on Platelet Count in Dengue-Induced Thrombocytopenia: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
作者： Lukman Nur Rahman, Adis Apriadi, Elvira Rahma Karmeilia, Sukarsi Endang Lestari
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是热带地区主要的公共卫生问题,并且通常与血小板减少症有关。番木瓜叶提取物(CPLE)已被广泛用作增加登革热患者血小板计数的常规药物。大量研究分析了番木瓜叶的功效,证实番木瓜叶可有效治疗血小板减少症。该系统概述是根据系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南进行的。Cochrane 偏倚风险工具 (RoB 2.0) 和 ROBINS-I 工具(非随机干预研究中的偏倚风险)已用于评估偏倚,Rstudio 已用于统计分析。与对照相比,番木瓜叶提取物证实血小板计数显着增加(SMD = 0.40;95% CI:0.18–0.63;p = 0.0003)。按天进行的亚组分析证实,影响在第 2 天和第 4 天不再广泛,但在第 3 天(SMD = 0.46;95% CI:0.04–0.87)和第 5 天(SMD = 0.63;95% CI:0.14–1.13)影响巨大,评估时间之间没有显着差异。在成人人群中(29 项研究;n = 4,133),干预措施产生了相当大的效果(SMD = 0.52;95% CI:0.31–0.73;p < 0.0001),并且从第 3 天到第 5 天,效果逐渐增强。在儿科人群中(7 项研究;n = 1,260),随着时间的推移,干预措施的影响很大,但在第 2 天的效果并不明显,大第 3 天(SMD = 0.54;95% CI:0.31–0.77)和第 5 天(SMD = 1.21;95% CI:0.96–1.45)的影响,并且评估日之间存在相当大的差异(p < 0.0001)。番木瓜叶提取物辅助治疗可有效增加成人血小板减少症登革热患者的血小板,但在儿童中效果不显着。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
登革热 仍然是热带地区主要的公共卫生问题,并且通常与血小板减少症有关。该综述重点关注番木瓜叶提取物 (CPLE) 对登革热引起的血小板减少症中血小板计数的影响。
证据基础与方法
本系统概述是根据系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南进行的。使用Cochrane偏倚风险工具(RoB 2.0)和ROBINS-I工具(非随机干预研究中的偏倚风险)评估偏倚,并使用Rstudio进行统计分析。
共识与结论
大量研究分析了番木瓜叶的功效,证实番木瓜叶可有效治疗血小板减少症。与对照相比,番木瓜叶提取物证实血小板计数显着增加(SMD = 0.40;95% CI:0.18–0.63;p = 0.0003)。
争议与缺口
按日进行的亚组分析证实,第 2 天和第 4 天的影响并不广泛,但在第 3 天(SMD = 0.46;95% CI:0.04–0.87)和第 5 天(SMD = 0.63;95% CI:0.14–1.13)显着,评估时间之间没有显着差异。在成年人群中(29 项研究;n = 4,133),干预具有显着效果(SMD = 0.52;95% CI:0.31–0.73;p < 0.0001),并且从第 3 天到第 5 天效果逐渐增强。在儿科人群中(7 项研究;n = 1,260),干预效果随时间变化,第 2 天效果不显着,第 1 天效果显着。第 3 天(SMD = 0.54;95% CI:0.31–0.77)和第 5 天(SMD = 1.21;95% CI:0.96–1.45),并且评估日之间存在显着差异(p < 0.0001)。
科研与实践启示
番木瓜叶提取物辅助治疗可有效增加成人血小板减少症登革热患者的血小板,但在儿童中效果不显着。

Effect of Carica papaya Leaf Extract on Platelet Count in Dengue-Induced Thrombocytopenia: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis

Authors: Lukman Nur Rahman, Adis Apriadi, Elvira Rahma Karmeilia, Sukarsi Endang Lestari
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains a prime public health trouble in tropical regions and is regularly related to thrombocytopenia. Carica papaya leaf extract (CPLE) has been broadly used as a conventional medicine to increase platelet counts in dengue sufferers. Numerous studies have analyzed the ability of Carica papaya leaves, which have verified effective as a therapy for thrombocytopenia. This systematic overview changed into performed according with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) Guidelines. The Cochrane Risk of Bias tool (RoB 2.0) and the ROBINS-I tool (Risk of Bias in Non-randomized Studies of Interventions) have been used to evaluate bias, and Rstudio became used for statistical analysis. Carica papaya leaf extract confirmed a significant increase in platelet count compared to the control (SMD = 0.40; 95% CI: 0.18–0.63; p = 0.0003). Subgroup analysis by way of day confirmed that the impact was no longer extensive on days 2 and 4, however giant on days 3 (SMD = 0.46; 95% CI: 0.04–0.87) and 5 (SMD = 0.63; 95% CI: 0.14–1.13), without a significant differences among the assessment times. Inside the adult populace (29 studies; n = 4,133), the intervention had a sizable effect (SMD = 0.52; 95% CI: 0.31–0.73; p < 0.0001) with an increasing effect from day 3 to 5. Within the pediatric population (7 research; n = 1,260), the intervention impact numerous over time, with a non-sizable effect on day 2, a large effect on day 3 (SMD = 0.54; 95% CI: 0.31–0.77), and day 5 (SMD = 1.21; 95% CI: 0.96–1.45), and a considerable difference among evaluation days (p < 0.0001). Carica papaya leaf extract adjunctive therapy effectively increases platelets in dengue patients with thrombocytopenia in the adult population, but not significantly in children.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Dengue fever remains a prime public health trouble in tropical regions and is regularly related to thrombocytopenia. The review focuses on the effect of Carica papaya leaf extract (CPLE) on platelet count in dengue-induced thrombocytopenia.
Evidence base and method
This systematic overview was performed according to the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) Guidelines. The Cochrane Risk of Bias tool (RoB 2.0) and the ROBINS-I tool (Risk of Bias in Non-randomized Studies of Interventions) were used to evaluate bias, and Rstudio was used for statistical analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Numerous studies have analyzed the ability of Carica papaya leaves, which have verified effective as a therapy for thrombocytopenia. Carica papaya leaf extract confirmed a significant increase in platelet count compared to the control (SMD = 0.40; 95% CI: 0.18–0.63; p = 0.0003).
Controversies and gaps
Subgroup analysis by day confirmed that the impact was not extensive on days 2 and 4, but significant on days 3 (SMD = 0.46; 95% CI: 0.04–0.87) and 5 (SMD = 0.63; 95% CI: 0.14–1.13), without a significant difference among the assessment times. Within the adult population (29 studies; n = 4,133), the intervention had a significant effect (SMD = 0.52; 95% CI: 0.31–0.73; p < 0.0001) with an increasing effect from day 3 to 5. Within the pediatric population (7 studies; n = 1,260), the intervention impact varied over time, with a non-significant effect on day 2, a significant effect on day 3 (SMD = 0.54; 95% CI: 0.31–0.77), and day 5 (SMD = 1.21; 95% CI: 0.96–1.45), and a significant difference among evaluation days (p < 0.0001).
Research and practice implications
Carica papaya leaf extract adjunctive therapy effectively increases platelets in dengue patients with thrombocytopenia in the adult population, but not significantly in children.

2025 年 1 月至 12 月圭亚那新阿姆斯特丹地区医院收治的儿科患者登革热的回顾性研究

A Retrospective Study of Dengue among Paediatric Patients Admitted to New Amsterdam Regional Hospital, Guyana, from January to December 2025
作者： Chandra Kumar, Kalesh Latchminarine
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是热带地区儿童住院的重要原因之一,但圭亚那新阿姆斯特丹地区医院收治的儿童的具体情况描述仍较为有限。目的:描述2025年在圭亚那新阿姆斯特丹地区医院因登革热住院的儿科患者的年龄分布、临床表现、血清学结果、严重程度分类及住院时长。方法:本研究为基于医院的回顾性分析,审查了2025年1月至12月期间住院儿童的医疗记录。总结了可用的年龄、临床、诊断及住院信息。结果:研究共纳入120名患者。3-5岁儿童占60例(50.0%),80名患者(66.7%)为女性。所有患者均记录有发热(120;100%),呕吐(81;67.5%)和皮疹(78;65.0%)。IgM阳性结果在106名患者中被记录(88.3%),NS1抗原阳性在71名患者中被记录(59.2%),IgG阳性在40名患者中被记录(33.3%)。根据报告的严重程度分类,36名患者(30.0%)为无预警迹象的登革热,69名患者(57.5%)为有预警迹象的登革热,15名患者(12.5%)为重症登革热。住院时间范围为1至7天,中位数为4天。结论:研究结果描述了该地区医院儿科登革热住院的临床和年龄分布情况,并表明预警迹象在住院患者中较为常见。这些结果可能支持当地的临床评估和规划,尽管它们并未建立人群水平的发病率或因果关系。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
描述 2025 年圭亚那新阿姆斯特丹地区医院登革热儿科患者的人口特征、临床表现、血清学结果、严重程度类别和住院时间。
证据基础与方法
一项以医院为基础的回顾性审查检查了 2025 年 1 月至 12 月期间入院儿童的医疗记录。
共识与结论
研究结果描述了该地区医院收治的儿科登革热患者的临床和人口统计分布,并表明警告信号在收治的患者中很常见。
争议与缺口
登革热是热带地区儿科住院的一个重要原因,但圭亚那住院儿童的具体描述仍然有限。
科研与实践启示
结果可能支持当地的临床审查和规划,尽管它们没有建立人群水平的 发病率 或因果关系。

A Retrospective Study of Dengue among Paediatric Patients Admitted to New Amsterdam Regional Hospital, Guyana, from January to December 2025

Authors: Chandra Kumar, Kalesh Latchminarine
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is an important cause of paediatric hospitalisation in tropical regions, but facility-specific descriptions of admitted children in Guyana remain limited. Objective: To describe the demographic characteristics, clinical presentations, serological findings, severity categories and duration of hospitalisation among paediatric patients with dengue at New Amsterdam Regional Hospital, Guyana, during 2025. Methods: A hospital-based retrospective review examined the medical records of children admitted between January and December 2025. Available demographic, clinical, diagnostic and hospital-stay information was summarised descriptively. Results: The study included 120 patients. Children aged 3–5 years accounted for 60 admissions (50.0%), and 80 patients (66.7%) were female. Fever was recorded in all patients (120; 100%), followed by vomiting (81; 67.5%) and rash (78; 65.0%). IgM positivity was recorded in 106 patients (88.3%), NS1 antigen positivity in 71 (59.2%) and IgG positivity in 40 (33.3%). Based on the reported severity classification, 36 patients (30.0%) had dengue without warning signs, 69 (57.5%) had dengue with warning signs and 15 (12.5%) had severe dengue. Hospital stays ranged from 1 to 7 days, with a median of 4 days. Conclusion: The findings describe the clinical and demographic distribution of paediatric dengue admissions at this regional hospital and indicate that warning signs were common among admitted patients. The results may support local clinical review and planning, although they do not establish population-level incidence or causal relationships.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
To describe the demographic characteristics, clinical presentations, serological findings, severity categories and duration of hospitalisation among paediatric patients with dengue at New Amsterdam Regional Hospital, Guyana, during 2025.
Evidence base and method
A hospital-based retrospective review examined the medical records of children admitted between January and December 2025.
Consensus and conclusions
The findings describe the clinical and demographic distribution of paediatric dengue admissions at this regional hospital and indicate that warning signs were common among admitted patients.
Controversies and gaps
Dengue is an important cause of paediatric hospitalisation in tropical regions, but facility-specific descriptions of admitted children in Guyana remain limited.
Research and practice implications
The results may support local clinical review and planning, although they do not establish population-level incidence or causal relationships.
