Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades Urbanas:revisión sistemática 城市社区登革热预防策略:系统回顾

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review
作者： Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
摘要中文翻译目的:证明在城市社区和厄瓜多尔环境中预防登革热的有效策略。方法:PubMed 和 SciELO 中的 síntesis narrativa 的修订(2012-2025);其中包括 15 个研究室:对系统的修订和元分析、厄瓜多尔的研究室、社区干预和经济评估。结果:控制力降低了昆虫学指标,从而影响了传播。El manejo 环境与社区参与、生活和受惠者的立方体最有利的结果。厄瓜多尔的水、水的感知和基本预防服务的故障。结论:城市登革热的预防需要综合、社区和支持、流行病学和基本服务的良好策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查旨在综合城市社区登革热预防策略有效性的证据,并评估其在厄瓜多尔国情中的适用性。
证据基础与方法
使用 PubMed 和 SciELO 数据库从 2012 年至 2025 年进行了系统评价。证据包括 15 项研究:有或没有荟萃分析的系统评价、在厄瓜多尔进行的研究、社区干预措施和经济评估。
共识与结论
审查发现,综合、基于社区和持续的策略,包括环境管理、纱窗和有盖水容器,在预防登革热方面显示出最有利的结果。然而,单独的化学控制降低了昆虫学指数,但没有显示出对 传播 的影响。
争议与缺口
审查强调,储水做法、风险认知和缺乏基本服务等因素影响了厄瓜多尔的预防有效性。没有明确提及结果中相互矛盾的发现或异质性。
科研与实践启示
城市地区的登革热预防需要综合、基于社区和持续的战略,以及流行病学 监测 和改善基本服务。

Estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas: revisión sistemática Dengue prevention strategies in urban communities: a systematic review

Authors: Angie Nicole Acosta-Obando, Gabriela Monica Echeverría-Chavez, Carlos Alejandro Troya-Altamirano
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: sintetizar la evidencia sobre la efectividad de las estrategias de prevención del dengue en comunidades urbanas y su aplicabilidad al contexto ecuatoriano. Método: revisión sistemática con síntesis narrativa en PubMed y SciELO (2012-2025); se incluyeron 15 estudios: revisiones sistemáticas con y sin metaanálisis, estudios en Ecuador, intervenciones comunitarias y una evaluación económica. Resultados: el control químico aislado redujo los índices entomológicos sin efecto demostrado sobre la transmisión. El manejo ambiental con participación comunitaria, las mallas en las viviendas y las cubiertas de recipientes mostraron los resultados más favorables. El almacenamiento de agua, la percepción del riesgo y la falta de servicios básicos condicionaron la prevención en Ecuador. Conclusión: la prevención del dengue urbano requiere estrategias integradas, comunitarias y sostenidas, con vigilancia epidemiológica y mejoras de los servicios básicos.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to synthesize evidence on the effectiveness of dengue prevention strategies in urban communities and assess their applicability to the Ecuadorian context.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review was conducted using PubMed and SciELO databases from 2012 to 2025. The evidence includes 15 studies: systematic reviews with and without meta-analyses, studies conducted in Ecuador, community interventions, and an economic evaluation.
Consensus and conclusions
The review found that integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, including environmental management, window screens, and covered water containers, showed the most favorable results in dengue prevention. However, chemical control alone reduced entomological indices without demonstrated impact on transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that factors such as water storage practices, risk perception, and lack of basic services influenced prevention effectiveness in Ecuador. There is no explicit mention of conflicting findings or heterogeneity in the results.
Research and practice implications
The review concludes that dengue prevention in urban areas requires integrated, community-based, and sustained strategies, along with epidemiological surveillance and improvements in basic services.

登革热感染肝脏中的线粒体重塑和代谢重编程:病毒趋向性与肝脂肪变性之间的拟议联系

Mitochondrial Remodeling and Metabolic Reprogramming in the Dengue-Infected Liver: A Proposed Link Between Viral Tropism and Hepatic Steatosis
作者： Showkot Ahmed, Vu Thi Thuy Tien, Jisoo Choi, Ki‐Jun Ryu, Dong‐Kun Lee, Jinsung Yang
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 感染经常与肝损伤的生化证据相关,并且在严重和致命的病例中已有肝脂肪变性的报道。然而,在细胞系、动物模型和尸检研究中,将肝细胞感染与脂肪变性联系起来的直接证据仍然支离破碎。这篇综述评估了线粒体重塑作为肝脏感染和脂质处理改变之间的候选联系。DENV 蛋白扰乱线粒体动力学、呼吸、氧化还原平衡和先天免疫信号,并且在受感染的细胞中还观察到线粒体质量控制受损。这些模型中已经报道了葡萄糖和脂质代谢的变化。一些肝癌研究报告说,在生产性感染期间脂肪酸氧化增加,而其他实验条件则与线粒体功能受损有关。这些反应之间的时间过渡尚未确定。尽管脂肪变性的直接因果途径尚未得到证实,但肝脏免疫细胞的炎症信号传导可能会进一步损害胰岛素信号传导并促进肝损伤。本综述重点关注肝脏结果,并区分在人类肝脏组织、暴露于 DENV 的肝脏模型、其他感染 DENV 的细胞类型和非登革热肝病中进行的观察。对于每个提议的步骤,我们都会确定测量的终点以及证据是否是描述性的、关联性的或机械性测试的。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
这篇综述评估了线粒体重塑作为肝脏感染和脂质处理改变之间的候选联系。
证据基础与方法
对于每个提议的步骤,我们都会确定测量的终点以及证据是否是描述性的、关联性的或机械性测试的。
共识与结论
一些肝癌研究报告说,在生产性感染期间脂肪酸氧化增加,而其他实验条件则与线粒体功能受损有关。
争议与缺口
尽管脂肪变性的直接因果途径尚未得到证实,但肝脏免疫细胞的炎症信号传导可能会进一步损害胰岛素信号传导并促进肝损伤。
科研与实践启示
本综述重点关注肝脏结果,并区分在人类肝脏组织、暴露于 DENV 的肝脏模型、其他感染 DENV 的细胞类型和非登革热肝病中进行的观察。

Mitochondrial Remodeling and Metabolic Reprogramming in the Dengue-Infected Liver: A Proposed Link Between Viral Tropism and Hepatic Steatosis

Authors: Showkot Ahmed, Vu Thi Thuy Tien, Jisoo Choi, Ki‐Jun Ryu, Dong‐Kun Lee, Jinsung Yang
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) infection is frequently associated with biochemical evidence of liver injury, and hepatic steatosis has been reported in severe and fatal cases. However, direct evidence connecting hepatocyte infection to steatosis remains fragmented across cell lines, animal models, and autopsy studies. This review evaluates mitochondrial remodeling as a candidate link between hepatic infection and altered lipid handling. DENV proteins perturb mitochondrial dynamics, respiration, redox balance, and innate immune signaling, and impaired mitochondrial quality control has also been observed in infected cells. Changes in glucose and lipid metabolism have been reported across these models. Some hepatoma studies report increased fatty acid oxidation during productive infection, whereas other experimental conditions are associated with impaired mitochondrial function. A temporal transition between these responses has not been established. Inflammatory signaling from hepatic immune cells may further impair insulin signaling and promote liver injury, although a direct causal pathway to steatosis remains unproven. This review focuses on hepatic outcomes and distinguishes observations made in human liver tissue, in DENV-exposed hepatic models, in other DENV-infected cell types, and in non-dengue liver disease. For each proposed step we identify the endpoint that was measured and whether the evidence is descriptive, associative, or mechanistically tested.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review evaluates mitochondrial remodeling as a candidate link between hepatic infection and altered lipid handling.
Evidence base and method
For each proposed step we identify the endpoint that was measured and whether the evidence is descriptive, associative, or mechanistically tested.
Consensus and conclusions
Some hepatoma studies report increased fatty acid oxidation during productive infection, whereas other experimental conditions are associated with impaired mitochondrial function.
Controversies and gaps
Inflammatory signaling from hepatic immune cells may further impair insulin signaling and promote liver injury, although a direct causal pathway to steatosis remains unproven.
Research and practice implications
This review focuses on hepatic outcomes and distinguishes observations made in human liver tissue, in DENV-exposed hepatic models, in other DENV-infected cell types, and in non-dengue liver disease.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

布什尔省伊朗海员中检测到低 登革病毒 血清流行率 - Bioengineer.org

Low Dengue Virus Seroprevalence Detected Among Iranian Seafarers in Bushehr Province - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A cross-sectional seroepidemiological study conducted between January and April 2025 in Bushehr Province, Iran, evaluated 270 asymptomatic male seafarers, including sailors, vessel captains, and logistics personnel, for dengue virus exposure. The study found a seroprevalence of 2.59 percent, with seven individuals showing circulating antibodies against dengue antigens. Two participants had immunoglobulin M (IgM) antibodies, indicating recent infection, while five had immunoglobulin G (IgG) antibodies, suggesting past exposure. The research team confirmed that none of the participants had prior dengue vaccination, ensuring that antibody reactivities were due to natural exposure. The findings were reported by Bioengineer.org on November 23, 2026, and highlight the low baseline infection rate among mobile transport workers. However, the 2024 dengue outbreak in Iran saw 1,057 total cases, including 196 international importations, underscoring the vulnerability of southern port regions where Aedes aegypti mosquitoes are established. The study provides critical baseline data for monitoring arboviral emergence in the region.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究是在2025年1月至4月之间进行的,新闻报道的发布日期是2026年11月23日。证据中没有明确说明事件日期,但研究结果是在2024年伊朗登革热暴发的背景下报告的。
地点与对象
该研究以伊朗布什尔省的海员为对象,专门检查了270名无症状的男性海事工人,包括水手、船长和后勤人员。人口包括年龄从 17 岁到 67 岁的个人。
事件
A cross-sectional seroepidemiological investigation identified a dengue virus seroprevalence of 2.59 percent among Iranian seafarers in Bushehr Province, indicating evidence of previous and recent arboviral exposure. This finding was reported by Bioengineer.org as part of a broader context of dengue activity in the region.
规模、影响与风险
研究发现,七人体内有针对 登革病毒 抗原的循环抗体,其中两人显示近期感染(IgM),五人显示过去感染(IgG)。血清流行率 总体较低,但 2024 年 暴发 伊朗登革热病例总数为 1,057 例,其中包括 196 例国际输入病例。由于埃及伊蚊的存在,伊朗南部港口地区存在登革热 传播 风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是 2024 年登革热 暴发 后全国流行病学调查的一部分。公共卫生当局正在监测沿海边界的虫媒病毒出现情况,调查结果为此类工作提供了关键的基线数据。调查涉及结构化调查问卷、书面知情同意书和研究方案批准。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Low Dengue Virus Seroprevalence Detected Among Iranian Seafarers in Bushehr Province - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A cross-sectional seroepidemiological study conducted between January and April 2025 in Bushehr Province, Iran, evaluated 270 asymptomatic male seafarers, including sailors, vessel captains, and logistics personnel, for dengue virus exposure. The study found a seroprevalence of 2.59 percent, with seven individuals showing circulating antibodies against dengue antigens. Two participants had immunoglobulin M (IgM) antibodies, indicating recent infection, while five had immunoglobulin G (IgG) antibodies, suggesting past exposure. The research team confirmed that none of the participants had prior dengue vaccination, ensuring that antibody reactivities were due to natural exposure. The findings were reported by Bioengineer.org on November 23, 2026, and highlight the low baseline infection rate among mobile transport workers. However, the 2024 dengue outbreak in Iran saw 1,057 total cases, including 196 international importations, underscoring the vulnerability of southern port regions where Aedes aegypti mosquitoes are established. The study provides critical baseline data for monitoring arboviral emergence in the region.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Thursday, November 26, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Low Dengue Virus Seroprevalence Detected Among Iranian Seafarers in Bushehr Province by November 23, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 Low Dengue Virus Seroprevalence Detected Among Iranian Seafarers in Bushehr Province Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A cross-sectional seroepidemiological investigation evaluating maritime personnel across southern Iran has identified a dengue virus seroprevalence of 2.59 percent, documenting evidence of previous and recent arboviral exposure while demonstrating a low baseline infection rate among mobile transport workers. Conducted between January and April 2025 across six major maritime ports within Bushehr Province, the study examined 270 asymptomatic male seafarers, including sailors, vessel captains, and logistics personnel undergoing routine occupational health assessments. Serological screening conducted using commercial enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays revealed that seven individuals possessed circulating antibodies directed against dengue virus antigens, comprising two participants exhibiting immunoglobulin M antibodies characteristic of recent viral interaction and five individuals possessing immunoglobulin G antibodies indicative of past infection. Although frequent international maritime voyages across the Persian Gulf connect southern Iranian harbors with neighboring territories where active dengue transmission occurs, the overall proportion of seroreactive individuals remained minimal, providing critical baseline data for public health authorities monitoring arboviral emergence along vulnerable coastal boundaries. Dengue fever represents an expanding mosquito-borne arboviral disease caused by four antigenically distinct yet genetically related virus serotypes designated DENV-1 through DENV-4, primarily transmitted to humans through the bites of competent Aedes mosquitoes. Clinical and immunological characterization established in epidemiological literature indicates that primary infection with a given serotype typically confers durable, lifelong immunity against subsequent exposure to that specific homologous virus; however, cross-protective immunological defense against heterologous serotypes remains incomplete and transient. Across tropical and subtropical territories worldwide, dengue has experienced considerable geographic expansion and rising incidence over recent decades, driven by intensive demographic shifts, unplanned urban development, and accelerated human mobility that generate favorable ecological niches for sustained mosquito proliferation and intensified human-vector interactions within urban and peri-urban environments. Discover more Explore History Buy Maps Learn Arabic Within the Eastern Mediterranean Region, dengue virus transmission has emerged as an increasingly severe public health challenge since the onset of the twenty-first century, even though limited surveillance frameworks and insufficient epidemiological records have historically obscured the true regional disease burden. In Iran, historical World Health Organization surveillance records between 2017 and 2023 documented an average of roughly twenty imported dengue infections annually, reflecting sporadic viral introductions rather than sustained domestic cycles. However, epidemiological surveillance detected a pronounced surge during 2024, characterized by 137 imported infections registered between May 15 and July 10, followed by the unprecedented confirmation of indigenous local transmission in June 2024 within the southern port of Bandar Lengeh in Hormozgan Province. A comprehensive national epidemiological investigation subsequent to that emergence documented 1,057 total dengue cases throughout the 2024 outbreak, which encompassed 196 documented international importations, confirming the vulnerability of southern Iranian port regions where competent mosquito vectors, notably Aedes aegypti, have become established. Discover more Check Symptoms Study Biology Book Lab Tests Track Outbreaks The wider Persian Gulf region exhibits diverse transmission intensities and persistent pathogen circulation, creating varying exposure risks for transport personnel operating along international commercial shipping routes. A comprehensive regional meta-analysis reviewing arboviral epidemiology across Gulf territories reported a pooled mosquito-borne viral infection prevalence of 22.5 percent among 34,367 tested human samples, identifying dengue virus as the most prevalent mosquito-borne pathogen circulating in human populations. Specific regional outbreaks further demonstrate localized transmission, such as documented epidemics in Oman where 59 confirmed indigenous cases occurred during 2018 and 2019, followed by a subsequent outbreak during 2022 and 2023 wherein molecular surveillance demonstrated DENV-2 as the predominant serotype in 95.68 percent of analyzed positive samples. Longitudinal surveillance in Qatar between 2013 and 2021 registered 166 clinical cases, and municipal vector surveillance in Dubai identified DENV-1 and DENV-2 within local Aedes aegypti populations during a 2024 outbreak, standing in contrast to southwestern Saudi Arabia where general population IgG seroprevalence reached 31.7 percent. To evaluate occupational exposure dynamics within this regional framework, the research team recruited 270 male maritime workers operating from ports in Bushehr Province, obtaining written informed consent and administering structured questionnaires under research protocol approvals granted by the Ethics Committee of Bushehr University of Medical Sciences. The enrolled study cohort displayed a mean age of 39.1 ± 11.8 years, encompassing participants ranging between 17 and 67 years of age, and comprised diverse occupational ranks including 154 sailors representing 57.0 percent of the cohort, 58 seafarers representing 21.5 percent, 42 ship captains representing 15.6 percent, and 16 logistics or marine support workers representing 5.9 percent. Crucially, questionnaire evaluations and individual clinical interviews confirmed that no enrolled worker possessed any prior history of dengue vaccination, ensuring that observed antibody reactivities represented natural exposure rather than vaccine-induced humoral responses. Furthermore, comprehensive clinical evaluations verified that every participant remained entirely free of symptoms or clinical signs suggestive of active acute dengue infection at the moment of blood specimen acquisition. Detailed exposure profiling captured quantitative occupational mobility metrics among the maritime workforce, identifying that 72 individuals, corresponding to 26.7 percent of the enrolled population, had completed at least one maritime voyage to foreign territories with documented dengue transmission during the twelve months preceding the study. Conversely, 198 participants, representing 73.3 percent of the cohort, reported no international travel to known transmission zones during that preceding annual window, having either remained exclusively in domestic waters or completed their most recent foreign voyage earlier. Among personnel with documented travel histories to endemic destinations, participants completed an average of 4.3 ± 5.1 round-trip voyages annually, with individual voyage frequencies ranging from a single journey to twenty-five trips per year. Individual journeys involved a mean total voyage duration of 16.2 ± 8.9 days ranging from 5 to 45 days, while the mean duration of physical stay within foreign ports reporting active dengue circulation averaged 12.1 ± 7.4 days per trip, with individual stays spanning between 2 and 30 days. Following collection at local port health examination facilities, peripheral venous blood specimens were centrifuged for serum separation and transported under standardized cold-chain management to the Molecular Reference Laboratory of the Vice Chancellor for Health at Bushehr University of Medical Sciences. Serological testing utilized commercial enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay kits manufactured by Vircell Microbiologists, specifically the Dengue ELISA IgG kit and the Dengue ELISA IgM Capture kit, both of which possess manufacturer-validated sensitivities of 98 percent, with specificities established at 100 percent and 99 percent, respectively. The standardized laboratory protocol involved dispensing 100 microliters of diluted sera, calibrators, and control reagents to microplate wells for a 30-minute incubation at room temperature, followed by five wash cycles and the addition of 100 microliters of peroxidase-conjugated anti-human antibodies. After an additional 60-minute incubation and three washing cycles, 100 microliters of chromogenic substrate reacted for 20 minutes under ambient conditions before enzymatic termination with 50 microliters of stop solution, with optical density evaluated at 450 nanometers. Laboratory analysis of the 270 serum specimens revealed that 263 workers, representing 97.41 percent of the examined cohort, exhibited negative optical density ratios below the 0.5 threshold for both antibody classes, confirming an absence of detectable humoral reactivity. Among the seven seropositive individuals constituting the overall 2.59 percent prevalence rate, two workers demonstrated detectable anti-dengue IgM antibodies reflecting recent viral stimulation, while five workers displayed anti-dengue IgG antibodies indicating past exposure. When evaluating infection status against international travel history, seropositivity was observed in 4 out of 72 recent travelers, yielding an infection rate of 5.6 percent, compared to 3 seropositive cases among the 198 past travelers whose last foreign voyage occurred over a year prior, corresponding to a 1.5 percent positivity rate. Statistical evaluations indicated that although the percentage of seroreactive cases was numerically elevated among recent maritime voyagers, the small absolute count of positive events precluded establishing a statistically significant relationship between recent travel history and confirmed dengue seropositivity. Geographical evaluation of seroprevalence across specific maritime employment hubs demonstrated observable variations among participating provincial harbors, although small localized sample cohorts require cautious interpretation of geographic discrepancies. Workers stationed in Bushehr accounted for three seropositive cases among sixty tested individuals, generating an observed seroprevalence of 5.0 percent, whereas personnel evaluated at Asaluyeh exhibited one seropositive case among six tested workers, yielding a nominal seropositivity rate of 16.7 percent. In Genaveh, testing identified one positive individual among twenty participants, establishing a 5.0 percent prevalence rate, while Kangan recorded one seropositive worker among sixty participants for a 1.7 percent rate, and Dayyer documented one positive case among eighty evaluated seafarers, representing 1.3 percent. Conversely, testing performed among workers employed at the port of Deylam revealed no seropositive individuals across the evaluated cohort, establishing a localized seroprevalence of zero percent. Researchers emphasized that these apparent differences reflect unequal sample distribution and restricted cohort sizes in smaller facilities rather than confirmed localized transmission gradients along the coastline. Despite providing valuable baseline occupational evidence, the study’s cross-sectional framework imposes methodological constraints, inherently preventing researchers from determining the exact chronological timing of viral exposure or establishing direct causal pathways regarding infection acquisition. Furthermore, the reliance on commercial enzyme-linked immunosorbent screening without confirmatory plaque reduction neutralization testing leaves open the possibility that some detected antibodies might represent cross-reactive flaviviral immune responses rather than definitive dengue virus encounters. The authors also noted that retrospective questionnaire data collection introduces potential recall bias regarding exact voyage durations, while detailed records regarding personal insect repellent usage and vessel-specific mosquito mitigation practices were unavailable. Nevertheless, compared with general population baseline rates previously recorded in Bushehr Province at 0.9 percent and Hormozgan Province at 0.7 percent, the detection of recent and past infections among maritime personnel highlights their role as a potential sentinel group, supporting recommendations for routine occupational serosurveillance coupled with integrated entomological and molecular monitoring to prevent localized disease establishment. Time
The study was conducted between January and April 2025, with the publication date of the news report being November 23, 2026. The event date is not explicitly stated in the evidence, but the findings were reported in the context of the 2024 dengue outbreak in Iran.
Location and population
The study focused on seafarers in Bushehr Province, Iran, specifically examining 270 asymptomatic male maritime workers, including sailors, vessel captains, and logistics personnel. The population included individuals ranging in age from 17 to 67 years.
Event
A cross-sectional seroepidemiological investigation identified a dengue virus seroprevalence of 2.59 percent among Iranian seafarers in Bushehr Province, indicating evidence of previous and recent arboviral exposure. This finding was reported by Bioengineer.org as part of a broader context of dengue activity in the region.
Scale, impact and risk
The study found that seven individuals had circulating antibodies against dengue virus antigens, with two showing recent infection (IgM) and five showing past infection (IgG). The overall seroprevalence was low, but the 2024 outbreak in Iran saw 1,057 total dengue cases, including 196 international importations. The risk of dengue transmission was noted in southern Iranian port regions due to the presence of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was conducted as part of a national epidemiological investigation following the 2024 dengue outbreak. Public health authorities are monitoring arboviral emergence along coastal boundaries, and the findings provide critical baseline data for such efforts. The investigation involved structured questionnaires, written informed consent, and research protocol approvals.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

孟加拉国登革热死亡人数达到 518 人,医院病例攀升 - 自然世界新闻

Bangladesh Dengue Deaths Reach 518 as Hospital Cases Climb - Nature World News
新闻简报孟加拉国正在经历2026年的重症登革热 暴发,截至2026年11月22日,死亡人数达到518人。卫生服务总局(DGHS)报告称,截至当天上午8点的24小时内,新增住院病例1,519例,新增2例死亡病例,住院病例总数达到173,473例。暴发 给该国的医疗保健系统带来了巨大压力,11 月 22 日有 5,070 名登革热患者在医院接受治疗。由于数据仍在审查中,DGHS 尚未确认两起死亡事件的具体地点。今年的登革热季节是有记录以来最严重的季节之一,死亡人数超过 2024 年的 575 人,接近 2023 年的 1,705 人死亡记录。公共卫生专家敦促全年进行蚊虫控制,以减轻疾病的传播,因为 暴发 已蔓延到达卡以外地区并持续到 11 月底。登革热是一种蚊媒疾病,在孟加拉国日益受到关注,目前尚无广泛使用的 疫苗,也没有特定的 抗病毒药物 治疗。DGHS 将继续监测情况并更新每日数据,这将决定 2026 年是否成为该国历史上最致命的登革热年。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布时间:美国东部时间 2026 年 11 月 23 日上午 8:23;活动日期:2026 年 11 月 22 日(24 小时至上午 8 点)。
地点与对象
孟加拉国;2026 年,有 173,473 人因登革热住院;2026 年 11 月 22 日星期日,全国各地医院有 5,070 名登革热患者接受治疗。
事件
据孟加拉国卫生服务总局 (DGHS) 称,2026 年 11 月 22 日至上午 8 点的 24 小时内,孟加拉国新增 2 例登革热死亡病例,新增 1,519 例住院病例,使全年死亡人数达到 518 人。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年11月22日,孟加拉国今年已有173,473人因登革热住院,当天有5,070名患者在医院接受治疗。距离今年还有一个多月的时间,死亡人数已达 518 人,接近 2024 年的 575 人。DGHS 报告称,截至周日上午的 24 小时内,已有 2,021 名登革热患者出院,全年康复总人数达到 167,885 人。
应对、状态与不确定性
DGHS 将继续公布每日数据,显示 2026 年的死亡人数是否会在年底前超过 2024 年。据报道,这两起死亡事件发生在不同地点,但具体地点尚未确定。在可以直接检查数字之前,DGHS 并未将这些地点包括在内。孟加拉国的公共卫生专家呼吁全年控制蚊子。

Bangladesh Dengue Deaths Reach 518 as Hospital Cases Climb - Nature World News

News Brief
Bangladesh is experiencing a severe dengue outbreak in 2026, with the death toll reaching 518 as of 22 November 2026. The Directorate General of Health Services (DGHS) reported 1,519 new hospital admissions and two additional deaths in the 24 hours to 8 a.m. on that day, bringing the total number of hospitalised cases to 173,473. The outbreak is placing significant pressure on the country's healthcare system, with 5,070 dengue patients being treated in hospitals on 22 November. The DGHS has not yet confirmed the exact locations of the two deaths, as the data is still under review. This year's dengue season is one of the worst on record, surpassing 2024's death toll of 575 and approaching 2023's record of 1,705 deaths. Public health experts are urging for year-round mosquito control to mitigate the spread of the disease, as the outbreak has spread beyond Dhaka and continues into late November. Dengue, a mosquito-borne illness, is a growing concern in Bangladesh, with no widely available vaccine and no specific antiviral treatment. The DGHS will continue to monitor the situation and update daily figures, which will determine whether 2026 becomes the deadliest dengue year in the country's history.
Original source text (English)
Home Animals Biology Environment Health & Medicine Tech Travel Space Natureworldnews.com Let's Connect Trending climate change Earth whales Bangladesh Battles Dengue and Measles at Once as Death Tolls Rise By Elias Reed Published: Nov 23 2026, 8:23 AM EST Facebook Twitter Linkedin Comment Mail MUNIR UZ ZAMAN/AFP via Getty Images Bangladesh recorded two more dengue deaths and 1,519 new hospital admissions in the 24 hours to 8 a.m. on Sunday, 22 November 2026, raising the country's dengue death toll for the year to 518, according to the Directorate General of Health Services (DGHS). The figures, reported by The Business Standard and The Daily Star, show that 173,473 people have been hospitalised with dengue in Bangladesh so far in 2026. On Sunday, 5,070 dengue patients were being treated in hospitals across the country. The mosquito-borne disease is not the only outbreak straining Bangladesh's health system. The DGHS also reported nine more child deaths from suspected measles on the same day, bringing that outbreak's toll to 1,352, The Business Standard reported. The two diseases are very different, but together they are placing heavy pressure on hospitals. The latest dengue numbers According to the DGHS data, 2,021 dengue patients were discharged from hospitals in the 24 hours to Sunday morning, and total recoveries for the year reached 167,885, The Business Standard reported. The two people who died were in different parts of the country, but the reports disagree on where. The Business Standard placed the deaths in Chattogram and Rangpur divisions, while The Daily Star said Rangpur and Khulna. NatureWorldNews has not included the locations until the DGHS figures can be checked directly. How 2026 compares The 2026 season is one of the worst Bangladesh has recorded. In 2023, the country had its deadliest dengue year on record, with 1,705 deaths and 321,179 hospitalised cases, according to DGHS figures reported by The Daily Star. In 2024, there were 575 deaths and 101,214 cases. In 2025, Bangladesh recorded 102,562 cases and 412 deaths by late December, according to a Xinhua report published by People's Daily Online. That means 2026, with 173,473 cases by 22 November, already has the second-highest number of recorded dengue hospitalisations after 2023, based on those figures. Its death toll of 518 is approaching 2024's total, with more than a month of the year still to go. Dengue was a much smaller problem in Bangladesh until relatively recently. The country began recording dengue deaths in 2000, and annual hospitalisations stayed far below today's levels for most of the years since. A large outbreak in 2019, with more than 101,000 cases, marked a turning point, and after a quieter 2020, case numbers rose again from 2021 onwards, according to DGHS figures reported by bdnews24. Three of the four largest seasons on record have now come since 2023. These are hospital-based figures. Many people with mild dengue never go to hospital, so the true number of infections is likely to be much higher. What dengue is Dengue is a viral infection spread by the bite of infected Aedes mosquitoes, mainly Aedes aegypti, which breed in small amounts of standing water in and around homes, such as in buckets, flower pots, tyres and blocked drains. Unlike malaria mosquitoes, Aedes mosquitoes bite mostly during the day. Most people with dengue have mild illness or no symptoms. Others develop high fever, severe headache, pain behind the eyes, muscle and joint pain, nausea and a rash. A small share develop severe dengue, which can cause bleeding, organ damage and dangerous drops in blood pressure. Severe dengue can be fatal without prompt hospital care, and people infected a second time with a different type of the virus face a higher risk. There is no specific antiviral treatment for dengue. Care focuses on fluids, rest and close monitoring for warning signs such as severe stomach pain, persistent vomiting, bleeding gums or extreme tiredness. Why the season is lasting so long Dengue in Bangladesh traditionally peaks during and after the monsoon, from around July to October. In recent years, the season has started earlier and continued later, with significant numbers of cases persisting into November and December. Warm temperatures speed up the mosquito's breeding cycle and the virus's development inside the mosquito, while rain provides breeding sites. Rapid, unplanned urban growth, especially in Dhaka, creates countless small containers and construction sites where water collects. Public health experts in Bangladesh have repeatedly called for year-round mosquito control rather than campaigns that begin only when cases rise. A double outbreak The measles outbreak adds to the strain. According to DGHS figures reported by The Business Standard, 1,352 deaths have been recorded since 15 March, most of them suspected rather than laboratory-confirmed: 1,247 suspected and 105 confirmed. More than 256,000 suspected measles cases have been recorded in that period. Measles mainly affects children and is prevented by vaccination, while dengue affects all ages and has no widely used vaccine in Bangladesh. Hospitals, however, must handle both at once, often in the same wards and with the same staff. Pressure on hospitals The daily numbers show the scale of the work for doctors and nurses. With 1,519 new dengue admissions and 2,021 discharges in a single day, hospitals are handling thousands of patient movements, on top of the more than 229,000 suspected measles patients hospitalised since March, according to DGHS figures reported by The Business Standard. Dengue patients often need careful monitoring of fluids and blood counts, which takes staff time even when the illness is not severe. In past seasons, Dhaka's large public hospitals have been the most crowded, though dengue has spread widely beyond the capital in recent years. Protecting yourself Health authorities advise people to remove standing water around homes at least once a week, cover water containers, use mosquito repellent and nets, and wear long sleeves where possible. Anyone with a high fever should seek medical advice, avoid painkillers such as aspirin and ibuprofen unless a doctor approves them, and go to hospital quickly if warning signs appear. What comes next With cases still high in late November, the season's final toll will depend on how quickly temperatures fall and how well mosquito breeding sites are controlled. The DGHS will continue to publish daily figures, which will show whether 2026 passes 2024's death toll before the year ends. Related Dengue and West Nile Mosquitoes Prefer Different People's Scent, a Clue for Targeted Repellents as Florida Dengue Surges Florida's Locally Acquired Dengue Cases Climb to 296 as Hillsborough County Drives the Outbreak Adults Make Up Two-Thirds of Pennsylvania's 1,136 Measles Cases as State Drives U.S. Surge Measles Hits Pregnant Women in More Than a Dozen States as Doctors Admit They're Learning as They Go Key Questions Answered How many people have died of dengue in Bangladesh in 2026? 518 as of 8 a.m. on 22 November 2026, according to the Directorate General of Health Services, after two more deaths in 24 hours. That is the highest annual toll since 2024, when 575 people died. How many dengue cases has Bangladesh recorded this year? 173,473 people have been hospitalised with dengue in 2026, with 1,519 new admissions in the 24 hours to 22 November. Is 2026 Bangladesh's worst dengue year? Not the worst. 2023 recorded 1,705 deaths and 321,179 cases. But 2026 already has the second-highest hospitalisations on record and is close to 2024's 575 deaths. How does dengue spread? It spreads through bites from infected Aedes mosquitoes, which breed in standing water around homes and mostly bite during the day. It does not spread directly from person to person. What are the warning signs of severe dengue? Severe stomach pain, persistent vomiting, bleeding from the gums or nose, blood in vomit or stool, and extreme tiredness or restlessness. Anyone with these signs should go to hospital immediately. Tags Health , Dengue fever © 2026 NatureWorldNews.com All rights reserved. Do not reproduce without permission. 佛罗里达州登革热病例达到 669 例。看看 暴发 的传播地点

Florida dengue cases hit 669. See where the outbreak spreading
新闻简报截至2026年11月21日,佛罗里达州已报告669例登革热确诊病例,这是美国数十年来最大规模的暴发。大多数病例集中在希尔斯伯勒县(494 例)和皮尼拉斯县(126 例),12 个县报告了本地感染病例。希尔斯伯勒县已确认一例死亡,原因是登革热感染以及感染性休克和多器官衰竭。佛罗里达州卫生部已向 21 个县发布咨询和警报,敦促居民采取预防蚊虫叮咬的预防措施。由于蚊子种群中的 登革病毒 活动,蚊虫管理人员正在加强 治疗,并建议居民清除其房产上的积水。暴发 预计将持续,由于最近的大雨,有可能进一步增加。登革热是一种由蚊子传播的疾病,会引起严重的疼痛,通常被称为“断骨热”,虽然很少致命,但 重症登革热 可能会危及生命。佛罗里达州卫生部正在与当地蚊虫控制合作伙伴协调,监测和应对 暴发,强调公众意识和预防措施的重要性。

Florida dengue cases hit 669. See where the outbreak spreading

News Brief
As of November 21, 2026, Florida has reported 669 confirmed cases of dengue, the largest outbreak in the United States in decades. The majority of cases are concentrated in Hillsborough County (494 cases) and Pinellas County (126 cases), with locally acquired cases reported in 12 counties. One death has been confirmed in Hillsborough County, attributed to dengue infection alongside septic shock and multi-organ failure. The Florida Department of Health has issued advisories and alerts for 21 counties, urging residents to take precautions against mosquito bites. Mosquito management crews are intensifying treatments due to dengue virus activity in the mosquito population, and residents are advised to eliminate standing water on their property. The outbreak is expected to continue, with potential for further increases due to recent heavy rains. Dengue is a mosquito-borne disease that causes severe aches and pains, often referred to as 'breakbone fever,' and while it is rarely fatal, severe dengue can be life-threatening. The Florida Department of Health is coordinating with local mosquito control partners to monitor and respond to the outbreak, emphasizing the importance of public awareness and preventive measures.
Original source text (English)
News Sports Things To Do Opinion Real Estate Advertise Obituaries eNewspaper Legals STATE Florida dengue cases hit 669. See where the outbreak spreading Cheryl McCloud USA TODAY NETWORK - Florida Nov. 25, 2026, 7:50 a.m. ET Florida dengue cases have reached 669, led by Hillsborough and Pinellas counties. Here are dengue symptoms to watch for. Can you prevent getting dengue? There's some good news for Florida residents trying to prepare for Thanksgiving despite repeated rounds of heavy rain: the number of new dengue cases is slowing. That doesn't mean there weren't any new cases in the latest report from the Florida Department of Health. But after seeing a surge of 100 new cases in one week, the last two weeks the increases of locally acquired cases of dengue have been much lower: 27 cases in the week of Nov. 14 and 18 cases in the week of Nov. 21. The mosquito-borne disease is also called breakbone fever, due to the pain it causes. Even better, there were no new locally acquired cases of any other mosquito-borne disease. The new statewide total for the year of dengue now stands at 669 as of Nov. 21, which is the largest outbreak in the United States in decades. The previous record for Florida cases in recent memory was 199 in 2023. In 2025, Florida reported 62 cases. The numbers may climb even higher in the weeks ahead after repeated rounds of flooding rain in Florida. ➤ Prevent dengue at home in Florida with this 10-minute mosquito inspection The bulk of the cases continues to be in Hillsborough County on Florida's west coast, which now has 494 confirmed cases. Nearby Pinellas County is now up to 126 cases. Locally acquired cases have been reported in 12 counties. One death has been confirmed in Hillsborough County, identified as 80-year-old Gay Small, who died of “dengue infection” along with septic shock and multi-organ failure, according to a death certificate. Florida's outbreak far exceeds the previous largest outbreak, which was 264 local cases in Hawaii's Big Island between September 2015 and March 2016. President Trump in September issued an order for a "campaign" to reduce the mosquito and tick population in Washington, D.C. That model could then be "applied as targeted, innovative, non-chemical pest control for the whole nation." Dengue isn't the only mosquito-transmitted disease that has been this year in Florida, although those have been in far fewer numbers than dengue: West Nile Virus, Chikungunya and Eastern Equine Encephalitis. Here's what you should know about mosquito-borne illnesses in Florida, symptoms to watch for, and how best to prevent mosquito bites, including advice from the Florida Department of Health. 21 counties under mosquito-borne illness advisories, alerts A mosquito-borne illness advisory has been issued for the following counties: Bay Brevard Broward Columbia Leon Manatee Martin Palm Beach Polk Sarasota Walton A mosquito-borne illness alert has been issued for the following counties: Alachua Citrus Duval Hillsborough Holmes Miami-Dade Orange Pasco Pinellas Seminole "A mosquito-borne illness advisory means the public should take precautions to reduce mosquito bites due to factors indicating local disease such as positive sentinels, very few presumptive or positive human cases, or other justifications," according to Sarasota County. "A mosquito-borne illness alert means that there has been heightened disease activity, a significant increase in vector (disease-capable) mosquito populations, or an increase in locally-transmitted human cases. "Residents should continue to take precautions, but exercise additional caution in preventing mosquito bites." Rate slows but dengue cases continue to climb in Florida According to Florida Department of Health data released Nov. 21, 12 counties have reported 669 locally acquired dengue cases. Dengue: 669 cases 494 in Hillsborough County 126 in Pinellas County 16 in Miami-Dade County 15 in Orange County 6 in Pasco County 3 in Citrus County 2 in Manatee County 2 in Seminole County 1 in Brevard County 1 in Broward County 1 in Palm Beach County 1 in Polk County West Nile: 10 cases 2 in Alachua County 2 in Duval County (including one asymptomatic blood donor) 1 in Columbia County 2 in Holmes County 1 in Leon (asymptomatic blood donor) 1 in Sarasota County 1 in Walton County Chikungunya: 2 cases 2 in Miami-Dade County Eastern Equine Encephalitis: 1 case 1 in Leon County What is Hillsborough County doing to try to control mosquitoes? "Mosquito management crews are increasing treatments by air and ground because the county’s mosquito surveillance in many neighborhoods is showing dengue virus activity in the mosquito population. "Residents are encouraged to look for and eliminate standing water on their property after recent heavy rains." Residents can sign up to be notified where aerial treatment will occur in neighborhoods. You can also go to the county's website to see when and where mosquito "operations" are scheduled to take place. Florida residents should take precautions to avoid mosquito-borne illnesses "Residents throughout the state should continue to take precautions from mosquito-borne illnesses by appropriately applying bug spray, avoiding areas with high mosquito populations, and wearing long pants and shirts when possible — especially during sunrise and sunset when mosquitos are most active," said Isabella White, FDOH press secretary via email Sept. 23. White said FDOH "continues to coordinate with local mosquito control partners to support surveillance and targeted response efforts in affected areas. These efforts help reduce mosquito populations, limit the risk of disease transmission, and protect residents across Florida." What are symptoms of dengue, breakbone fever? "Dengue fever can be a painful, debilitating disease but is rarely fatal," the Florida Department of Health said. Symptoms appear three to 14 days after the bite of an infected mosquito and include: sudden onset of fever severe headache eye pain muscle and joint pain (giving the disease the nickname “breakbone fever”) bleeding Gastrointestinal symptoms like vomiting and diarrhea may also be present in some cases. "Dengue fever symptoms usually last four to seven days. The disease is often diagnosed incorrectly because the symptoms are similar to influenza and other viruses," the CDC said. "Around one out of every 20 people infected by a dengue virus develops severe dengue, a life-threatening medical emergency that requires immediate medical attention," according to YaleMedicine. Why is dengue called 'breakbone fever?' Dengue is sometimes called “breakbone fever” because, in addition to fever, "it can cause severe aches and pains, especially in the legs, joints, and back," according to YaleMedicine. Dengue does not actually cause bones to break, according to YaleMedicine. Is dengue contagious? Dengue is spread by mosquitoes. Mosquitoes become infected with dengue after biting a person with the virus. Those infected mosquitoes then spread the virus to other people through bites. The Centers for Disease Control said a woman infected with the dengue virus during pregnancy could pass the virus to her fetus, causing death to the fetus, low birth weight, or premature birth. Can dengue be treated? "While there is no cure for dengue and there are no specific medications for the condition, getting adequate hydration and plenty of rest, and taking acetaminophen for pain and fever can help alleviate symptoms while the body’s immune system fights the virus," according to YaleMedicine. "In most cases, people recover from dengue within a week. For those with severe dengue, treatment by health care professionals can significantly lower the risk of death." What are the best mosquito repellents to use? According to the Florida Department of Health, repellents with the following active ingredients provide the best protection: DEET Picaridin IR3535 Oil of lemon eucalyptus Para-menthane-diol 2-undecanone, a plant-derived ingredient 'Drain and cover' helps keep mosquitoes away The FDOH offered this advice to help keep mosquitoes away from your Florida home: Drain Drain water from garbage cans, old tires, pool covers, or any other containers where water collects. Empty and clean birdbaths and pet water bowls at least once or twice a week. Protect boats and vehicles from rain with tarps that don’t accumulate water. Maintain swimming pools in good condition and ensure they are appropriately chlorinated. Empty plastic swimming pools when not in use. Cover If you must be outside when mosquitoes are active, cover up. Apply mosquito repellent to exposed skin and clothing. Use mosquito netting to protect children younger than 2 months old. Cheryl McCloud is a journalist for the USA TODAY Network-Florida's service journalism Connect team. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

多种 登革病毒 基因型在越南 2023 年 暴发 期间共同流通 - Bioengineer.org

Multiple Dengue Virus Genotypes Co-Circulated During Vietnam’s 2023 Outbreak - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A genomic surveillance study of Vietnam’s 2023 dengue outbreak, published on November 24, 2026, revealed the co-circulation of multiple dengue virus genotypes, challenging previous assumptions of single-lineage dominance. During the 2023 transmission season, Vietnam documented 149,557 infections and 36 fatalities, a decline from 2022’s 367,729 cases. The research identified a dominance of dengue virus serotype 2 (88.89 percent) and the presence of serotype 1 (10.0 percent) and serotype 4 (1.11 percent), with no serotype 3 cases detected. The study noted a demographic shift toward adult patients, who made up 66.89 percent of hospitalized individuals. Researchers used an antibody-dependent enhancement propagation model to recover viral material and conducted genomic sequencing on 53 isolates, resulting in 47 consensus sequences. The findings suggest a transition from traditional lineage replacements to a regional convergence zone, emphasizing the need for updated surveillance strategies. The study also highlights the interconnectedness of dengue transmission across Southeast Asia, including Thailand and northern Vietnam, and recent detections in Yunnan Province, China, of Cosmopolitan lineages from Thailand and Cambodia in 2024.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究报告了越南2023年登革热暴发,发表日期为2026年11月24日。事件日期为2023年传播季节,研究涉及2000年至2015年的纵向数据。
地点与对象
The outbreak occurred in Vietnam, specifically in Vung Tau province and Da Nang. The affected population included hospitalized febrile patients, with a notable demographic shift toward adult patients, who constituted 66.89 percent of all admitted individuals.
事件
基因组监测 调查揭示了 2023 年越南 暴发 期间多种 登革病毒 基因型的共同传播,颠覆了之前的单谱系优势模式。该研究记录了 2023 年 传播 季节期间越南发生 149,557 例登革热感染和 36 例死亡。
规模、影响与风险
Vietnam recorded 149,557 dengue infections and 36 deaths in 2023, which is a decline from 2022’s 367,729 cases. The study identified a dominance of dengue virus serotype 2 (88.89 percent) and the presence of serotype 1 (10.0 percent) and serotype 4 (1.11 percent). No cases of serotype 3 were detected. The findings suggest a complex genotypic landscape and a transition toward a multifaceted regional convergence zone.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究涉及全面的病毒学传播、病毒滴定和基因组测序。研究人员使用 抗体依赖性增强 传播模型来克服病毒活力下降的问题。基因组分析证实了血清型 1 的基因型 I 和 IV 以及血清型 2 的大都会和亚洲 1 基因型共同循环。该研究强调需要更新 监测 策略。

The study also highlights the interconnectedness of dengue transmission across Southeast Asia, including Thailand and northern Vietnam, and recent detections in Yunnan Province, China, of Cosmopolitan lineages from Thailand and Cambodia in 2024.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Thursday, November 26, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Multiple Dengue Virus Genotypes Co-Circulated During Vietnam’s 2023 Outbreak by November 24, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 Multiple Dengue Virus Genotypes Co-Circulated During Vietnam's 2023 Outbreak Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A genomic surveillance investigation of Vietnam’s 2023 dengue outbreak has revealed the concurrent co-circulation of multiple viral lineages, overturning previous patterns of single-lineage dominance. During the 2023 transmission season, Vietnam documented 149,557 dengue infections and 36 fatalities amidst an unprecedented global surge that produced 6.5 million reported cases and 6,800 deaths across the world. Although national case numbers represented a decline from the 367,729 cases registered in 2022, molecular characterization of clinical isolates demonstrated an unexpectedly complex evolutionary landscape. Rather than a solitary viral lineage driving transmission, researchers identified diverse co-circulating genotypes across dengue virus serotype 1 and serotype 2. The findings suggest that Vietnam is transitioning from traditional cyclical lineage replacements toward a multifaceted regional convergence zone. This complex genotypic landscape highlights how persistent local viral transmission intertwines with broader Southeast Asian transmission networks, complicating vector-borne disease control and challenging conventional serotype-level surveillance strategies throughout the endemic country. A prominent feature of the examined cohort was a marked demographic transition toward adult patients, who constituted 66.89 percent of all admitted individuals presenting with febrile illness. Historically considered a pediatric infection in hyper-endemic parts of Southeast Asia, dengue in Vietnam has exhibited a measurable long-term shift toward older age brackets. Longitudinal clinical records compiled from 2000 to 2015 demonstrated that the mean age of clinical cases climbed steadily from 12.2 to 16.8 years, indicating an ongoing epidemiological trajectory rather than an isolated anomaly confined to 2023. Researchers noted that the strong adult presence among hospitalized patients likely reflects shifting population-level immunity, waning cross-protection from childhood exposures, altered urban mobility, or the introduction of viral genotypes able to evade pre-existing heterologous antibodies. Discover more genotypic genotypes Track Outbreaks The research team evaluated a total of 148 blood samples gathered from hospitalized febrile patients, with 146 individuals recruited in Vung Tau province and two admitted in Da Nang. Laboratory screening using reverse transcription–polymerase chain reaction assays confirmed dengue virus infection in 90 of the 148 patients, establishing a positivity rate of 60.81 percent. Quantitative reverse transcription–polymerase chain reaction serotyping of the positive specimens identified an overwhelming dominance of dengue virus serotype 2, which accounted for 80 cases, or 88.89 percent of confirmed infections. Dengue virus serotype 1 represented nine cases, corresponding to 10.0 percent, whereas a single sample, or 1.11 percent, tested positive for serotype 4. Notably, no cases of serotype 3 were detected within the collected cohort. Following initial diagnostic confirmation in Vietnam, the serum specimens were transported to Japan under cold storage for comprehensive downstream virological propagation, viral titrations, and high-throughput next-generation genomic sequencing protocols. Discover more Check Symptoms Study Biology Genotypes Order DNA Kits To overcome reduced viral viability and lower viral titers caused by prolonged specimen storage and international transport, the researchers applied an antibody-dependent enhancement propagation culture model. Ninety serum samples were each diluted 1 to 50 in minimum essential medium supplemented with 2 percent fetal bovine serum and mixed with the cross-reactive mouse monoclonal antibody mAb4G2. After a thirty-minute incubation at 4 degrees Celsius to facilitate the formation of virus-antibody complexes, the suspensions were inoculated onto Fc-gamma receptor-expressing baby hamster kidney cell lines. The cultures were incubated at 37 degrees Celsius with 5 percent carbon dioxide for one hour to enable Fc-gamma receptor-mediated entry, effectively mimicking the natural mechanism of antibody-dependent enhancement. The cell monolayers were subsequently overlaid with maintenance medium and maintained for five to seven days before harvest. This low-passage approach recovered sufficient viable viral material while minimizing the potential selective pressures and genetic drift associated with prolonged laboratory cell culture. Viral ribonucleic acid was extracted from culture supernatants using commercial isolation kits and quantified via real-time assays to verify library input thresholds. Whole-genome next-generation sequencing was attempted on 53 viral isolates selected based on lower cycle threshold values below 25, robust culture recovery, and RNA yields, while considering patient demographic representation. Dual-stranded complementary DNA was synthesized, purified with magnetic beads, and converted into sequencing libraries using the Illumina Nextera XT DNA Library Preparation Kit. Sequencing performed on an Illumina MiSeq instrument produced 47 consensus genome sequences across serotypes 1 and 2. Twenty-six of these sequences—comprising three serotype 1 and 23 serotype 2 genomes—satisfied all rigorous quality thresholds for complete genome deposition in NCBI GenBank under accession SUB16454349. The remaining 21 sequences displayed partial coverage or ambiguous regions, while the single serotype 4 isolate failed library preparation due to insufficient RNA and was omitted from downstream genomic analyses. Phylogenetic analysis of the seven recovered dengue virus serotype 1 sequences uncovered two distinct lineages circulating within the Vietnamese cohort. Six of the seven consensus sequences, accounting for approximately 86 percent of serotype 1 isolates, clustered within genotype I. These isolates grouped closely with previously documented Vietnamese strains, demonstrating sustained endemic transmission and local viral maintenance over consecutive epidemic seasons. In contrast, one isolate, representing 14 percent of the sequenced serotype 1 cohort, clustered within genotype IV. This secondary lineage grouped alongside strains circulating more broadly across maritime Southeast Asia, including historical references from the Philippines, Indonesia, and regional island territories. While the limited number of serotype 1 genomes precluded formal population-frequency calculations across the wider country, the clear co-existence of genotype I and genotype IV confirms the concurrent presence of locally sustained lineages alongside sporadic viral introductions during the 2023 transmission period. Among the 40 dengue virus serotype 2 consensus sequences, maximum-likelihood phylogenetic trees revealed that 39 isolates (approximately 98 percent) belonged to the Cosmopolitan genotype, while one isolate (2 percent) grouped into the Asian 1 genotype. This distribution illustrates continuous local circulation of the Cosmopolitan lineage alongside ongoing regional connectivity. Parallel evolutionary patterns have been recorded across the Mekong basin, where Thailand experienced co-circulation of Cosmopolitan and Asian 1 genotypes across 2016 through 2018, and northern Vietnam documented both lineages during its 2019–2020 epidemic. Furthermore, recent molecular surveillance in Yunnan Province, China, identified Cosmopolitan lineages originating from Thailand and Cambodia in 2024. These matching phylogenetic trees indicate that mainland Southeast Asian dengue transmission operates as an interconnected network characterized by recurring cross-border exchanges. Rather than isolated domestic surges, regional mobility facilitates the ongoing introduction and convergence of divergent lineages across neighboring boundaries. To track fine-scale genetic evolution within the dominant lineage, the investigators carried out comparative mutational analyses comparing six high-quality 2023 Cosmopolitan genomes against 35 historical Vietnamese genomes collected between 2019 and 2022. The viral sequences were mapped against the annotated DENV-2 reference genome NC_001474.2 to differentiate synonymous from non-synonymous amino acid changes across viral coding sequences. Fourteen non-synonymous amino acid substitutions were shared between historical and 2023 isolates, demonstrating temporal persistence of established lineage signatures across successive outbreak years. Specific shared mutations—such as NS1-Q2735R, NS1-R2936K, NS3-K5081R, NS3-K6317R, NS5-L8230V, and NS5-K10241R—occurred at high prevalence across both sampling eras. Additional shared mutations, including E-Q1092H, E-H1381N, and NS3-S5555N, displayed elevated frequencies among the 2023 outbreak strains. Proteinwise distribution revealed that non-synonymous substitutions were concentrated within the viral non-structural protein NS5, which harbored 14 total mutations including 10 outbreak-specific changes. The envelope glycoprotein contained nine total substitutions, non-structural protein 1 harbored eight, and non-structural protein 3 contained seven. Prominent 2023-specific alterations included E-E1075K, E-K1312E, NS1-T2770A, NS3-A4999T, NS5-R8857G, and NS5-K9968T. The authors noted that the higher absolute mutation count observed in NS5 likely reflects its large coding length rather than an intrinsically elevated evolutionary rate. Nevertheless, because envelope and non-structural proteins govern host receptor binding, membrane fusion, replication complex assembly, and immune modulation, the accumulation of structural alterations illustrates active genomic diversification within contemporary circulating lineages. These structural mutations provide concrete molecular reference points for monitoring how emerging viral variants interact with population immunity across Southeast Asia. The authors emphasized critical study limitations, cautioning that findings from 47 consensus sequences cannot be interpreted as definitive population-level fixation. Because 98.65 percent of clinical samples originated from hospitalized patients in Vung Tau province, the cohort may not capture the full spectrum of dengue diversity across Vietnam. Furthermore, low-passage propagation in cultured BHK cells could introduce in vitro selective pressures, and cross-sectional sampling without molecular-clock dating prevented direct determinations of introduction timelines. Clinical severity records and travel histories were not integrated into genomic models, and functional phenotypic assays were not conducted to measure whether specific mutations alter viral fitness or immune neutralization. Nevertheless, the documented convergence of multiple DENV-1 and DENV-2 genotypes underscores the urgent necessity of continuous whole-genome surveillance. Moving beyond simple serotyping is vital for evaluating tetravalent vaccines like TAK-003, tracking cross-border viral transmission, and strengthening epidemic preparedness across an increasingly interconnected region. Dengue fever cases surge in Ho Chi Minh City, with nearly 2,500 reported in a week. - Vietnam.vn

News Brief
Dengue fever has seen a significant surge in Ho Chi Minh City, Vietnam, with 2,428 cases reported in the most recent week, marking a 25.7% increase compared to the average of the previous four weeks. This rise has raised concerns, as the total number of cases since the start of the year has reached 49,232. The Ho Chi Minh City Center for Disease Control (HCDC) has issued recommendations for proactive prevention, including eliminating stagnant water, covering containers, and using mosquito nets and repellents. Additionally, the health sector advises individuals to recognize early warning signs of severe illness and consider vaccination if eligible. The simultaneous increase in hand, foot, and mouth disease cases adds pressure on public health resources. Dengue fever, caused by the Dengue virus, is primarily transmitted through Aedes mosquitoes and can lead to severe complications such as dengue shock and multiple organ failure. The HCDC emphasizes the importance of maintaining preventive measures to curb the spread of the virus, especially in high-risk areas.

English Login Homepage Topic News Political System Destination Event Travel Happy Vietnam Enterprise Product Heritage Museum Figure Multimedia Data Home Culture – Society Y tế Dengue fever cases surge in Ho Chi Minh City, with nearly 2,500 reported in a week. Dengue fever in Ho Chi Minh City continues to be a complex issue, with 2,428 cases reported in the most recent week, a 25.7% increase compared to the average of the previous four weeks. Check Symptoms Báo Đầu tư•20/11/2026 Follow Vietnam.vn on Google Add Vietnam.vn on Google 0 According to the Ho Chi Minh City Center for Disease Control (HCDC), the city recorded 2,428 cases of dengue fever last week, a 25.7% increase compared to the average of the previous four weeks. From the beginning of the year to the end of week 46, the total number of dengue fever cases in the city has reached 49,232, indicating that the pressure to prevent and control the epidemic remains a serious concern. Given the increased risk of disease outbreaks, the health sector advises people to proactively eliminate mosquitoes, remove mosquito breeding grounds, recognize early signs of severe illness, and consider getting vaccinated according to professional guidelines. Localities with high numbers of cases per 100,000 people include Con Dao Special Zone, Ngai Giao ward, and Minh Thanh commune. The increase in cases in many areas necessitates strengthening epidemiological surveillance, early detection and handling of outbreaks, and raising public awareness of disease prevention. Find Treatments At the same time, hand, foot, and mouth disease also recorded an upward trend with 1,621 new cases in week 46, 9.2% higher than the average of the previous four weeks. The total number of hand, foot, and mouth disease cases since the beginning of the year has reached 49,274. The simultaneous outbreaks of these two infectious diseases are creating additional pressure on surveillance, prevention, and public health care. Discover more Read Travel Guides Book Local Tours Plan Trips Dengue fever is an infectious disease caused by the Dengue virus, primarily transmitted through Aedes mosquitoes. The disease can affect people of all ages, with common symptoms including sudden high fever, headache, muscle and joint pain, nausea, and possibly bleeding. Check Symptoms Of concern is that the course of the disease does not always correspond to the level of fever. Some cases may worsen during the fever-reducing phase, usually between the 3rd and 7th day of the illness, due to plasma leakage, circulatory disorders, or severe bleeding. Patients should pay particular attention to warning signs such as severe abdominal pain, persistent vomiting, bleeding gums, nosebleeds, lethargy, restlessness, cold extremities, or decreased urination. If these symptoms appear, they should quickly go to a medical facility for assessment and timely treatment to avoid the risk of dengue shock, multiple organ failure, and life-threatening complications. You may also like Furthermore, self-medication at home also poses risks, especially when patients use pain relievers and fever reducers like aspirin or ibuprofen, which can increase the risk of bleeding. Individuals suspected of having dengue fever should be examined and monitored according to a doctor's instructions; they should absolutely not be complacent just because their fever has subsided. Check Symptoms Given the increasing number of cases, the HCDC (Ho Chi Minh City Center for Disease Control) recommends that people continue to implement preventive measures at home and in the community, focusing on eliminating stagnant water containers, covering water containers, regularly cleaning the environment, and eliminating mosquito larvae. At the same time, people should proactively prevent mosquito bites by sleeping under mosquito nets, wearing long clothing, and using appropriate mosquito repellents. Preventive measures need to be maintained regularly, as mosquitoes that transmit the Dengue virus can breed in small water containers right in residential areas, schools, and workplaces. Controlling mosquito breeding sources is crucial to limiting the risk of transmission, especially in areas with high numbers of cases. Besides mosquito control measures, vaccination is opening up another dengue prevention option for eligible individuals. Health experts note that the dengue virus has four serotypes: DENV-1, DENV-2, DENV-3, and DENV-4. People who have previously had dengue fever can still be reinfected by a different serotype, while the risk of severe complications in subsequent infections should be considered. According to doctors at the Long Chau Vaccination System, the dengue fever vaccine has been used in Vietnam for people aged 4 and older according to approved guidelines. The vaccination schedule consists of two doses, 3 months apart. Individuals who have previously had dengue fever also need to have their health status assessed to determine the appropriate vaccination time. Check Symptoms Vaccination schedules should be determined based on age, medical history, immune status, and contraindications. Pregnant women or immunocompromised individuals should consult a doctor at the vaccination facility for advice, screening, and the development of a suitable vaccination schedule. You may also like According to the World Health Organization (WHO), the use of dengue fever vaccines should be considered based on the epidemiological situation and transmission rate in each locality. WHO currently recommends deploying the TAK-003 vaccine to children aged 6-16 years in areas with high dengue transmission rates. This recommendation for community-based vaccination should be distinguished from the age range for which the vaccine is licensed in Vietnam. Check Symptoms Experts emphasize that vaccines can help reduce the risk of dengue fever and hospitalization, but they do not guarantee absolute protection and do not replace measures such as mosquito control, elimination of breeding grounds, and disease surveillance. 计算研究确定大豆异黄酮染料木黄酮为登革热包膜抑制剂 - Bioengineer.org

Computational Study Identifies Soy Isoflavone Genistein as Dengue Envelope Inhibitor - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 23 日发表的一项计算研究发现,大豆异黄酮染料木黄酮是 登革病毒 包膜蛋白的潜在抑制剂。该研究由一个团队使用计算机方法进行,评估了 登革病毒 四种血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)的结构包膜蛋白,并分析了它们与 71 种植物来源的 抗病毒药物 化合物的相互作用。研究发现金雀异黄素表现出最强的结合亲和力,特别是在包膜蛋白的高亲和力346至390位点,具有多个范德华接触和氢键。这些发现表明金雀花素可能是 抗病毒药物 治疗登革热的有希望的候选药物,登革热每年影响超过 3.9 亿人,导致约 22,000 人死亡。然而,该研究纯粹是计算性的,需要进一步的实验验证以确认其在现实场景中的功效。该研究是利用数十年来从亚洲、南美洲、北美和澳大利亚等不同地区收集的病毒分离株序列进行的。该研究不提供临床数据或现实世界的结果,在进行额外测试之前,其影响仍然是理论上的。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 11 月 23 日发表的一项计算研究确定,大豆异黄酮染料木黄酮是一种登革热包膜抑制剂。该研究的结果基于数十年来进行的计算机筛选和分子对接模拟。
地点与对象
该研究的重点是登革病毒,它流行于东南亚、拉丁美洲、太平洋岛屿和加勒比海等热带和亚热带地区。该研究涉及从亚洲、南美洲、北美和澳大利亚收集的病毒分离株。
事件
发表在《生物医学计算机模拟研究》上的一项计算研究发现,大豆异黄酮染料木黄酮是 登革病毒 包膜蛋白的潜在抑制剂。该研究评估了多种病毒谱系并进行了分子对接模拟以评估结合亲和力。
规模、影响与风险
Dengue is reported to affect over 390 million people annually, with approximately 22,000 fatalities. The study highlights the lack of targeted antiviral pharmaceuticals for dengue, emphasizing the need for alternative therapeutic approaches. Genistein demonstrated strong binding affinities across multiple functional pockets of the envelope protein, suggesting its potential as a therapeutic candidate.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是一项计算研究,不涉及临床试验或现实世界数据。它提供了金雀异黄酮作为登革热包膜蛋白抑制剂的潜力的计算机证据。这些发现是初步的,需要进一步的实验验证。

Computational Study Identifies Soy Isoflavone Genistein as Dengue Envelope Inhibitor - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A computational study published on November 23, 2026, identified soy isoflavone genistein as a potential inhibitor of the dengue virus envelope protein. The research, conducted by a team using in silico methods, evaluated the structural envelope protein across four serotypes of dengue virus (DENV-1 to DENV-4) and analyzed their interactions with 71 plant-derived antiviral compounds. The study found that genistein exhibited the strongest binding affinities, particularly in the high-affinity 346 to 390 site of the envelope protein, with multiple van der Waals contacts and a hydrogen bond. These findings suggest genistein could be a promising candidate for antiviral therapy against dengue, which affects over 390 million people annually and results in approximately 22,000 deaths. However, the study is purely computational and requires further experimental validation to confirm its efficacy in real-world scenarios. The research was conducted using sequences from viral isolates collected over multiple decades from various regions, including Asia, South America, North America, and Australia. The study does not provide clinical data or real-world outcomes, and its implications remain theoretical until additional testing is performed.
